Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.14

Chapter 1301
After the rules of the Illusory Battlefield changed, it took three days for the situation to slowly stabilize from an abnormally intense state.
Many quick-witted cultivators fled the moment the rules changed.
The main city was no longer safe. On the contrary, it was the most dangerous place …
Right now, the wilderness was their hiding place.
After squeezing out all the Fantasy Points from the cultivators in the main city, some experts no longer lingered in the main city. Instead, they turned their gazes to the endless wilderness outside the main city. Now, not only could they obtain Fantasy Points by killing all kinds of vicious beasts in the wilderness, they could also find some hidden cultivators.
Therefore, the cultivators in the Illusory Battlefield began to quickly disperse!
Luo Zheng did not participate in this phase of the hunt.
Several hundred thousand Fantasy Points were meaningless to Luo Zheng.
Now that Luo Zheng was ranked second in this great world, his target should be the top ten cultivators.
Previously, the cultivators that Luo Zheng plundered were not specific targets. Usually, they were cultivators in a certain main city. Whoever showed up, he would treat them as his prey.
Now, it was difficult for him to find a top-ranked cultivator in the vast sea of people …
Of course, for Ji Nan, Jin Hai, and the others, this phase was more important.
Now, Ji Nan was ranked sixty-third in this great world, Jin Hai was ranked fifty-seventh, and Phantom was ranked eighty-first …
The top fifty cultivators of each great world were qualified to enter the second phase. They were not too far away from the fiftieth rank. Now was the time for them to fight with all their might.
This team was temporarily disbanded …
After Luo Zheng left the main city, he found a corner in the wilderness and took out the Sacred Spear of Slaughter.
Under the nourishment of the primal chaos aura, Luo Zheng discovered that the Sacred Spear seemed to have undergone some changes.
When he poured the primal chaos aura into it, the Sacred Spear of Slaughter seemed to have encountered sweet dew for a long time. The surface of the spear cracked, which was why it emitted popping sounds like beans.
After carefully sizing it up, Luo Zheng discovered that the Sacred Spear of Slaughter was slightly bent!
The two types of divine wood, one black and one white, were entangled with each other to create this Sacred Spear. The divine wood was so strong that it was almost indestructible in the Heavenly Dao.
But now, it was actually slightly bent?
After sizing it up again, Luo Zheng discovered that the middle part of the Sacred Spear of Slaughter seemed to have a bit of green in it.
Looking over, Luo Zheng's expression changed abruptly!
They turned out to be two fresh green buds …
"Even if it was a divine wood, it was ultimately forged into the Sacred Spear of Slaughter. Even if divine wood is special and immortal and indestructible, it should not grow these shoots …"
After thinking, Luo Zheng speculated that it was because of the primal chaos aura.
After speculating, Luo Zheng Was Luo Zheng's, he became more and more.
However, he did not have a deep understanding of the divine tree. In the end, this was only Luo Zheng's own speculation. As for what kind of mutation existed in the Holy Spear of Slaughter, it was likely that no one could answer Luo Zheng's question.
Luo Zheng Luo 'Luo Zheng Domain, Luo's Luo Zheng Spear, Sacred Luo Zheng Luo Zheng Luo Holy Luo …......
That was what Luo Zheng had in mind.
The real Sacred Spear of Slaughter should still be in Ming Dao's hands. After all, he had brought this Sacred Spear of Slaughter into the Illusory Battlefield.
It would be very difficult to obtain the Sacred Spear of Slaughter. That place was probably the core area of the Fiend Night Clan.
Would it be possible to make a copy of the Sacred Spear of Slaughter?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's hand flashed, and a metal certificate appeared.
The current chaotic battles did not have much meaning to Luo Zheng. If he could walk through the Karma Hall again, he would only have to spend five million Fantasy points.
Moreover, the Fantasy points reward from the Karma Hall would definitely not be low!
Entering the Karma Hall was a risk for any cultivator. Take Huan Ling, Ji Nan, and the others for example. They were not far away from the top 50. Rather than risking their lives in the Karma Hall, it was safer to rob other cultivators. After all, if they died in the Karma Hall, they would probably have no chance of entering the top 50.
With the Fantasy points Luo Zheng had accumulated, even if he died and lost half of his Fantasy points, it was impossible for him to fall out of the top 50.
And if he succeeded, Luo Zheng would be able to make a copy of the Sacred Spear of Slaughter!
This risk was very worth it for Luo Zheng.
"The biggest problem is whether anyone is willing to enter the Karma Hall …"
After checking, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed.
The consciousness transmitted from the metal certificate told Luo Zheng that there were nine people gathered in the Karma Hall. Only one more person was needed to open the Karma Hall!
It seemed that there were still some cultivators who were not willing to give up!
In any case, with their current Fantasy points, they would definitely not be able to enter the top 50. They might as well take out five million Fantasy points and fight!
Without much hesitation, Luo Zheng paid another five million Fantasy points. Using the metal certificate in his hand, Luo Zheng stepped into that special space for the second time …
Time passed very quickly …
In the blink of an eye, another six days had passed.
In these six days, the intense battles in the Illusion Battlefield had gradually come to an end.
There was an invisible hand beside the martial artist. The name of this hand was' Strength '. It was this hand that distributed everyone's Fantasy Points.
Under the plundering of many experts, the Fantasy points rapidly condensed on those experts' bodies. These experts' Fantasy points then condensed on the stronger cultivators' bodies …
Those "penniless" cultivators had already lost the value of plundering.
At this moment, Luo Zheng once again stepped out from behind a door of light. There was now a small imprint on the Sacred Spear of Slaughter in his hand!
This was the second time Luo Zheng stepped into the Karma Hall. This time, he did not encounter Chen Huang Yijian. Instead, he encountered other True Gods. In the Karma Hall, Luo Zheng obtained forty million Fantasy points.
What made Luo Zheng feel strange was that he was still unable to surpass the first rank. He was still ranked second in this Great World!
Who was first? Luo Zheng was truly curious. However, it was impossible for him to accurately find that person among the hundreds of millions of cultivators. Therefore, he did not know.
"I wonder what kind of expression Xun will have when she sees the Sacred Spear of Slaughter." Luo Zheng stared at the imprint on the Sacred Spear of Slaughter and a smile appeared on his face. During this period of time, the two sprouts on the Sacred Spear of Slaughter grew longer and longer. They were tightly entwined together.
Not long after Luo Zheng left the Karma Hall, the mysterious girl's voice once again resounded in the sky.
"The second stage of the Illusion Battlefield has begun. The top fifty cultivators of each Great World, please move!"
Following this voice, all the cultivators in the Illusion Battlefield raised their heads at the same time and looked at the endless sky.
Some people were only one rank away from entering the top fifty. At this moment, they were roaring with unwillingness. They really wanted to know how much of a difference there was between their Fantasy points and the person in front of them …
Some people rejoiced in their hearts. Originally, they did not have much hope. However, due to luck, they managed to kill a cultivator and obtain millions of Fantasy Points. This allowed them to rise to the top 50 and obtain the qualifications to enter the second stage.
In these fifty Great Worlds, a total of two thousand five hundred people would enter the second stage.
These two thousand five hundred people were the best of the best. They were either extremely strong or had heaven-defying luck …
Among these two thousand five hundred people, the one who was the happiest and also the most worried was Xi Youqin.
She was happy because she could finally leave this Great World and find another place to look for Luo Zheng. She was one step closer to Luo Zheng. However, she was worried that Luo Zheng would not be able to enter the top fifty of a Great World. If that happened, she would not have the chance to see Luo Zheng!
While she was feeling both happy and worried, a golden door of light appeared in front of Xi Youqin. With light steps, she stepped inside.
At the same time, a golden door of light appeared in front of Luo Zheng. "The second stage? The top 50 … Then, behind the golden door of light, the people I'll meet will be the strongest of the 50 Great Worlds! They will also be the most outstanding geniuses in the entire universe! "

Chapter 1302
Luo Zheng was traveling in a golden space. The surrounding light was like a thorn that wrapped around him.
After traveling for a long time, he finally saw the end of this space.
The moment Luo Zheng stepped out of the golden light gate, three blood-red fortresses appeared in front of everyone's eyes …
"Three blood-red fortresses, so that's how it is …"
When Luo Zheng obtained the Teleportation Token, he discovered three main cities marked with red markings in that special space. However, at that time, these three main cities were in a state where he couldn't enter. Even with the Teleportation Token, he couldn't enter these three main cities.
It turned out that these three main cities were the second stage of the Illusory Battlefield …
"Weng …"
Behind Luo Zheng, golden light gates opened one after another. The cultivators scattered across the 50 great worlds all passed through the golden light gates.
The moment they passed through the golden light gates, their first reaction was the same as Luo Zheng's. Their first reaction was to look at the three huge blood-red fortresses in front of them.
Next, they sized each other up. Their eyes were filled with vigilance.
None of the first two thousand five hundred cultivators were easy to deal with.
They represented their families, forces, and races to fight for the highest glory.
"Rumble …"
At this moment, the blood-red fortresses suddenly began to shake.
The gate of the blood-red fortress was a huge beast head. This beast head had a ferocious appearance, as if it was a creature from hell. It crawled in front of the gate, exuding a savage aura. The two huge eyes on the beast head quietly overlooked the front. With a single glance, one's heart would beat faster!
At this moment, the gate of the fortress opened. The beast head opened its gloomy mouth …
"The gate is open!"
"Let's go in?"
"What the hell is this thing? It's just a statue, but its aura is so terrifying?"
As the light gates opened, the cultivators gathered on the plain in front of the blood-red fortresses and sized up the gate in front of them.
The two thousand five hundred cultivators were divided into three groups and entered three different blood-red fortresses. There were more than seven hundred cultivators standing on the plain in front of each fortress …
Some cultivators were very decisive and didn't stop at all. After the city gate opened, they walked forward.
Even more martial artists hesitated. They were only a step away from the top of the Dreamland Battlefield, so they had no choice but to be careful.
Until the mysterious girl's voice sounded again …
"Warriors, please enter the Blood Castle." The mysterious girl's cold voice sounded again.
After hearing these words, the originally hesitant cultivators all revolved their True Essence and flew towards the city gate. The originally hesitant cultivators now seemed to be scrambling to be the first to leave ….
From the moment the gate opened, Luo Zheng had already started walking towards the city gate. His speed was neither fast nor slow.
Seeing those cultivators surpass him, intending to seize the initiative in this main city, Luo Zheng's face only revealed a faint smile.
After entering the mouth of the beast head, there was an open passage in front of them. Following the passage, they arrived at the center of the Blood Castle. There was a huge hall, and in the center of the hall was a pyramid.
On the pyramid, there were many concave caves. These caves were ten feet deep and ten feet high, and within these caves, there were statues!
"This … this is actually my statue!"
"My statue is also here …"
"Why would everyone's statue appear on this pyramid?"
"Ranking! I am ranked 170th on the Dreamland Battlefield, and my statue is also on the pyramid. 170th position, this is … everyone's ranking! "
When someone discovered this secret, everyone began to size it up.
Before, the cultivators could only check their own ranking, but they knew nothing about the cultivators who were ranked ahead or behind them.
Now, through this pyramid, everyone could directly understand their own ranking.
Luo Zheng approached the pyramid and glanced around …
He discovered Ji Nan's statue at the bottom of the pyramid.
"Two thousand and thirty-first place …"
After seeing Ji Nan's ranking, Luo Zheng faintly smiled. She finally broke into the top 50 and entered the second stage of the Dreamland Battlefield.
Following these statues, Luo Zheng saw the statues of Jin Hai, Huan Ling, and the others … but he didn't see the statue of Cang Mo.
This guy still failed at the last step and was eliminated?
After looking at the bottom row, Luo Zheng's gaze slowly moved up. The higher the ranking of the pyramid, the higher the position.
As his gaze swept through the crowd, his gaze suddenly froze. His eyes widened, and his face was filled with disbelief. Then, he let out a deep breath.
"I didn't expect him to come …"
Luo Zheng jumped up and occupied the fifth floor of the pyramid. In front of Luo Zheng stood the statue of a human cultivator.
This cultivator held a sword in one hand, and his face was full of heroic spirit and pride. Under his sword-like eyebrows, there was a pair of star-like eyes. It was Hua Tianming, who came from the same Great Thousand World as Luo Zheng!
There were 100,000 worlds in the universe, and there were as many as a trillion Divine Sea Realm cultivators. However, there were only 2,500 cultivators who stood here representing each world. That was to say, on average, only one Divine Sea Realm cultivator from every four worlds could enter the second stage …
However, in the world where Luo Zheng came from, there were two people who entered the top 2,500.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's face also revealed a smile. Looking at Hua Tianming's statue, there was a trace of vicissitudes, and his temperament became more profound.
However, he didn't know where Hua Tianming had ascended to and which world he entered the Dreamland Battlefield from. Since he could enter the top 2,500, he probably had the strength of a Heaven's Pride!
That sword-wielding young man had caught up with him so quickly.
In the world below, Luo Zheng had been ahead of Hua Tianming by a lot. At that time, the gap between Hua Tianming and Luo Zheng was almost impossible to predict.
However, even Luo Zheng didn't expect that after six or seven years, Hua Tianming would catch up with him one day!
It should be known that Luo Zheng's speed of progress in these years couldn't be underestimated. He also had the Immortal Mansion as his backing. The gap between him and Hua Tianming would only grow bigger and bigger. However, now Hua Tianming was ranked 540th in the pyramid, which really surprised Luo Zheng! Fei Fei
"Brother Tianming must have a new opportunity. Otherwise, no matter how talented he is, it's impossible for him to advance so quickly! He's not in this scarlet castle. Unfortunately … I still have some good wine in my spatial ring. If I meet him, I'll drink a hundred of his jars first! "
The three scarlet castles divided the 2,500 martial artists. The martial artists of this scarlet castle were all gathered in this spacious hall. Luo Zheng could see everything clearly at a glance. Naturally, he didn't find any trace of Hua Tianming. Presumably, he was in another scarlet castle.
Meeting Hua Tianming was already very, very unexpected for Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng never imagined that what happened next would make him speechless!
As Luo Zheng climbed up the pyramid, he wanted to engrave everyone's appearance in his mind. These people were all his opponents, so he naturally wanted to remember their characteristics …
"Why did they leave these statues inside?"
Luo Zheng was very curious about this question. However, the mysterious girl who presided over this Illusory Battlefield didn't give any hints, so everyone could only guess.
"This is myself." Luo Zheng fixed his gaze on his own statue and pondered for a moment. He even reached out his hand to touch it, and then immersed his consciousness into the statue. However, he still couldn't find anything special about it.
Thinking back and forth, Luo Zheng still couldn't think of a reason. He could only continue climbing.
However, just as he reached the first floor, which was the top of the pyramid, his gaze suddenly focused on a woman …
When Luo Zheng saw this woman, his pupils suddenly dilated, and his eyes widened like copper bells.
In contrast, although Luo Zheng was surprised when he saw Hua Tianming, he quickly felt relieved. With Hua Tianming's talent, coupled with his perseverance, it wasn't impossible for him to encounter a great opportunity and reach this step!

Chapter 1303
Luo Zheng was shocked, and he couldn't help but rub his eyes, wondering if he was seeing things...
This woman was wearing an apricot yellow silk robe and a brocade firework skirt that trailed on the ground, outlining her beautiful figure. There was a kind of cold temperament between her eyebrows, and her entire person had an incomparably beautiful bearing …
"Youqin … is actually ranked 43rd. Is there a mistake?"
Luo Zheng had a bitter taste in his mouth. He never thought that Xi Youqin would participate in the Illusory Battlefield!
Xi Youqin was only at the Divine Core realm when he first met her!
Although she had the Extreme Purple Yin Body, she had never been willing to cultivate. She even viewed cultivation as her biggest enemy.
Ever since Luo Zheng met Xi Youqin, she had never cultivated properly for even a single day...
Why was she able to stand here?
Why was she ranked 43rd?
Was it because of her Extreme Purple Yin Body?
Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out...
After being separated from Xi Youqin for so many years, Luo Zheng had missed her a lot.
Was she still the same clingy little girl from before?
In just a few years, Xi Youqin had become more generous, and a charming confidence could be seen on her face. Luo Zheng couldn't compare the Xi Youqin in his memories with the current Xi Youqin, as if they were two completely different people.
"Youqin, is she also in the other two blood red castles?"
When he thought about how he would be able to see Xi Youqin again, he couldn't help but feel a sense of anticipation...
Fated encounters were wonderful beyond words.
In the world where Luo Zheng came from, there were three people who managed to make it into the top 2500. Was this fate, or just a coincidence?
...
In front of another blood red castle.
Xi Youqin's clothes fluttered in the wind, her pretty eyes filled with nervousness as her gaze skimmed over the statues of the pyramid layer by layer.
"There's no Luo Zheng's statue on this floor..."
"There's no statue on this floor either..."
While circling around, she kept mumbling to herself, and her expression became more and more nervous.
At this moment …
A warrior in a brocade robe also flew up and followed beside Xi Youqin.
This martial artist looked extremely young, probably less than twenty years old. There was a cold aura between his eyebrows.
While Xi Youqin was sizing up the statues, the warrior was sizing up Xi Youqin …
First floor, second floor, third floor …
Xi Youqin scanned the floors one by one, and her expression became more and more disappointed. Her little mouth also pouted. The higher she went, the lower the possibility of finding Luo Zheng!
At this moment, Xi Youqin realized that someone had been following her.
Her mood was a little bad. At this moment, she suddenly turned her head. Her extremely beautiful eyes contained a trace of anger. "You! Why are you following me?! "
This warrior had been quietly sizing up Xi Youqin from behind. When he entered the blood red castle, he was attracted by Xi Youqin's special aura. This time, he wanted to befriend her.
However, when he saw that Xi Youqin seemed to be seriously looking for something, he did not open his mouth to speak. Now that he was suddenly shouted at by Xi Youqin, he was slightly startled.
Since he was young, no one had dared to shout at him like this.
Even the Saint Lords and Realm Lords were polite and respectful when they saw him...
This was because he was a Daoist. The Mahayana Lotus had thirty-six leaves, and he had already reached the peak!
He was Xuanyuan Chenfeng, the person who had always been in the first half of the first stage of the Illusory Battlefield!
When he entered the blood red castle, many warriors had already recognized Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
Many people deliberately kept a certain distance from him. There were also some warriors who had the intention of climbing up to him and befriending this legendary Daoist.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's attention had always been focused on Xi Youqin!
Just when Xuanyuan Chenfeng was startled, Xi Youqin jumped again. Her body fluttered as she stood on the fourth floor of the pyramid and continued to look for Luo Zheng's statue.
The startled expression on Xuanyuan Chenfeng's face only existed for a moment. Then, it disappeared without a trace. Looking at Xi Youqin's back, the interest in his eyes seemed to become more and more intense!
He followed her again and said, "Miss, you seem to be looking for something? I am Xuanyuan Chenfeng. I can help you... "
In the universe, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was probably one of the few people who had the highest chance of crossing the Heavenly Dao. He was destined to be the main character of this great battle, the strongest among his peers!
According to his character, he would not take the initiative to say his name. However, this lady did not recognize him and was extremely cold. He was a little depressed.
However, after hearing his name, she should be able to understand …
Xi Youqin stopped and turned back to stare at Xuanyuan Chenfeng. Xuanyuan Chenfeng showed an indifferent expression. He could almost guess what Xi Youqin would say next.
"You are Xuanyuan Chenfeng?"
"The legendary Daoist of Gods No Thoughts?"
"..."
Xuanyuan Chenfeng had already heard these words countless times. It made him very tired.
However, when it came from the mouth of this beautiful lady, it made him feel very comfortable.
However …
Xi Youqin's eyes only blinked a few times. She frowned and said, "Xuanyuan Chenfeng? Who are you? Please don't follow me! "
She was currently annoyed. How could she have a good tone? After saying that, she flew up again!
Xi Youqin did not pay attention to the strong people in the universe. How many Empyreans, how many Realm Lords, their relationships, who was the strongest person in the universe …
Many martial artists discussed as if they were familiar with their family treasures.
However, Xi Youqin did not have the slightest interest in these. She cultivated martial arts for so many years only because of a sentence from the nameless old man.
She could give Luo Zheng a surprise and be able to accompany Luo Zheng more.
This reason was enough. As for the famous genius Xuanyuan Chenfeng? Who is he? Xi Youqin did not know him at all.
Many martial artists on the pyramid below were also paying attention to Xuanyuan Chenfeng's movements. Now that they saw this fellow suffer, they were gloating in their hearts!
"Hehe, Xuanyuan Chenfeng actually has this kind of time!"
"That lady is so arrogant. It's impossible that she hasn't heard of Xuanyuan Chenfeng, right?"
"I admire that lady. Whether she really doesn't know him or is pretending not to know him …"
"Some women will loosen the reins only to grasp them better. How can a martial artist who can step into the second stage not know about the geniuses in the universe?"
Everyone's discussion was extremely low.
However, a part of it still entered Xuanyuan Chenfeng's ears.
His face slightly sank, and the gloomy look between his brows spread a little. His brows furrowed, and he once again chased after Xi Youqin.
In Xuanyuan Chenfeng's dictionary, the word 'failure' did not exist.
His existence was a miracle …
Even if he had to cross the Heavenly Dao and become a True God, he would definitely not fail!
This was the ability of a Daozi!
Moreover, this Xi Youqin's temperament was so fierce that it evoked a primitive desire to conquer in his heart!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng was not particularly fond of women, but there would be a day when a young man would be attracted to a woman of the opposite sex. He was no exception. Right now, he was attracted to Xi Youqin, although he did not know why he was attracted to her.
Just as Xi Youqin climbed to a higher level of the pyramid, her eyes suddenly revealed an intense joy!
"Ranked seventy-seventh! I knew it … I knew it. You will definitely come! You will definitely not disappoint me! "
It was on this level that she found Luo Zheng's statue!
At this moment, Xi Youqin did not hesitate at all. She went straight to Luo Zheng's statue, and reached out her hand to hold the statue's face. Her eyes contained a pool of tenderness.
In the past six years, this face had appeared countless times in her dreams.
She had countless illusions about the day she met Luo Zheng …
Now that she found Luo Zheng here, it meant that Luo Zheng was in another Blood Castle!
"The three Blood Castles seem to be unable to cross each other." Xi Youqin's eyes flashed slightly. The current Xi Youqin wished she could grow a pair of wings on her back and fly directly to Luo Zheng's side.
However, due to the rules of the Illusory Battlefield, she was unable to do so.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who had followed Xi Youqin all the way up, saw Xi Youqin happily holding a statue's face. When he saw that the statue was a handsome man, his face became completely gloomy!
At the same time, there was a strong sense of loss in his heart, which made him very irritated.
"Who is this person?" Xuanyuan Chenfeng restrained his temper and asked faintly from behind.

Chapter 1304
Xi Youqin didn't even give Xuanyuan Chenfeng a perfunctory attitude.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng was such a proud person, so he naturally felt embarrassed.
Seeing her looking at the statue with such tenderness and love, Xuanyuan Chenfeng naturally wanted to ask what was going on … …
After finding Luo Zheng's statue, Xi Youqin's mood was extremely good. Facing Xuanyuan Chenfeng's question, she actually had some patience.
"He … … is the person I like."
Xi Youqin stared at Luo Zheng's statue in a daze. Her eyes were filled with a blurred color.
She originally wanted to say that Luo Zheng was her husband … …
However, after thinking about it carefully, she still hadn't married Luo Zheng. She didn't have a real status.
Originally, when martial artists got married, they didn't care about this kind of thing.
However, Xi Youqin's perspective was different. Originally, she didn't care about it. She believed that Ning Yudie had a place in Luo Zheng's heart.
However, the situation was different now. Ning Yudie probably hadn't ascended yet, but she could always be by Luo Zheng's side!
She would rely on her own efforts to make Luo Zheng give her a real status.
This was Xi Youqin's true thoughts … …
If other people knew that this martial artist ranked in the top 50 of the Illusory Battlefield only had such thoughts, they would probably vomit blood!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng became more and more curious about the statue.
"Miss, what's this person's name … …"
He obviously wanted to know Xi Youqin, but now, he didn't even know Xi Youqin's name. Instead, he asked about the statue's name. This Xuanyuan Chenfeng could be considered a weirdo.
Xi Youqin looked at Xuanyuan Chenfeng indifferently and replied, "His name is Luo Zheng."
Hearing this name, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's eyes suddenly lit up.
It was actually that Luo Zheng. He was ranked 77th!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng never would have thought that this person was actually the world-famous Luo Zheng!
He didn't know much about Luo Zheng. In fact, he disdained to know about him. In Xuanyuan Chenfeng's world, only a few Daoists were qualified to be his opponents.
Even some of the more powerful geniuses were no match for him.
As for Luo Zheng …
The main reason was that this guy had been using the Roaring Command to shout in his ear. Even if he didn't want to understand, he couldn't avoid the harassment of the Roaring Command.
Who would have thought that this fellow would also make it into the top 100?
However, the 77th place was clearly not enough.
In Xuanyuan Chenfeng's eyes, there were only a few people who were qualified to be his opponents. For example, Lie Qianhan and the other Daoists. The rest of the people were nothing but bystanders in his eyes.
"So he is Luo Zheng." Xuanyuan Chenfeng's face was indifferent, but the arrogance in his heart was immediately revealed …
How could Xi Youqin not hear the hint of disdain in Xuanyuan Chenfeng's tone? However, she was too lazy to pay attention to it. She only carefully and seriously sized up Luo Zheng's statue.
…
In another blood-colored castle.
A handsome young man walked around the pyramid briskly. Every time he passed by a statue, he would gently raise his eyebrows and instantly analyze the statue's appearance, temperament, and age. Then, he would firmly remember it in his mind.
This young man was Hua Tianming.
After Luo Zheng ascended, Hua Tianming was given an important position in the Cloud Hall as a meritorious person.
However, with Hua Tianming's experience, he was destined not to be restricted to the Cloud Hall and the Central Domain …
The path of cultivation was long. There was always someone better than you. Many martial artists lived in stability after achieving a certain level of success. However, Hua Tianming's character was definitely not satisfied with this.
From this point, it could be seen that he had the same ambition as Luo Zheng. He hoped to stand at a higher position!
Standing at a higher position, he did not seek to look down on all living beings. He only sought to explore the origin and truth of this world!
Not long after stabilizing in the Cloud Hall, Hua Tianming chose to go out and experience the world …
In six years, he once again passed through the Chaotic Astral Ocean. He even crossed the four Holy Kingdoms and arrived at the extreme north of the Holy Kingdoms, the largest wild continent!
In this wild continent, Hua Tianming experienced life and death situations. He encountered several unique races and wild ancient beasts comparable to the Divine Ocean Realm …
In the end, he discovered a huge secret on this wild continent.
And this secret was a secret that Luo Zheng did not know when he merged with the will of the world! Because this secret originally did not belong to this world, and the memories of the will of the world could only search for things in this world.
For Hua Tianming, this secret was a great fortune.
It was this fortune that directly increased his strength. He did not even have time to return to the Central Domain before the world produced a strong repulsive force that forced him to ascend.
And the place he ascended to was completely different from Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin!
He had ascended to one of the Great Worlds in [Gods No Thoughts] … He had just ascended for two years, and had just received a certain degree of importance in one of the Holy Lands in that Great World. He had entered the core Martial Hall of the Holy Lands, but before he had the time to display his true strength and talent, the Illusory Battlefield had already opened.
When the Illusory Battlefield had just opened, the martial artists of the tenth rank Holy Land even persuaded Hua Tianming to make some preparations before entering the Illusory Battlefield.
But Hua Tianming did not hesitate at all and stepped into the Illusory Battlefield.
Then, in this Illusory Battlefield, Hua Tianming's ranking soared …
Ten million, one million, one hundred thousand …
The martial artists of the Holy Land in Gods No Thoughts, including the Holy Lord of the Holy Land, were all dumbfounded when they saw Hua Tianming's performance.
There were naturally some powerful geniuses in their Holy Land, but in the end, they were only ranked 120,000. However, this unknown Sword Cultivator from their Holy Land had suddenly charged into the top 100,000. How could they not be surprised and excited?
But Hua Tianming's speed did not stop …
90,000 …
80,000 …
70,000 …
Compared to Luo Zheng, Hua Tianming's desire to obtain Illusory Battlefield points was not very strong. His motive for participating in the Illusory Battlefield was different from Luo Zheng's. He had only entered to see how powerful the geniuses of this universe were.
In the end, when Hua Tianming returned from the Karma Hall, his Illusory Battlefield points soared. He ranked around 2,000 and entered the second stage of the Illusory Battlefield!
This scene made Hua Tianming's Holy Land almost go crazy with joy!
This was an honor that belonged to Hua Tianming and the tenth-grade Holy Land.
He was in the top 2,500!
He was either a prodigy or an existence second only to a prodigy.
Ordinary tenth rank Holy Lands simply did not have the resources to nurture prodigies!
How could they not be surprised by Hua Tianming's sudden appearance? At first, when Hua Tianming's name was reported to the Holy Lord of the Holy Land, the Holy Lord was still at a loss. He had no idea that such a genius existed in his Holy Land …
He carried his sword on his back and climbed up the pyramid, his gaze still indifferently sweeping past the statue.
"Found it. Luo Zheng, 77th!"
After searching for Luo Zheng's ranking, Hua Tianming did not look surprised at all.
In these two years, Hua Tianming naturally knew some of Luo Zheng's deeds. He had also heard Luo Zheng's stories in the universe. Hua Tianming was also familiar with some of these stories …
In his opinion, Luo Zheng would definitely enter the second stage. If Luo Zheng did not enter, that would be a surprise.
After seeing Luo Zheng's statue, Hua Tianming's eyes shone even brighter. The sword on his back even trembled slightly …
That was the power of excitement.
Even though he knew he was falling behind, he still had to work hard to catch up.
Hua Tianming had been silently chasing after Luo Zheng all this time.
Even though Luo Zheng had left him in the dust and he could not even see Luo Zheng's back, Hua Tianming was not discouraged in the slightest.
In one's life, there was always a goal. Even if he failed, at least he pursued it.
He looked down on his goal and enjoyed the process.
However … in the end, he still managed to complete a goal that seemed impossible.
After being left thousands of miles behind by Luo Zheng, he finally caught up. He just did not know how big the gap between him and Luo Zheng was!
"Don't worry, we will meet in the end." Hua Tianming reached out and pressed down on the sword hanging on his back. The sword obediently stopped trembling …
He and Luo Zheng were not only friends, but also competitors …
After that, Hua Tianming nodded at Luo Zheng's statue, then left and continued to climb the pyramid.
But after walking a few steps, he stopped again, and his expression suddenly froze. Like Luo Zheng, he also stopped in front of Xi Youqin's statue. His face was also full of surprise, but he was not as shocked as Luo Zheng.
"I didn't expect her to be ranked this high," Hua Tianming said indifferently as he stared at Xi Youqin's statue. Thinking of the relationship between Xi Youqin and Luo Zheng, a smile appeared at the corner of his mouth. He wondered what Brother Luo Zheng's expression would be when he saw Xi Youqin …
Not long after, a voice once again sounded in the three blood-colored castles.
"Martial artists, please stand in front of your own statue …"

Chapter 1305
At this stage, the ranking competition in the Dream Battlefield had reached the most intense stage.
The names on the pyramid were constantly shaking, and the attention of the outside world was also unprecedented …
"Lie Qianhan actually jumped to the first place! Unbelievable, didn't he drop to the 200th place before? "
"Xuanyuan Chenfeng also returned to the top 3 …"
"Who the hell is that Purple Conch? I've never heard of this person, but he actually broke into the top 10 … "
The top 100 of the Dream Battlefield was the target of everyone's discussion.
After all, the entire Dream Battlefield was divided into 50 great worlds. At this stage, the geniuses of the 50 great worlds couldn't compete with each other, so the strength gap of the top 100 couldn't be directly measured. They were all the strongest in each great world.
As Ning Yu Die transferred her True Essence into the pyramid, she stared at the name on it.
Seeing Luo Zheng's name would always make her feel at ease.
With her understanding of the Dream Battlefield, she more and more understood what kind of state her husband was in now, and what kind of future he would have.
Those in the top 100 were the darlings of the heavens and the earth. Even the gods had to take care of them. Any one of them would be highly regarded by the major races, because the future Dao Child would be born from these people, and the True Gods of the universe would also be born from them …
Once, Ning Yu Die had heard about the Great Era Competition from Luo Zheng.
Then what was this Great Era Competition about?
Not only Ning Yu Die herself was confused, even Luo Zheng himself wasn't particularly clear about it.
Now, she thought that this Great Era Competition was about the Divine Path!
However, Ning Yu Die didn't know that understanding this Great Era Competition was also very narrow.
In fact, not only Ning Yu Die, but the vast majority of the martial artists in the universe also didn't know much about it.
They only knew that after the Great Era Competition arrived, geniuses were like a spring, and ordinary geniuses were no longer qualified to be called geniuses …
What was a genius? Being different, possessing incomparable talent and ability, that was what a genius was.
When the number of geniuses in the Great Era Competition increased, then everyone was no longer considered a genius.
In the current universe, the true geniuses could only be the geniuses of the major forces, the geniuses of the major races.
These people had generally opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower, and if they reached the Grand Completion of the Lotus Flower, they would become Daoists, and this was the possibility of becoming a True God.
And these people not only had the possibility of becoming a True God, they also carried the fate of the universe.
As the battle of the Great Era Competition continued to evolve, the crisis of the universe would erupt sooner or later, and this kind of crisis was unavoidable for every warrior and every living being in the universe.
It was the same in the previous Evolution Eras.
In the previous Evolution Era, the crisis that erupted in the Great Era was the fall of the stars and the descent of the fire demons.
In the Evolution Era before that, the crisis that erupted in the Great Era was the Divine Punishment Plague …
In the Evolution Era before that, the crisis that erupted in the Great Era was the fall of the stars …
In the Evolution Era before that, it was the events of 10 billion years ago. There was no historical data to be found, and the warriors in the universe could only trace back the history of three Evolution Eras. Moreover, this history was extremely incomplete.
After all, after rounds of derivation and extinction, all civilizations would dissipate, and there would be very few things that could be passed down to the next Evolution Era.
"Which force does Xi Youqin belong to? Why is she standing at the 60th place? "
Ning Yu Die's face was a little gloomy. She was naturally not convinced by Xi Youqin's ability to stand above Luo Zheng.
"This Xi Youqin seems to be very unfamiliar, but I heard that this woman seems to have some background. She seems to be from the Extreme Purple Realm," someone answered.
"Extreme Purple Realm, huh? Extreme Purple Yin Body, so that's how it is." Although Ning Yu Die knew that Xi Youqin had the Extreme Purple Yin Body, and that the giant swords she controlled were also very powerful, she did not know that the Extreme Purple Realm was actually so powerful in the Upper Realm. Nurturing a single Xi Youqin could allow her to enter the top 100 of the entire universe?
However, at this moment, a whip suddenly lashed out at Ning Yu Die!
That whip directly exploded in front of Ning Yu Die's face!
Although it did not hit Ning Yu Die, after the whip exploded, it brought with it a strong gust of wind, like a knife slashing towards her!
At this moment, a faint ice crystal appeared on Ning Yu Die's face. This ice crystal was arranged according to a certain distance, forming a special layer of True Qi protection.
The knife-like gust of wind cut on the ice crystal, and the surface of the ice crystal cracked, revealing Ning Yu Die's angry face.
If not for the protection of this ice crystal, her face would have been cut into pieces. Although there was a way to restore her face, she would still suffer a lot of damage!
"You, what do you want to do!"
Ning Yu Die suddenly stood up, her face full of anger as she stared at the middle-aged woman not far away.
Although she was a slave here, pouring her own True Qi into the Jade Clear Holy Lands' Dream Space for free, she was not a slave of the Jade Clear Holy Lands!
Not far away, the middle-aged woman holding the whip stared at Ning Yu Die with a sneer on her face, "I said, no noise is allowed here, but you keep breaking the rules. This whip is just a warning, if there is a next time, it will not be a matter of this whip anymore."
"I was just talking in a low voice, it did not affect the True Qi pouring, not to mention the others were also talking, you … why are you targeting me?" Ning Yu Die asked coldly.
"Heh," The middle-aged woman held the whip in her hand, her face full of disdain, "Others? You are just an ascender from the Lower Realm, what qualifications do you have to compare yourself with others? You lowly … "
At this moment!
A black broken blade circled around, flashed a few times in the space, and then floated beside the middle-aged woman!
This broken blade was extremely fast, almost unavoidable, and instantly cut off the middle-aged woman's right arm!
Accompanied by a spray of blood, the middle-aged woman's mouth burst out with a squeal like a pig being slaughtered.
When the martial artists who were pouring True Qi saw this scene, their faces did not change much, but they also stood up and watched the scene in front of them.
Losing an arm was not a rare thing for a martial artist, but someone dared to hurt someone here, that explained the problem. This person must have a considerable status in the Jade Clear Holy Land.
After all, even a Holy Land Holy Land's Holy Lord had personally come to observe the Dreamland Hall. How could an ordinary person dare to do anything here? Wasn't that the same as seeking death?
"Who? Why did you hurt me!? "
The middle-aged woman clutched her arm, her eyes filled with bewilderment. This middle-aged woman could be considered a smart person. She knew that the person who dared to cut off her arm here was not someone she could provoke!
But, she hadn't done anything wrong, so why did someone cut off her arm? Why?
The middle-aged woman was unconvinced in her heart, thus she asked!
Before long, a person stepped out from the space. This person had a Divine Transformation realm cultivation. Many martial artists with keen eyes recognized him. This person was the Jade Clear Holy Land's Ninth Palace Master, and was also a top 100 ranked character of the Jade Clear Holy Land!
"Cutting off your arm is only to help you remember some etiquette," The Ninth Palace Master coldly said. He turned to look at Ning Yudie, and that icy expression immediately melted away as if it had met the bright sun, replaced by a gentle smile, "You … are you Miss Ning?"
Ning Yudie was stunned. This person's cultivation was unfathomably deep, so why was he so polite to her?
After she ascended, she had been despised everywhere she went and treated unfairly. Her heart was filled with regret. If she had known this would happen, she wouldn't have ascended. The Divine Sea realm was nothing in the Jade Clear Holy Land!
"I am Ning Yudie," Ning Yudie replied truthfully.
The Ninth Palace Master smiled amiably, "Would Miss Ning be willing to come with me?"
Under such politeness, a strange look appeared on Ning Yudie's face, but she still nodded slightly, "But I still have to infuse my True Essence here …"
"Miss Ning's words are too polite. Don't mention this matter again," The Ninth Palace Master smiled.
And so, Ning Yudie left under the lead of the Ninth Palace Master …
Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, the Ninth Palace Master personally came to take away a woman who had ascended. Naturally, everyone began to talk about it.
"Who is that woman? How could she be taken care of by the Ninth Palace Master? "
"Look at her beautiful face, could it be that she has caught the eye of some big shot in the Jade Clear Holy Land?"
"What's the use of being beautiful? Not to mention those big shots, even you and I have a lot of beautiful women around us. "

Chapter 1306
This sudden laughter made Luo Zheng slightly uncomfortable.
When they were in the outside world, Luo Zheng had a lot of interactions with her. Although they had never met, his impression of her was that she was a lively girl.
However, after entering the Illusory Battlefield, she started to meticulously read out the rules of the battlefield and became extremely cold.
"Not bad, Luo Zheng. You've entered the 77th place!" This girl's voice was directly transmitted to Luo Zheng's mind.
Luo Zheng was stunned for a while before he asked, "Luo Yan, is there any news?"
"…"
The other side was silent for a while, then a very dissatisfied voice was heard. "Why is this the first thing you say to me every time?"
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes. Wasn't this nonsense?
Although his Roaring Command could be used infinitely, he wouldn't be so idle as to tell her a story. Wasn't it just to ask her to help find out the whereabouts of Luo Yan?
"No news?" Luo Zheng asked.
The mysterious girl said indifferently, "The Divine Realm is much larger than you think … I sent back the news a few years ago, but there's still no news." In desperation, the mysterious girl could only explain to Luo Zheng in this way.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a disappointed expression. He sighed slightly and asked, "What do you want? Why did you suddenly appear?"
He originally wanted to investigate this bamboo slip, but he didn't expect the mysterious girl's consciousness to suddenly appear.
"Nothing. This time, I'm personally hosting the Illusory Battlefield. Don't you want to ask me for help?" the mysterious girl said with a smile.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng immediately refused. "No need."
Although Luo Zheng didn't know the true meaning behind the Illusory Battlefield, he also knew that this was the place where the geniuses of the Divine Ocean Realm in the universe competed. After entering the Illusory Battlefield, he relied on his own strength and had never relied on Qinglong or Xun. Since he had already reached this step, why would he still need the help of others?
He knew that he would eventually face those geniuses and Dao Children.
For example, the combined Ji Luoxue and the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao. However, martial artists could only rely on themselves in the end. They might be able to show off for a while with the help of others, but that was ultimately the power of others.
"Don't be like this … I really can help you," the mysterious girl continued.
"I said I don't need it." Luo Zheng frowned. "I just need you to inquire about Luo Yan. That's all."
"…" The other side instantly fell silent.
In the center of the three scarlet castles, there was a ring-shaped cloud floating in the sky.
In the depths of the clouds, there was a palace that looked like it was carved out of crystal …
At the edge of the palace, there was a young girl sitting on the railing.
This young girl was wearing a light blue Jadeite Mist Gown, and her green pleated skirt was spread out behind her. Her slender and round legs were hanging along the railing in the clouds, and her pair of fair and delicate feet were faintly discernible in the clouds. Looking from behind, her small shoulders seemed to have been carved out, and her back view looked very lonely … … … … … …..
She was the host of this Illusory Battlefield, and she was also the mysterious girl who had been listening to Luo Zheng's stories.
At this moment, her cheeks were puffed up, and her face was full of anger!
"I've never seen such a person. You don't know how to appreciate kindness! Hmph … I don't care if you live or die! "
Ever since Luo Zheng had entered the Illusory Battlefield, this was the first time the mysterious girl had seen Luo Zheng in person. Her gaze had followed Luo Zheng from beginning to end, from one main city to another, and then to the Karma Hall …
At this stage, she still trusted Luo Zheng's strength very much. Although she had paid close attention to him, she had not helped him.
At the second stage, she wanted to take the initiative to help Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng was her friend …
However, although she had talked with Luo Zheng for many years, she did not understand Luo Zheng's character.
It was not that Luo Zheng's character was pedantic. He did not pursue absolute fairness. After all, there was no fairness in this world from birth. Talent, opportunity, background, luck …
There were too many things that could affect a person's fate. A completely fair world did not exist.
However, Luo Zheng knew that this Illusory Battlefield was also a review for Luo Zheng himself. This kind of opportunity could only come once per Evolution Era. He did not want to miss this opportunity, nor did he want to obtain this opportunity by cheating.
The young girl's beautiful eyebrows slightly knitted. Her beautiful eyes moved, and there was a mischievous smile in them.
"You don't need my help. Humph … I will make it so that you can't avoid it! No one can disobey this girl! "
She had been in charge of this universe for almost six Evolution Eras. One Evolution Era was 3.6 billion years, and six Evolution Eras would be 21.6 billion years …
To her clan, lifespan was an eternal existence …
In the river of time, she had sealed herself away most of the time.
One hundred million years was just a snap of her fingers to her. Making time flow faster, or making time jump in her consciousness, was almost an instinct of her clan.
In the previous Evolution Eras, the young girl had passed through like this.
After reaching this Evolution Era, she suddenly felt bored and woke up from the Heavenly Dao.
When she was bored, singing was also not bad …
She did not expect that it would be fine if she did not sing. Once she sang, she almost messed up the entire world.
In the end, a group of Celestial Venerables begged her to stop singing.
She wanted to use the Roaring Token to chat, but that group of Celestial Venerables tightly held the Roaring Token, afraid that they would waste one. This was simply too much!
In fact, the production method of the Roaring Token was secretly released by her. Otherwise, although this world was big and there were many living beings, it was still too quiet for her who was bound by the Heavenly Dao …
Later, the Roaring Tokens increased, and it became lively, but still, no one was willing to respond to her.
She was still very bored …
Until Luo Zheng appeared.
This guy attracted her attention as soon as he appeared.
He seemed to be looking for a woman called Luo Yan. However, he relied on the Roaring Token to shout all over the world, but he did not get a response.
In the end, he quarreled with two Celestial Venerables …
Then, he started to count!
At that time, the mysterious girl felt that Luo Zheng was an interesting guy!
At the same time, she was also very curious. Where did Luo Zheng get so many Roaring Tokens? Or did he also have a Roaring Token like her that could be used infinitely?
She was not interested in finding out how Luo Zheng got his Roaring Token. As long as this guy was willing to chat with her, she was very satisfied …
Later, she listened to Luo Zheng tell her stories and agreed to help Luo Zheng find his sister. In these few years, she no longer fell into a deep sleep. Or rather, every time she fell into a deep sleep, she would force herself to wake up. Therefore, the time she slept was often less than a year and half, and not tens of millions of years like before, or even hundreds of millions of years …
Now that she had made a decision, she slowly raised her right hand, and a purple halo appeared on her slender arm. The moment this halo appeared, it quietly spread to the surroundings. Without anyone noticing, it enveloped the entire Illusory Battlefield!
Luo Zheng naturally did not know this mysterious girl's plan.
After the mysterious girl's voice disappeared, he finally breathed a sigh of relief and once again investigated the golden bamboo slip. Only then did he realize that this golden bamboo slip was actually a Heavenly Dao law imprint for the statue!
However, this Heavenly Dao law actually required 500 million fantasy points to exchange for. Seeing this condition, Luo Zheng suddenly felt dizzy.
Even the first place of the Illusory Battlefield could not possibly have 500 million fantasy points, right?
Thus, Luo Zheng turned around and chose the Strength Bamboo Slip, the Defense Bamboo Slip, and the Speed Bamboo Slip to exchange for these three bamboo slips.
Some people were extremely extreme and concentrated all their fantasy points on one bamboo slip, but Luo Zheng still pursued balance as much as possible. However, Luo Zheng did not know that when he was exchanging, the mysterious girl had already done something to him.

Chapter 1307
The number of Fantasy Points accumulated during the second stage would no longer determine the ranking of the Illusion Battlefield.
Now, they were in the process of elimination. As long as their statues were damaged, they would be eliminated.
Thus, every martialist would invest all their Fantasy Points into their statues before the end of the time and use them to strengthen their statues.
100,000 Fantasy Points could only strengthen their statues once, and the degree of strengthening wasn't obvious.
They knew that they would have to rely on themselves for the next battle. It was impossible to rely on the statues to fight.
Thus, most of the martialists used their Fantasy Points to strengthen their defenses. This was the most reliable method. After all, the top 2,500 martialists had absolute confidence in their own strength.
As for this statue made of an unknown stone? It would be fine as long as it didn't shatter and cause them to be eliminated …
There was originally a purple star on the chest of this statue.
When they strengthened their statues 100 times, which was 50 million Fantasy Points, an additional purple star would appear on their chests!
Most people had more than 5,000 Fantasy Points. Only the martialists ranked below the top 2,000 would accumulate slightly fewer Fantasy Points. Thus, after everyone exhausted their accumulated Fantasy Points, a purple mark would appear on their chests and a second purple star would appear.
During this strengthening process, a heavily armored martialist brought his statue to the second level.
This martialist had a square face, and his equipment was very exaggerated. He held a huge shield that was as tall as a person in his left hand and a silver sword in his right. His eyes were burning and his expression was very solemn. The statue behind him also held a huge shield and a heavy sword.
Luo Zheng was methodically strengthening his statue when he saw a flash of white light at the corner of his eyes. He glanced at it and raised his eyebrows imperceptibly.
This person was ranked seventh in the pyramid!
Because of his heavy equipment, Luo Zheng had a deep impression of him. However, he didn't know which faction this fellow belonged to.
When this person came up, everyone started whispering.
"Jiang Zhengyi of the Demi-Immortal Race …"
"I heard he's crazy …"
"Don't underestimate this person. He's a Dao Disciple of the Demi-Immortal Race!"
When Luo Zheng heard these discussions, Luo Zheng's heart jumped slightly.
So this was a Daoist?
Now, Luo Zheng already knew that Daozi was a martial artist who had fully bloomed his Mahayana Lotus. However, this person looked strange. Which Divine Path did he cultivate?
"Justice No.2! We're on standby! "
Jiang Zhengyi waved the heavy sword in his hand and pointed at a position. His statue stood there according to his orders, and at the same time, it knelt down on one knee and kowtowed to him.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was stupefied.
He had already said that this statue was his clone. Controlling a clone to kneel and worship him, could this person really be crazy? How could a lunatic become a Daoist?
After Jiang Zhengyi gave the order, he started to use his consciousness to browse through the bamboo slips like the others …
After making this decision, he actually chose to strengthen his strength. He used all his fantasy points to exchange for the Power Bamboo Scroll!
Streaks of red light surrounded his statue! Every time the red light flashed, it meant that he was going to strengthen it once. From the looks of it, he was probably only preparing to strengthen his strength!
Everyone looked at this Demi-Immortal Daoist as if they were looking at an idiot.
This statue was as weak as mud and would break with a light touch. What was the use of purely strengthening his strength?
If he used that statue to punch him, he would probably break into pieces. This Jiang Zhengyi didn't plan on passing the second stage, right?
"I've always heard that the Demi-Immortal Daoist Jiang Zhengyi was a strange fellow. Today, I've seen it for myself. He's no different from an idiot," a martial artist said mockingly.
Jiang Zhengyi seemed to be concentrating on his statue. When he heard this, he suddenly turned his head and said to the martial artist, "Talking about others behind their backs is an extremely immoral thing!"
After saying this, Jiang Zhengyi waved the heavy sword in his hand and pointed it at the martial artist. The shiny sword reflected the martial artist's face. Jiang Zhengyi continued, "I'll remember your face. I can't punish you here, but if we meet in the outside world, I definitely won't let you off!"
When he said this, his expression was extremely serious. It didn't seem like he was joking. It was as if since he said this, he had to do it.
Hearing this, the martial artist's expression turned ugly …
No matter how much of an idiot Jiang Zhengyi was, he was still a Demi-Immortal Daoist. Furthermore, it was rumored that this fellow never joked and always did what he said.
After saying this, Jiang Zhengyi no longer paid attention to the martial artist and continued to invest fantasy points to strengthen his statue.
Soon after, a purple light flashed and a second purple star appeared on the statue's chest. This meant that Jiang Zhengyi had invested more than 50 million fantasy points. However, he did not stop there. Instead, he continued to strengthen the statue's power.
However, no one expected that not long after, the chest of Jiang Zhengyi's statue flashed with purple light again.
A third purple star had appeared!
"Why is there a third purple star?"
"This Jiang Zhengyi probably has more than 100 million fantasy points! That's why there's a third purple star! "
"So many fantasy points are wasted on strengthening my strength. What a waste …"
No one knew how to quantify the strength of the statue and could only make a rough estimate.
However, everyone could guess that the purple star on the statue represented the degree of strengthening the statue itself …
Seeing this scene, everyone sighed in their hearts. This fellow actually had so many fantasy points, but he was wasting them on strengthening the statue!
One had to know that everyone only had two ways to strengthen the statue. One was to infinitely strengthen the statue's defense. The other was to do the same as Luo Zheng, to strengthen the statue evenly. What was the use of strengthening the statue?
However, no one reminded Jiang Zhengyi. Even if they did, this fellow wouldn't listen.
After Luo Zheng strengthened the speed jade slip, he began to strengthen the strength and defense jade slips.
However, at this moment, a strange scene occurred …
He hadn't strengthened it for long when the chest of his statue flashed with purple light and a second purple star appeared!
According to their speculations, the appearance of a second five-star required 50 million fantasy points. However, Luo Zheng had only used less than 8 million fantasy points. How could there be an additional purple star?
At this moment, no one was paying attention to Jiang Zhengyi.
Although they knew that Jiang Zhengyi was ranked very high and was ranked seventh in the entire Illusory Battlefield, they didn't know how many fantasy points he had accumulated. Using this point, they could make a rough estimate!
Currently, Jiang Zhengyi was still constantly strengthening himself. Everyone held their breaths as they watched this scene.
Not long after, a fourth purple star appeared!
In other words, Jiang Zhengyi had at least 200 million fantasy points …
These cultivators who had entered the top 2,500 were somewhat arrogant. Since they had climbed to the top 2,500, they felt that the gap between them and the experts in front of them wasn't that big!
However, Jiang Zhengyi had more than 200 million fantasy points and was only ranked seventh?
How many fantasy points had the sixth person plundered?
What about the fifth person?
Fourth, third, second …
At this moment, they all sucked in a breath of cold air. They finally realized how big the gap was between them and the geniuses in front of them!
This exclamation also attracted Luo Zheng's attention.
"This fellow really has 200 million fantasy points?" Luo Zheng's pupils constricted.
No wonder …
Luo Zheng had plundered close to 100 million fantasy points in that large world but still wasn't able to surpass the first place. It was likely that the first place had a lot of fantasy points as well.
These fellows were indeed monstrous. The higher the rank of the cultivators, the greater the gap between them …
After a long time, Jiang Zhengyi finally stopped strengthening his statue. Judging from the time he stopped strengthening, everyone guessed that he had between 230 million and 240 million fantasy points. He definitely hadn't reached 250 million fantasy points. Otherwise, he would have been able to strengthen a fifth five-star purple star.
As for Luo Zheng, he also sped up the strengthening process …
He was still puzzled. The second five-star purple star had appeared very early on. He originally thought that it might have been because he had distributed it better and thus had a greater impact on the statue's strength.

Chapter 1308
It wasn't strange for two purple stars to appear.
Most people would be able to form a second purple star after spending all their fantasy points.
However, those who could strengthen a third purple star were few and far between …
Only the top 20 in the Illusion Battlefield would have so many fantasy points.
In this scarlet castle, there were only seven or eight cultivators who could strengthen the third purple star.
"Look, that kid also formed a third purple star!"
Some cultivators noticed the statue beside Luo Zheng.
When that cultivator shouted, everyone's gazes gathered. When they noticed Luo Zheng, they were slightly stunned.
"This guy, I remember he's only ranked around 70."
"To be precise, he's ranked 77."
"That's not right. This guy's ranking is behind me. He definitely has fewer fantasy points than me, but why can't I form a third purple star?!"
These cultivators were very meticulous in their observations. They had clearly memorized their respective rankings.
The rankings were based on the number of fantasy points. The cultivators ranked above Luo Zheng couldn't form a third purple star. How could Luo Zheng form a third purple star?
A helpless smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face when he heard these discussions.
He also didn't know what had happened …
However, he didn't talk about the discussions of others. Instead, he continued to invest fantasy points into the bamboo slips and continued to strengthen his statue.
When Luo Zheng invested more than 40 million fantasy points, the statue's chest flickered with purple light again!
"Buzz …"
The fourth purple star condensed on his chest.
"The fourth one!"
"No way, how did he do it?"
"I don't think he invested that many fantasy points …"
At this moment, the righteous Jiang Zhengyi also looked over and stared at Luo Zheng's statue.
All the cultivators looked over at Luo Zheng …
"This guy didn't cheat, right?"
"Cheat? Cheating in the Illusion Battlefield? Are you joking? "
"If he didn't cheat, how could he form a fourth purple star …"
However, before they could finish their words, another purple star condensed above Luo Zheng's statue ….
The fifth one …
Normally, forming the fifth purple star would require 250 million fantasy points.
Even the seventh-ranked Dao Child of the Demi-Immortal Clan hadn't been able to condense it, but Luo Zheng had.
Everyone looked at the statue of Luo Zheng. Their faces were blank, and they didn't know what to say.
"There won't be a sixth one …"
However, before he could finish speaking, a purple light flashed, and the sixth purple star appeared.
Luo Zheng glanced at the sixth purple star above the statue. His expression was numb.
He had already guessed where the problem lay. It was most likely that the mysterious girl had done something.
"This fellow is too willful …"
Luo Zheng said in a low voice. However, he had no other choice. He couldn't just throw all these fantasy points away and waste them here.
As Luo Zheng continued to invest his Fantasy Points, purple stars appeared one after another.
The seventh one …
The eighth one …
The ninth one …
When Luo Zheng used up all his Fantasy Points, ten purple stars appeared on the chest of the statue!
This statue emitted a vigorous aura that was even stronger than Luo Zheng's aura …
Every time a purple star appeared, the shock in everyone's eyes increased. At this moment, they were very quiet as they stared at Luo Zheng in a daze.
Luo Zheng sized up his statue, and a trace of satisfaction appeared on his face. He then raised his head and looked at the circle of light at the top of the blood-colored castle. This circle of light was already 90% complete. It seemed that the countdown was about to end. With a thought, he controlled the statue and prepared to leave with it.
"You … how did you do it?" a warrior asked. Everyone wanted to know the trick.
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders. "I'm not sure either."
After Luo Zheng left, everyone started to discuss again.
"Who is this fellow?"
"Being ranked 77th, he definitely isn't a nobody. However, he looks very unfamiliar …"
"Hurry up and get ready. The countdown is about to end."
Luo Zheng brought the statue to the platform above the blood-colored castle's gate and looked at the plains in the distance.
According to the mysterious girl, this round of assessment would be faced with an endless beast tide …
The statue would be the first target of the beast tide, and the warrior would be the second target. If the warrior died, there was no problem as he could revive. However, once the statue was destroyed, he would be eliminated!
After everyone strengthened their statues, they began to occupy the advantageous terrain.
Some people climbed to the top of the castle, some hid in a corner, and even more people were distributed on both sides of the castle …
Soon after, the circle of light completely disappeared.
The moment the circle of light dissipated, it emitted a blood-colored light that suddenly spread out.
"Hu …"
Luo Zheng, who was standing next to the statue, looked at the endless plains in front of the blood-colored castle's gate and saw a thin stretch of black gas rising straight up.
"Ge ge ge …"
A series of disorderly chirps could be heard from afar.
A blue light flashed in Luo Zheng's eyes, and his face revealed a trace of seriousness.
"Pangolins?"
What appeared in Luo Zheng's vision was a group of innumerable black pangolins.
These pangolins were about the same size as adult wolves and had a pair of extremely sharp wings. Although they had wings, they relied on a pair of thick feet to run wildly on the ground.
"This is …"
"This is the Nether Armor Beast. This beast grew up in the Underworld. Its abdomen is like a drum, hiding explosive sacs. After killing it, it will produce a violent explosion!"
"This is the first wave!"
Seeing countless Nether Armor Beasts approaching, Luo Zheng's fighting spirit became stronger and stronger.
No matter whether the mysterious girl helped him cheat or not, he had to pass the second stage of the assessment.
"Ge ge ge …"
The noisy cries were getting closer and closer.
Many warriors looked more and more serious.
These geniuses were very clear that the first stage of the assessment was only to distinguish them from the hundreds of millions of Divine Sea Realm warriors.
Now they had entered the elimination stage, which was the most critical stage!
"Here they come!"
Those Nether Armor Beasts were like a black carpet, rushing over from the plain. However, in the end, they still gathered at the gate of the Scarlet Castle and swarmed in through the mouth of the beast head!
"Ge ge ge …"
Most of the Nether Armor Beasts went straight to the Scarlet Castle, but some of them noticed Luo Zheng on the platform. They turned their heads and charged at him.
Or rather, they charged at Luo Zheng's statue.
"Die!"
There was no hesitation on Luo Zheng's face. With a gentle wave of the Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword, chaotic wind blades spread out from the long sword!
"Swish, swish, swish, swish …"
These wind blades formed a circular encirclement, wrapping Luo Zheng and the statue in the middle.
The Nether Armor Beast in the front exploded the moment it was cut by the wind blades, creating a powerful impact!
"Bang!"
After this Nether Armor Beast exploded, the sharp scales on its back also spewed out, rushing toward Luo Zheng and the statue like hidden weapons.
At first, Luo Zheng was still a little worried. Although his statue had been strengthened, the specific degree of strengthening was uncertain. However, those sharp scales nailed onto the statue one by one, not leaving a single mark!
Seeing this, Luo Zheng was relieved.
With a slight thought, his statue rushed out first and began to crush those Nether Armor Beasts …
This statue was like a puppet controlled by Luo Zheng. He didn't even need to do it himself. From the gate of the castle, it crushed all the way to the hall.
These Nether Armor Beasts were like an ocean, occupying the entire Scarlet Castle.
All the warriors were desperately protecting their statues. They would rather bear the impact of the explosion of the Nether Armor Beasts than let their statues suffer the slightest bit of damage.
Just now, a warrior was careless and his statue was surrounded by dozens of Nether Armor Beasts. Under the explosion, the statue was blown to pieces, and that warrior was directly eliminated!
Among them, the most extreme was Jiang Zhengyi.
Although his statue was extremely fragile, he used all his fantasy points to strengthen the power of his statue.
However, at this moment, his statue was enveloped by a translucent light shield.
This was the "Sacred Shield of the Heart" that Jiang Zhengyi had comprehended. As long as his mind was not destroyed, the Sacred Shield would never be broken!
With such a sacred shield draped over the statue, it brandished the giant sword in its hand. Those Nether Armor Beasts were like ants in front of the statue, and they were easily crushed.
However, when everyone's gaze landed on Luo Zheng's statue, they were all speechless.
Luo Zheng didn't give the statue any protection. He just relied on himself to rampage through those Nether Armor Beasts …
