Chapter 1309
It was probably because this statue had been strengthened by Luo Zheng to a ridiculous degree …
Luo Zheng was also very surprised and speechless.
This Scarlet Castle should be a process of elimination, but that mysterious little girl had directly helped him strengthen this statue to an astonishing degree.
With this statue's current strength, it would be very difficult for Luo Zheng to destroy it.
This directly led to a problem.
During this process of elimination, he seemed to have become an existence that had nothing to do and was like a bystander.
Wave after wave of beasts rushed into the Scarlet Castle like a tide.
From the Nether Armor Beasts to the Wind Tooth Dragons now …
Luo Zheng's statue was like a boulder, standing in the middle of the castle's hall.
As for the other warriors, they were either running around after being crushed by the beasts, or struggling to hold on.
Towards the end, even Jiang Zhengyi could not hold on!
After all, the beasts became stronger as they went on. Although the Sacred Shield he condensed was very strong, it was also consuming his willpower …
Luo Zheng cut the head of a Wind Tooth Dragon with a sword, but his face was full of indifference.
The Wind Tooth Dragons' first target was the statues, so they would not dodge his attack …
Luo Zheng could probably pass this round of elimination by sleeping. He was completely reduced to a spectator.
"Unnecessary." Luo Zheng curled his lips, his face revealing a helpless expression. Although he knew that the mysterious girl had good intentions, she had made him lose any difficulty in challenging her.
He lowered his head to look at his Sumeru Ring, and gently pressed it. Accompanied by a flash of light, the Teleportation Token appeared in his hand.
With the strength of this statue, he did not need to do anything to pass this round of elimination.
Accompanied by a flash of light, Luo Zheng escaped into that strange space.
At this moment, the marked main cities in this space had completely disappeared, leaving only the three red main cities in the center, which were the three Scarlet Castles.
Without much consideration, Luo Zheng chose one of the main cities and abruptly sunk into it.
Now, the three Scarlet Castles were undergoing the baptism of the beasts, and warriors were constantly being eliminated.
Among the 2,500 warriors, thousands of their statues had been destroyed by the beasts. The moment their statues were destroyed, they were kicked out of this place and eliminated …
Although they were unwilling, the rules of the Illusory Battlefield were like this. If they could not comply, they would be eliminated.
"Bang!"
Accompanied by a blazing white light, Luo Zheng directly appeared at the top of the Scarlet Castle's hall, and then fell down heavily.
Some of the warriors who were fighting against the ferocious beasts were stunned when they saw this scene.
What happened?
Why did this guy appear at this time?
In the hall of the blood-colored castle, seven flying swords of different colors floated around Xi Youqin.
These seven flying swords were the inheritance treasures of the Indefinite Tower. For this War of the Worlds, the Indefinite Old Man naturally would not be stingy and handed them over to Xi Youqin to bring into the Illusory Battlefield.
But before this, she had never used to use them …
But in order to protect her statue, Xi Youqin did not hesitate to use these seven flying swords.
The seven flying swords shuttled back and forth between her and her statue, forming seven colorful lines that protected her from top to bottom. But even so, there were some cracks on Xi Youqin's statue.
In comparison, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who was beside Xi Youqin, seemed to be at ease.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng had plundered about 300 million fantasy points in the Illusory Battlefield! Using these fantasy points to strengthen the statue, it was naturally much stronger than ordinary warriors. Moreover, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's strength was not ordinary, and he seemed to be at ease in this round of elimination.
Of course …
No matter how at ease he was, he could not be compared to Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng directly threw his statue in another Scarlet Castle, and ran over to "stroll" by himself.
"Miss, you still haven't told me your name!" Xuanyuan Chenfeng said indifferently beside Xi Youqin. Although there was a trace of gloominess between his brows, his words were refined. In addition to his handsome appearance, he gave people a feeling of bathing in the spring breeze.
"Move aside!"
Xi Youqin said coldly. The seven flying swords shuttled back and forth even faster, but her attitude was extremely cold.
"Swish, swish, swish …"
In the blink of an eye, she cut more than ten Wind Fang Dragons into pieces.
These Wind Fang Dragons were not very strong, but they were beings of law born in the wind. Before they died, they would turn into wind blades that could penetrate through any hole. If one was careless, one's statue would be damaged, so one had to be very careful when dealing with them.
Xi Youqin did not care if she was eliminated. Her master did not set a rule that she had to be first …
But she was only a step away from meeting Luo Zheng, so how could she give up so easily?
The most important thing was that Xuanyuan Chenfeng was nagging at the side, which made Xi Youqin's mood very irritated!
"Miss, if you tell me your name, I, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, can guarantee that you will not be eliminated," Xuanyuan Chenfeng said. He stretched out his hand and patted lightly. Waves of invisible energy spread out.
All the Wind Fang Dragons within a radius of ten zhang seemed to be choked by an invisible hand. They were lifted in the air, constantly struggling …
Then, he stretched out his hand and gently pinched!
These hundreds of Wind Fang Dragons were pinched into two by him!
"Swish swish …"
Hundreds of Wind Fang Dragons were killed in an instant, and they immediately turned into tens of thousands of sharp wind blades, flying toward Xi Youqin and Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's expression did not change. He gently waved his hand, and all the wind blades automatically dissipated. When those wind blades shot over, they turned into gusts of wind …
"So strong." Seeing this scene, Xi Youqin's eyes slightly narrowed.
The current her was no longer the Xi Youqin of the past. After six years of cultivation in the Uncertain Tower, she had a new understanding of the martial path.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's move was indeed somewhat unimaginable. He actually directly dissolved the wind blades!
However, just as Xuanyuan Chenfeng killed those Wind Fang Dragons, a large number of Wind Fang Dragons immediately filled the gap and continued to rush toward Xi Youqin …
At this moment, Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not attack. His figure was like the wind, dodging, allowing those Wind Fang Dragons to attack Xi Youqin.
Xi Youqin lightly bit her dark red lips. The seven flying swords' coordination became even tighter …
Actually, with her strength, it was not difficult to deal with these Wind Fang Dragons. However, when she was strengthening the statue, she wasted a large amount of fantasy points. The strengthening of the statue's defensive power was not enough.
Her statue was simply unable to withstand the cutting of the wind blades. Just a slight touch would leave a deep mark!
However, if her sword formation was used against the enemy, although it could be watertight, it was slightly strenuous to block these wind blades.
"Pa!"
In a moment of carelessness, a wind blade directly pierced through her sword formation. One of the wind blades was not blocked by her and directly hit her statue, almost cutting it in half!
Seeing this scene, Xi Youqin's face revealed a trace of hesitation. There was also a trace of helplessness in her eyes.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng still had a gentle smile on his face as he indifferently looked at Xi Youqin. With his great reputation, he did not lack women.
As long as Xuanyuan Chenfeng opened his mouth, those proud women would be at his disposal.
As a Daozi, he was the main character of this era. Who could resist him?
However, this woman in front of him was the first woman to arouse his interest. He never expected that this woman would be so cold to him …
With Xuanyuan Chenfeng's arrogant personality, he believed that there was nothing he could not do.
"Miss … It wasn't easy for you to fight your way here. It would be a pity if you were eliminated …"
However, Xi Youqin ignored him. She snorted coldly at him and continued to bitterly hold on …
Not far away, there was a group of Wind Fang Dragons that covered the sky and rushed towards her.
Her slender eyebrows furrowed, and the seven long swords all turned around, forming a spiral sword formation, and strangling towards the front!
Meanwhile, she began to retreat with the statue behind her!
However, just as she retreated, she saw several wind blades rushing towards her from the other side.
These Wind Fang Dragons had already filled the entire hall of the castle. As long as one Wind Fang Dragon was killed, it would scatter wind blades. Therefore, everyone in the hall was in danger because they did not know where the wind blades would come from.
Previously, many warriors had already fallen because of this.
As she retreated, Xi Youqin's expression suddenly changed. Her seven flying swords had already shot forward, but they were unable to stop those wind blades!
Seeing this, the corners of Xi Youqin's mouth curled up as resentment flowed out of her eyes.
"Damn it, I was just one step away from meeting that enemy!"
The statue was destroyed, and she was about to be eliminated. Her long-cherished wish to see Luo Zheng again would have to be pushed back!

Chapter 1310
Although she knew that she was about to be eliminated, Xi Youqin was not sad at all.
For the other geniuses, this round of the Battle of the Ages was a torrent of courage.
If they succeeded, they would bear the fate of the entire universe, and at the same time, they would obtain endless resources and a bright future.
As long as they persevered for one more step, they might be able to reach the peak. At this moment, after being eliminated, their feelings would probably be like falling into an abyss.
However, in Xi Youqin's opinion …
These were just trivial matters of no consequence.
Her goal was never to compete with the countless geniuses in the universe.
She left the Purple Extreme Realm just to find Luo Zheng. As for the high hopes that her Master had placed on her … that was only her Master's high hopes.
Most of the martial artists would probably find it hard to understand that she had competed all the way out of hundreds of millions of martial artists just for such a speechless goal.
At this moment, Xi Youqin only felt extremely regretful …
That guy was obviously in another scarlet castle. Due to the restrictions of the rules, she could not surpass him. She felt very regretful.
However, at this moment, she suddenly felt a figure flash by.
That figure stopped beside her, blocking in front of her statue …
"Puff, puff, puff, puff …"
Several wind blades, like sharp blades, hit the figure and produced a series of muffled sounds.
Who was this guy …
Why did he use his body to block it?
Even if these geniuses used their bodies to block the wind blades, they would definitely be seriously injured, right?
She raised her head slightly and saw a handsome face. There was a faint smile on his face and his eyes were shining with an indescribable expression.
It was this smile that made Xi Youqin a little dazed.
Her heart was full of questions, and this place was still full of danger, but she was too lazy to pay attention to them.
The seven flying swords that had lost control were like puppets that had lost their strings. They fell to the ground and emitted a pleasant sound when they collided with each other.
Without the resistance of the sword formation, groups of wind teeth dragons attacked again …
If she did not resist, her statue would be torn apart at any time, and she would be eliminated …
However, she did not care anymore. After all, she had already seen this person.
Without paying attention to anything, she threw herself into Luo Zheng's arms.
For the past few years, she had been suppressing her longing while cultivating. However, these longing could only be suppressed and could not be erased.
But the more he suppressed it, the crazier these thoughts grew. Recently, it had almost reached the point where it was hard to suppress.
It was also at this moment that she vented her longing. A pair of jade-like arms tightly hugged Luo Zheng. She raised her head and stared at Luo Zheng, her eyes filled with infatuation.
As Xi Youqin pounced on him, Luo Zheng took the opportunity to step back and gently supported her with a gentle smile on his face.
However, he wouldn't be like Xi Youqin, who didn't care about anything.
Facing the surging Wind Fang Dragons, Luo Zheng's Primal Chaos Sea kept surging. After drawing out strands of Primal Chaos Qi, Luo Zheng's finger kept spinning.
Then, rings of light began to appear on the ground …
He had to protect Xi Youqin and deal with the Wind Fang Dragons, so he could only use the Thunder Calamity Annihilation.
Although the Thunder Calamity Annihilation consumed a lot of energy, it was extremely useful in this situation.
After a moment, lightning flashed in the air. The lightning arrived in an instant and covered the rings of light.
The powerful lightning directly poured down, and the Wind Fang Dragons were instantly killed by the lightning. Before they could shoot out the wind blades, they were directly swallowed by the Thunder Calamity Annihilation. This was no ordinary Law of Lightning. Instead, it had truly triggered the might of the Lightning Tribulation.
Even Xuanyuan Chenfeng's thick eyebrows could not help but slightly raise. He looked at Luo Zheng with an undetectable gloomy expression.
"This is that Luo Zheng!" Xuanyuan Chenfeng had seen Luo Zheng's statue through Xi Youqin. "A technique that directly triggers the Thunder Calamity Annihilation. Interesting …"
Then, Xuanyuan Chenfeng suddenly thought of an important question …
Wasn't this guy in another blood-colored castle?
Why did he suddenly appear here?
Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not take Luo Zheng's method seriously. A technique that could trigger the Thunder Calamity Annihilation Annihilation Annihilation Annihilation Annihilation was rare, but it only made him curious. As a Dao Disciple, he was the only one in the universe. He did not take any opponents of the same level seriously.
He just did not understand what kind of magical ability this guy had. How could he jump from one blood-colored castle to another? And this guy did not need to protect his statue?
Luo Zheng naturally did not know that there was a guy beside him who was full of doubts about him. He was just coaxing Xi Youqin while dealing with the Wind Fang Dragons.
"I've been looking for you for a long time!" Xi Youqin acted coquettishly in Luo Zheng's arms.
"Me too," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
"Really?"
Xi Youqin's eyes flashed, but the light quickly dimmed. She shook her head, "I don't believe it!"
"Really …"
Many warriors were tired of dealing with the Wind Fang Dragons to prevent themselves from being eliminated, but this blood-colored castle had become a place for two people to talk about love.
After retreating, Luo Zheng led Xi Youqin and the statue to a corner of the castle. This place formed an angle, and there was only one path. He only needed to block the front to protect Xi Youqin and her statue.
Xi Youqin did not think so much. Although her current ranking was higher than Luo Zheng's, and her strength could compete with the geniuses of the universe, she did not have the consciousness of an expert. She was still that little girl who liked to fiddle with her cooking skills.
Now that Luo Zheng was beside her, she naturally handed everything over to him. As for the power of the thunder behind her, and how dangerous it was, she did not even bother to look back. She just put her small nose close to Luo Zheng's chest, like a kitten sniffing this long-lost scent …
"Why are you participating in the Illusory Battlefield?" Luo Zheng asked softly.
Xi Youqin's eyes blinked twice, "To find you!"
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng did not know whether to laugh or cry, "I want to ask, how did your cultivation advance so quickly …"
Through the memories of the World Will, Luo Zheng knew that Xi Youqin had left the Vast Thousand World and entered the Extreme Purple Realm.
Before he ascended, he knew that Xi Youqin was hiding something from him.
However, she treated it as a secret and refused to reveal it to Luo Zheng. Therefore, Luo Zheng was so shocked when he saw Xi Youqin's statue in the blood-colored castle.
A person who had no interest in cultivation had advanced to the second stage of the Illusory Battlefield, and her ranking was higher than his. At that moment, Luo Zheng felt a sense of defeat. There was only one question in his mind. What was going on?
Hearing Luo Zheng's question, Xi Youqin giggled. She was very satisfied with Luo Zheng's question.
During Luo Zheng's dual cultivation celebration, Xi Youqin escaped into the Extreme Purple Realm and inadvertently became a disciple. She had been hiding it from Luo Zheng all this time.
At that time, Xi Youqin was extremely disappointed, and she was extremely jealous of Ning Yudie.
However, at this moment, Xi Youqin was very satisfied. At least that woman was still bitterly cultivating in the Mortal World, while she could snuggle up in Luo Zheng's arms. For Xi Youqin, this was a temporary victory! It was also the reward for her hard work over the past six years!
Therefore, she slowly and methodically told Luo Zheng what had happened back then.
Hearing the last part, Luo Zheng was also moved.
He didn't expect Xi Youqin to have done so much behind his back, and the only purpose of doing all this was to stay by his side. Thinking about how he hadn't given her anything in return, he couldn't help but feel ashamed.
Probably reading Luo Zheng's thoughts from his eyes, Xi Youqin chuckled. She stared into Luo Zheng's eyes and continued, "Don't feel ashamed. Just promise me one thing!"
"What is it?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
At this moment, Xi Youqin's expression became extremely serious. "Owe me one. Marry me officially."
Luo Zheng stared at Xi Youqin and was slightly speechless. Then, he stared deeply at Xi Youqin and nodded seriously.
Xi Youqin's obsession was so deep that it had even integrated into her state of mind. In other words, she didn't have the so-called heart of martial arts. All her obsession was Luo Zheng!
After Luo Zheng nodded, Xi Youqin smiled with relief.

Chapter 1311
At this moment, all the martial artists in the blood-colored hall were fighting with all their might.
Only a small number of people could do it with ease.
For example, Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
He had more fantasy points, and the strengthened statue was much stronger than others. With his methods, if he was eliminated here, everyone would be eliminated.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng was a legendary figure!
He had a rough childhood. When he was born, he attracted the stars. It was a good omen for the Xuanyuan Clan.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng belonged to an insignificant side branch in the huge Xuanyuan Clan.
This phenomenon that was supposed to be an auspicious omen had instead brought him a calamity of death!
One of the main branches of the clan found his side branch in the shortest time possible and replaced Xuanyuan Chenfeng with a leopard cat. They planned to drown the real Xuanyuan Chenfeng …
However, for a favored child like Xuanyuan Chenfeng, even if he was just an infant, he had luck that ordinary people could not hope for.
Not only did he not die, but he also grew up tenaciously in a special environment. In the end, he soared to the sky and became a Daoist of the Xuanyuan Clan. And he used the name of a Daoist to take revenge for the past.
Although Xuanyuan Chenfeng was full of confidence now, because of his complicated childhood experience, he was full of distrust towards everything.
Until now, no woman had appeared by his side.
According to the rules of the Four Great Clans of the Gods No Thoughts, when these Dao Children and Geniuses reached a certain age, they would be married. However, every marriage was rejected by Xuanyuan Chenfeng. Therefore, there was a rumor in the Four Great Clans that Xuanyuan Chenfeng might have a preference for men.
He was not far away. When he heard Xi Youqin's words, he felt a little emotional. He had never met such a woman before …
Wave after wave of beasts swept over.
The number of martial artists in the three blood-colored castles decreased rapidly from 2,500.
Two days and two nights later …
Most of the martial artists were already exhausted, and the wave after wave of Beast Tide finally stopped.
At this moment, there were only 300 martial artists left in the blood-colored hall. This round of elimination eliminated 90% of the martial artists.
The rules of the Illusion Battlefield were set up to be extremely cruel. The final goal was to select the main character after the Battle of the Era …
In the first stage, only 2,500 out of 100 million Divine Sea Realm martial artists were selected.
In the second stage, only three hundred of the two thousand five hundred martial artists would be selected.
Under such a selection, the group of people who remained in the end were the prodigies and Daoists of the various major races.
And these 300 people were destined to be the most dazzling 300 stars in the universe!
Not long after, the tops of the three blood-red castles opened up and joined together, forming three massive arched bridges that connected head to tail. And in the center of these three castles, a thick purple pillar of light slowly rose up. Within this pillar of light, there were fishes that swam about. They emitted the might of the heavens and earth, enough to make all living beings submit to them!
There were only a hundred martial practitioners left in each Blood Castle.
Now that the castle was open, they all jumped onto the arched bridge and walked towards the purple light pillar in the center.
"We should go too," Luo Zheng said to Xi Youqin with a smile.
She didn't even look at the purple beam of light when it appeared. She was still nestled in Luo Zheng's arms.
Xi Youqin nodded slightly and followed behind Luo Zheng onto the arched bridge.
In the 50 Big Worlds, the most powerful people who were selected from the hundreds of millions of martial artists were gathered here.
In this encounter, in addition to observing the fishes swimming in the purple light pillar, everyone was also silently sizing each other up.
Some of them already knew each other …
For example, Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Lie Qianhan.
Some people had distinctive features, such as Jiang Zhengyi, who was wearing a heavy helmet and had a righteous face. At a glance, one could tell that he was the Daoist of the Demi-Immortal Clan …
Of course, Luo Zheng himself was extremely famous.
But there were only a few people who had actually seen him …
However, before he could get close to the purple light pillar, a person with a sword blocked in front of Luo Zheng.
"This is really surprising. I thought that with your strength, you wouldn't even be able to enter the second stage. I didn't expect that not only did you successfully enter the second stage, but you also didn't get eliminated … Interesting! Really interesting! "
The sword in this person's hand was an inconspicuous dark iron sword, which was even a little broken.
This time, he blocked Luo Zheng's path and slightly lowered his long sword with an arrogant expression. It was Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, whom Luo Zheng had met in the Karma Hall!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who was not far away, heard Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's words, and his eyes flashed slightly …
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao came from Gods Without Thoughts and had participated in the Four Clans Competition before, so he naturally knew this person. However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was also curious. How did this Luo Zheng provoke this person?
Hearing this, everyone's eyes immediately focused on this side.
They had more or less heard of Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao and his magical sword …
However, they didn't know that this guy seemed to have a grudge with another person. Why did he want to provoke him like this?
Hearing Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's words, Luo Zheng replied indifferently, "It's not that interesting. At least, I'll give you a chance."
"What do you mean, give me a chance?" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was slightly taken aback.
"I'll give you a chance to eliminate me," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
As soon as he said this, the others were also slightly taken aback.
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was only a prodigy of Gods Without Thoughts, but with that strange sword in his hand, his strength was probably not inferior to a Daozi's!
Who was this young man in front of them?
His tone was so arrogant that he didn't even take Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao seriously?
"Very good. Then I'll have to thank you for giving me this chance!" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's eyes were filled with anger. He had been tricked by Luo Zheng in the Karma Hall, and he was very unwilling to accept it. Losing in the Karma Hall was a great insult to him, and he wanted to settle this score with Luo Zheng.
Because of this dispute, a few of them quickly noticed Luo Zheng …
Not far away, there was a woman dressed in purple. Her skin was as white as snow, and she was slim and elegant. She was Ji Luoxue, who had once lost to Luo Zheng.
When she saw Luo Zheng, snowflakes fluttered in the air, and she also slowly walked over. Her gaze focused on Luo Zheng, and she said calmly, "We meet again."
After entering the second stage of the Illusory Battlefield, Ji Luoxue took this opportunity to merge her two bodies. At this moment, Ji Luoxue was in her true, complete form, and her aura had changed slightly.
"Who is she?"
At this moment, Xi Youqin saw that Ji Luoxue's appearance was extraordinary, but she was hugging Luo Zheng's right arm. She frowned slightly and stared at Ji Luoxue.
Noticing Xi Youqin and Luo Zheng's intimate actions, Ji Luoxue's eyes revealed a trace of astonishment. Judging from Xi Youqin and Luo Zheng's intimate actions, they should be a couple who knew each other. However, Ji Luoxue knew nothing about their backgrounds. Which clan were they from?
Before Luo Zheng could speak, another voice sounded from the side. "Brother Luo Zheng!"
The person who came was carrying a sword on his back. His face was full of heroic spirit, and his eyes were sharp. It was Hua Tianming, who had already stepped into the Divine Ocean Realm.
"Brother Tianming!"
A long-lost smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
To be able to meet Xi Youqin and Hua Tianming at the same time here, Luo Zheng was indeed extremely surprised.
Meeting old friends, of course he was happy …
But it was also because everyone heard Hua Tianming call out "Brother Luo Zheng" that they suddenly understood.
So this person was Luo Zheng!
At this moment, not only was Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao surprised, even Ji Luoxue, who had fought fiercely with Luo Zheng before, had a strange glint in her eyes.
Ever since she entered the Illusory Battlefield, she had only tasted defeat once, and it was at the hands of this person in front of her.
However, she had never imagined that this guy was actually Luo Zheng, who often told stories …
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1312
In the second stage, the Dream Points that everyone collected would no longer determine the ranking of the Dream Battlefield.
Now, it was a process of elimination. As long as the statue was destroyed, it would be eliminated.
Therefore, every martial artist would invest all their Dream Points into the statue before the end of the time period to strengthen it.
100,000 Dream Points could only strengthen one's statue once, and the degree of strengthening wasn't obvious.
They knew that they would have to rely on themselves for the next battle. It was obviously impossible to rely on the statue to fight.
Therefore, most of the martial artists would use their Dream Points to strengthen the defense. This was also the safest method. After all, the martial artists who entered the top 2,500 would have absolute confidence in their strength.
As for the statue that was made of an unknown stone? As long as it didn't shatter and cause him to be eliminated …
There was originally a purple star on the chest of the statue.
When they strengthened it 100 times, which meant that they consumed 50 million Dream Points, another purple star would appear on their chest!
Most of the martial artists had more than 5,000 Dream Points. Only the martial artists who were ranked outside the top 2,000 would have slightly lower Dream Points. Therefore, when everyone used up the Dream Points that they had accumulated for a long time, a purple mark would appear, and a second purple star would appear.
During the strengthening process, a martial artist in heavy armor brought his statue to the second stage.
This warrior had a square face, and his equipment was very exaggerated. In his left hand was a giant shield that was as tall as a person, and in his right hand was a silver heavy sword. His eyes were bright and threatening, and his expression was incomparably serious. The statue behind him also held a giant shield and heavy sword in its hands.
Luo Zheng was methodically strengthening his statue. When he saw a flash of white light in the corner of his eye, he glanced at it indifferently, and his eyebrows twitched imperceptibly.
This person was ranked seventh in the pyramid!
Because of his heavy equipment, Luo Zheng had a deep impression of him. However, he didn't know which force this guy came from.
As soon as this person appeared, everyone whispered to each other.
"Jiang Zhengyi of the Sky Immortal Clan …"
"I heard that he is a psychopath …"
"Don't underestimate this person. He is the Dao Child of the Sky Immortal Clan!"
When these discussions fell into Luo Zheng's ears, his heart skipped a beat.
So this is the Dao Child?
Now, Luo Zheng already knew that the Dao Child was a martial artist who had fully opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower. However, this person looked strange. Which Divine Path did he cultivate?
"Justice No. 2! Await orders here! "
Jiang Zhengyi waved the heavy sword in his hand and pointed to a position. His statue stood there according to his order. At the same time, it also knelt on one knee and kowtowed to him.
Luo Zheng was dumbfounded by this scene.
He had already said that this statue was his avatar, and yet he was controlling an avatar to kneel before him. Could this person really be crazy? How could a psychopath become a Dao Child?
After Jiang Zhengyi issued the order, he began to use his consciousness to browse the bamboo slips like the others …
After making a decision, he actually began to choose to strengthen his strength and exchanged all his Fantasy Points for the strength bamboo slips!
Rays of red light surrounded his statue! Every time the red light flickered, it meant that the statue was strengthened. From the looks of it, he was probably preparing to strengthen his strength!
Everyone looked at the Sky Immortal Dao Child as if they were looking at an idiot.
This statue was as fragile as mud. It would shatter with a light touch. What was the use of purely strengthening his strength?
If he used the statue to punch it, it would probably be smashed to pieces. Did Jiang Zhengyi not plan to pass the second stage?
A martial artist sneered and said, "I've always heard that the Sky Immortal Jiang Zhengyi is a strange fellow. Today, I've seen it for myself. He's no different from an idiot."
Jiang Zhengyi seemed to be concentrating on his statue. After hearing this, he suddenly turned his head and said to the martial artist, "It's extremely immoral to talk about others behind their backs!"
After saying that, Jiang Zhengyi waved the heavy sword in his hand and aimed it at the martial artist. The bright sword body reflected the martial artist's face. Jiang Zhengyi continued to say, "I'll remember your face. I can't punish you here, but if I meet you in the outside world, I won't let you go!"
When he said this, his expression was extremely serious. It didn't seem like he was joking. It was as if he had to do what he said.
Hearing this, the martial artist's face suddenly became a little ugly …
Even if Jiang Zhengyi was an idiot, he was still a Daoist of the Sky Immortal Clan. Rumor had it that this guy never joked and always did what he said.
After Jiang Zhengyi finished speaking, he no longer paid attention to the Martial Artist. He continued to invest Fantasy Points to enhance the power of his statue.
Soon after, a purple light flashed, and a second purple star appeared on his statue's chest. This meant that Jiang Zhengyi had invested more than 50 million Dream points, but he didn't stop there. He continued to strengthen the statue's power.
However, no one expected that not long after, the chest of Jiang Zhengyi's statue flashed with purple light again.
A third purple star appeared!
"How can there be a third purple star?"
"I'm afraid that Jiang Zhengyi has more than 100 million Dream Points! That's why a third purple star appeared! "
"So many Dream Points are wasted on strengthening the statue. It's such a waste …"
No one knew how to quantify the statue's strength, so they could only make a rough estimate.
However, they could guess that the purple star on the statue represented the statue's degree of strengthening …
Seeing this scene, everyone sighed inwardly. This guy has so many Dream Points, but he wants to waste them on strengthening the statue!
It should be known that there were only two ways to strengthen the statue. One was to strengthen the statue's defense indefinitely, and the other was to strengthen it evenly like Luo Zheng. What was the use of strengthening the statue?
However, no one reminded Jiang Zhengyi. Even if they did, he wouldn't listen.
After Luo Zheng strengthened the speed jade slip, he started to strengthen the power and defense jade slips.
However, at this moment, a strange scene happened …
He hadn't strengthened it for long when a purple light flashed on his statue's chest, and the second purple star appeared!
According to their speculation, the second five-star needed 50 million Dream Points to appear, but Luo Zheng had spent less than 8 million Dream Points. How could there be another purple star?
At this moment, no one paid attention to Jiang Zhengyi.
Although they knew that Jiang Zhengyi's rank was very high in the entire Dream Battlefield, they didn't know how many Dream Points he had accumulated. Now, they could use this point to make a rough calculation!
As Jiang Zhengyi was still strengthening, everyone held their breath and stared at the scene in front of them.
Not long after, the fourth purple star appeared!
In other words, Jiang Zhengyi had at least 200 million Dream Points …
The warriors who had entered the top 2,500 were somewhat arrogant. Since they had climbed to the top 2,500, they thought that the gap between them and the experts in front of them wasn't that big!
However, Jiang Zhengyi had more than 200 million Dream Points, but he was only ranked seventh?
How many Dream Points did the sixth place plunder?
What about the fifth place?
Fourth, third, second …
At this moment, they all sucked in a breath of cold air, finally realizing how big the gap between them and the Daoist geniuses in front of them was!
Luo Zheng's attention was also attracted by the commotion.
"This guy really has 200 million Dream Points?" Luo Zheng's pupils shrank slightly.
No wonder …
Luo Zheng had plundered nearly 100 million Dream Points in that big world, but he still couldn't surpass the first place. It seemed that the first place had a lot of Dream Points.
These guys were really abnormal. The higher the rank, the bigger the gap between the warriors …
After a long time, Jiang Zhengyi finally stopped strengthening the statue. According to the time when he stopped strengthening, everyone guessed that he had between 230 million and 240 million Dream Points. He definitely hadn't reached 250 million. Otherwise, he would have strengthened the fifth purple star.
As for Luo Zheng, he also accelerated his strengthening speed …
He was still doubtful. The second purple star had appeared on his statue very early. At first, he thought that it was because he had distributed it better, so the statue's strength would be affected more.

Chapter 1313
The mysterious girl's gaze pierced through the layers of clouds and the purple light. She stared at Luo Zheng in a daze, and her eyebrows slightly furrowed.
"Luo Zheng is a person without a destiny?"
The Fateless in this universe were extremely rare, and their experiences were often very rough.
Others didn't know how the Fateless came to be, but she was very clear about it.
When a living being was born, their destiny was fixed. No living being in this universe could jump out of this hole …
To become a person without a destiny, either he didn't come from the Heavenly Dao, or he had the Heart Stone, and experienced the cycle of life and death to erase his destiny.
But what was his purpose?
Thinking of the Luo Yan that Luo Zheng asked her to find, she fell into deep thought.
Hearing the mysterious girl's words, Luo Zheng also had some doubts.
The number of the Dayan was fifty …
It seemed to coincide with the number of fifty in the Illusory Battlefield.
If two were taken, there would be a fixed number and a variable number!
Why was there such a division of destiny? What were the variables?
At present, there were two hundred people with fixed destiny, and ninety-nine people with variable names …
People like Xi Youqin and Hua Tianming were variables, and Xuanyuan Chenfeng was fixed destiny. Obviously, this division wasn't based on strength.
"Since I'm a Fateless, I can't even be counted as a variable?"
This matter of destiny was too vague. Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out at the moment, so he stopped thinking.
Maybe there was an invisible hand in this universe that was arranging something, or maybe there was an invisible figure behind him that was arranging something.
However, Luo Zheng would only act according to his heart.
If this destiny was his, he would follow it.
If this destiny was against him, he would break it!
…
…
At this moment.
At the top of the Illusory Battlefield of all the big clans in the universe, the pyramid formed by the names began to dissipate …
The light on the stars in the sky also retracted from the top of each big world.
Except for the three hundred people, all the other Divine Sea Realm warriors returned from the Illusory Battlefield …
Ning Yudie stared at the collapsing pyramid with a strange light flashing in her eyes.
"Has the Illusory Battlefield ended?" Ning Yudie asked.
Ninth Palace Master smiled and shook her head. "The most important thing in this Illusory Battlefield is the fight for the top position. This is just the beginning. However … soon, you will not only see the names."
"I can see Luo Zheng?" Ning Yudie's eyes flashed.
The Ninth Palace Master nodded slightly. "According to the usual practice, this should be the case. However, I don't know if Luo Zheng has been eliminated. The second stage of the Illusory Battlefield is very harsh. It is a competition between many heaven's prides. It is already very good for Luo Zheng to enter the top two thousand five hundred.
In the second round, the two thousand five hundred martial artists had to go through rounds of elimination.
However, ever since they entered the Blood Red Castle, the names of the first two thousand five hundred warriors were fixed at the front.
No one knew whether they had been eliminated or not.
And now, it was time to reveal it.
Just as he said that …
The Illusory Space in the hall began to flash with magnificent colors.
Those colors were like the most illusory energy. They began to flow and smear. At the top of the hall, a magnificent picture of a fairyland appeared.
Immortal mountains, clouds, and pavilions …
There was also a figure with Ying Ying's pupils standing at the top of the clouds.
This kind of evolution was slowly displayed in all the Illusory Spaces in the universe …
The Illusory Space was similar to the Roaring Command. The Roaring Command used the law of causality to send sound to the entire universe, while the Illusory Space used the law of causality to create a projection.
This was not a product of the universe. It was a kind of grace that the mysterious girl had given to all the living beings in the universe. She had released a privilege, which was why it was so easy for the Holy Lands to build these Illusory Spaces.
At this moment, the entire universe could see the scene inside through the reverse projection of the Illusory Space!
Those figures became clearer and clearer. Ning Yudie's eyes became more and more serious.
Not only her, but all the martial artists in the universe who were watching the Illusory Battlefield looked very serious at this moment …
"I see him."
Ning Yudie's eyes suddenly flashed. Her gaze was firmly fixed on Luo Zheng.
At this moment, Ning Yudie was also very excited.
Since ascending, she had maintained the reverence of an ascender for the entire Upper Realm.
In the Great Thousand World where she came from, Divine Sea Realm martial artists were rare. However, there were trillions of them in this universe. There were geniuses everywhere. Even a tenth-rank Holy Land could not control a large world. However, there were more than 100,000 of such worlds …
There were greater opportunities here, but also greater challenges.
She had overestimated her husband. It was not easy for him to enter this tenth-rank Holy Land.
She never thought that he would stand at the peak in just a few years …
The person she looked up to was her pride!
Her eyes gradually became blurred. However, all of a sudden, her eyebrows rose. Her eyes revealed a strong sense of depression.
There was a woman snuggling up to Luo Zheng with a happy face. This woman was Xi Youqin.
"Luo Zheng, is he among them?" Ninth Palace Master did not know Luo Zheng, so he could only ask her.
Ning Yudie lowered her eyes and said with an unnatural expression, "He is …"
"You really live up to expectations! Congratulations, "Ninth Palace Master said with a smile, but when he saw Ning Yudie's expression, he couldn't help but mutter to himself. This time, this woman should be happy to see Luo Zheng, so why was her expression so ugly? So Ninth Palace Master asked again, "I wonder which one of them is Luo Zheng?"
Ning Yudie said faintly, "The thirteenth one from the right."
Ninth Palace Master looked over. His eyes narrowed, and a strange look appeared on his face. He was also a smart person.
The thirteenth one from the right was a handsome young man, and beside this young man was a beautiful woman … No wonder Ning Yudie's expression was so ugly.
Everyone had a gossipy heart.
Ninth Palace Master was also curious. This Luo Zheng was an expert in flirting. The woman beside him must also be someone with extraordinary strength. In this short period of time in the Illusory Battlefield, he could stick to this woman like glue. He was really amazing …
In fact, it was not only Ninth Palace Master.
Now, the whole world could see the scene of Xi Youqin showing off her love!
Those who knew Luo Zheng admired him in their hearts. No matter who that woman was, she was at least a genius from a big force. This Luo Zheng was really amazing. How many days had it been in the Illusory Battlefield? His actions were too fast!
Those who did not know him naturally admired him. They also secretly guessed the identity of Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin. Could it be that these two people were a pair of Taoist companions who had entered the top 300 at the same time?
"Sisters, look! That should be Luo Zheng! "
"Youqin has good taste! That guy is quite handsome! "
"Of course. Although there is no such thing as a genius in our Extreme Purple Realm, Youqin is now a genius of our Extreme Purple Realm. How can the person she likes be weaker than her?"
"…"
Because of the special nature of the Extreme Purple Realm, they couldn't build a dreamy space. Many warriors in the Uncertain Tower had to cross the Extreme Purple Realm to see this scene. They had to cross the Extreme Purple Realm to go to another big realm and use other people's territory to see it.
Ninth Palace Master was curious and could not help but ask, "Do you know the woman beside Luo Zheng?"
"Xi Youqin," Ning Yudie replied coldly.
Although Ning Yudie already knew that Xi Youqin was ranked at the top, how could she be happy when she saw this scene with her own eyes?
"Oh? You know this woman? Where is she from … "Ninth Palace Master asked again. It was fine if she did not ask, but the more she asked, the more curious she became.
"She is from the same Great Thousand World as me," Ning Yudie replied again.
"What!"
Ninth Palace Master was shocked at this moment.
Among the final 300 people selected from the Illusory Battlefield, two of them were from the same Great Thousand World?
This kind of coincidence made her unable to wrap her head around it.
One had to know that only four people had entered the Illusory Battlefield among the 3,000 great realms of the Human Dao Alliance, and Luo Zheng was one of them …
How could someone from the Lower Realms obtain two quotas? This was simply too inconceivable.
"You're saying that Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin are both from the same Great Thousand World that you ascended to?" Ninth Palace Master confirmed again.

Chapter 1314
Hua Tianming went out to train and encountered a fortuitous encounter and ascended.
However, he did not leave the Cloud Hall and was still considered a member of the Cloud Hall …
It was not inappropriate to regard him as Ning Yudie's disciple. This was the truth.
However, when the Ninth Palace Master of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground heard Ning Yudie's words, he was stunned.
He stared blankly at Ning Yudie. Judging from her expression, it did not seem like she was spouting nonsense …
However, among these three hundred people, three of them came from the same Greater World? This was too inconceivable!
Although Ning Yudie realized that these three hundred people were extraordinary, she was not from the Upper Realm. She only knew that these three hundred people were the most outstanding people in the Divine Ocean Realm.
However, she did not understand the significance of these three hundred prodigies to the various clans. She did not know what kind of brilliant future they would have, and she did not know how much effort and resources a large force would need to cultivate a prodigy!
However, the Ninth Palace Master was very clear.
A tenth-grade Sacred Ground like the Jade Pure Sacred Ground was not qualified to cultivate a prodigy!
Seeing the Ninth Palace Master looking at her as if she was a monster, Ning Yudie said straightforwardly, "Speaking of which, Luo Zheng is also a member of the Cloud Hall. He's also considered my disciple."
"…"
The Ninth Palace Master was stunned for a while before he asked, "May I ask what rank Sacred Ground this Cloud Hall is?"
In the Ninth Palace Master's impression, there was no Sacred Ground above Rank Eight in the Lower Realm, right?
"It's considered a Rank Six Sacred Ground," Ning Yudie answered truthfully.
With the current status of the Cloud Hall in the Central Domain, it was already considered a Rank Six Sacred Ground …
"Rank Six Sacred Ground …"
The Ninth Palace Mistress muttered to herself. In the Upper Realm, a Rank Six Holy Land was a force that could not even be mentioned in public. For example, the Jade Pure Holy Land had countless small Holy Lands under it, but they were all Rank Eight, Rank Nine …
A Rank Six Sacred Ground was not even qualified to be a subordinate Sacred Ground of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground!
If someone like the Ninth Palace Master personally visited a Rank Six Sacred Ground, the Lord of the Rank Six Sacred Ground would probably kneel to welcome him.
But in such a sixth-grade Holy Land, there were actually two martial artists that stood in the top 300 rankings of the Illusory Battlefield!
Ninth Palace Master's head was also buzzing. She didn't know how to rely on such a bizarre matter.
Ning Yudie ignored Ninth Palace Master and continued to stare at the illusory realm outlined above the Illusory Space. At this moment, she recalled Xi Youqin's mysterious appearance at that time, and then she escaped into the Extreme Purple Realm. Presumably, it was for today …
…
All the forces in the universe were paying close attention to the picture scroll above the Illusory Space. Naturally, there were all kinds of discussions.
"With Xuanyuan Chenfeng entering the competition, our Xuanyuan Clan will definitely be number one!"
"Wang Luoxue will be able to step into the Divine Dao with this battle! Become a Daoist … "
"The God of Barbarians will be born among us demons …"
"…"
Many warriors in Misty Cloud Heavenly Palace craned their necks to look at the warriors in the picture scroll …
"Luo Zheng has indeed passed the second stage and is ranked among them." Xiao Daolai, the former Palace Lord of Misty Cloud Palace, shook his head and smiled.
"Master …" Standing beside Xiao Daolai was his beloved disciple, Ai Anxin.
Ai Anxin had actually also passed the first stage, but she was eliminated in the second stage.
She and Luo Zheng didn't belong to the same scarlet castle. Instead, she was in the same castle as Xi Youqin. However, before Luo Zheng moved over, she had already been eliminated, so she didn't meet Luo Zheng.
"I'm already very satisfied that Anxin can enter the second stage," Xiao Daolai said with a smile. He naturally knew that his beloved disciple had done her best.
As her master, he was very clear about the cruelty of the Illusory Battlefield. Even the prides of those great forces had also been eliminated. How could he ask Ai Anxin not to enter the top 300?
"Who is the girl beside Luo Zheng?" someone beside him asked.
Yun Luo, who was standing in the center, stared at Xi Youqin. This girl was very unfamiliar to her.
Her doppelgangers in the Vast Thousand World were in the Eastern Region and the Sea God Continent. She had never seen Xi Youqin before …
"This girl seems to be using the sword formation of the Extreme Purple Realm." Ai Anxin had some impression of Xi Youqin. The sword formation of the Extreme Purple Realm was very special, and she had recognized it at that time.
"A pride from the Extreme Purple Realm? Luo Zheng is really good at seducing girls! They're like glue in such a short time, haha … "someone beside her laughed.
"But I don't think it's the first time she's met Luo Zheng …"
…
Luo Zheng stood where he was, waiting quietly with the other warriors.
After ascending from the purple light, everyone had escaped into a fairyland shrouded in clouds and mist.
After being baptized by the countless Rules of Heaven in the purple light, everyone had a new kind of understanding in their hearts.
The imprints left by the swimming fish in their bodies allowed them to construct the rudiments of the Rules of their own Inner Worlds.
While waiting, everyone focused on comprehending the Divine Scripture …
"The reward for the top ten in this round of the Illusory Battlefield is ten God Spark Disks. When the time comes, I will personally place them in your dantian …"
"Huff, huff, huff …"
The mysterious girl's voice suddenly sounded.
Ten gray discs slowly floated in the sky.
The three hundred warriors below looked up, and many of their eyes shone brightly!
Of course, there were also many people who didn't know what they were …
For example, Luo Zheng, Xi Youqin, Hua Tianming, and others.
"Luo Zheng, what are God Spark Disks?" Xi Youqin looked up and asked.
Luo Zheng shook his head and stared at the ten disks in the sky, a puzzled look on his face.
In the Emperor's Corridor, Luo Zheng knew that to become a True God, one had to have their own Godhead …
But he didn't know what these God Spark Disks were.
"Hey, you don't even know this?" A warrior next to him looked disdainful. "An Inner World can't contain a Godhead! These God Spark Disks are used to contain the Godhead. Only by putting them into your Inner World can you contain them when you have your own Godhead in the future! "
"Then, where do the Godheads come from?" Xi Youqin asked again.
"This …" That warrior couldn't answer this question. There were very few True Gods in the universe. The warriors in this group were either Heaven's Chosen or Dao's Chosen. Although the chances of them becoming True Gods weren't small, they were still Divine Sea Realm warriors. There was still a long way to go before they could become True Gods. They had only heard about these secrets from rumors. How could he answer Xi Youqin?
"You are the favored children of this round of evolution. The Heavenly Dao will naturally give you the greatest opportunity. Even if you are unable to compete for the top ten, you will still be strengthened. The basis for the strengthening will be the strengthening of the statues in the second stage …"
Hearing the mysterious girl's voice, everyone's faces were filled with surprise and joy.
In the second stage, they had invested all their Fantasy Points into the statue and strengthened it greatly.
Whether it was strength, soul strength, or some kind of law strengthening …
And these investments would be returned to them?
These people had invested tens of millions or even hundreds of millions of Fantasy Points. The degree of strengthening could be said to be huge!
This reward was something no one had expected.
Luo Zheng was also slightly taken aback when he heard this.
Compared to the other warriors, he didn't invest many Fantasy Points, but he knew that his statue was probably much stronger than the others! It could be said that it was comparable to his statue. And these strengthening would be returned to him?
"As for the top three among you, there will naturally be other rewards. At the same time, I have to warn you that while you receive the rewards, you will also bear an even heavier fate …"
Hu hu hu …
After she finished speaking, a huge square chessboard appeared in front of them. This chessboard was a hundred thousand feet long.
At the same time …
A pink figure quietly appeared in the center of the chessboard.
That figure's face couldn't be seen clearly, but when she spoke, everyone knew that she was the host of this Illusory Battlefield …
Luo Zheng also deliberately looked at this illusory figure a few times, trying to recognize the appearance of this mysterious girl, but he did not know what method she had used. At a casual glance, this illusory figure was a graceful woman, but if one looked carefully, it was like a dream, like a shadow, and there was no way to distinguish it.
