Chapter 1315
The pink figure gently floated down like a fairy in an illusion world.
Just like Luo Zheng, the entire universe knew of the existence of this mysterious girl.
However, they knew nothing about her origin, purpose, or where she existed …
Like Luo Zheng, the other cultivators also wanted to distinguish her appearance, but at a glance, they couldn't see her true appearance.
When the pink figure floated to the edge of the chessboard and stood in front of everyone, she reached out and waved her hand gently. Black and white circular chess pieces appeared around her.
These chess pieces were about ten feet wide. After floating in the air, they floated towards everyone.
"Geniuses, please step onto the chess pieces …"
After the pink figure opened her mouth, her cold voice sounded again.
But when everyone heard it, they found that this voice didn't come from here, but from the sky …
Presumably, this pink figure wasn't her real body. What everyone saw was just an illusion.
However, she was the host of this Illusory Battlefield. As soon as her voice fell, the three hundred people stepped onto the chess pieces together.
Even if Xi Youqin didn't want to be separated from Luo Zheng, she had no choice at this moment.
The sharp-eyed people noticed at this moment …
Previously, in the purple light pillar, everyone was divided into two sides. One was the fixed number, and the other was the variable.
At this moment, all the fixed number cultivators were standing on white chess pieces, and all the variable cultivators were standing on black chess pieces.
As for Luo Zheng, he wasn't divided at first, but at this moment, he was also standing on a black chess piece.
"Huhuhu …"
When everyone stepped onto the chess pieces, they instantly floated into the air under the control of the pink figure.
The cultivators standing on the black chess pieces were divided into one side, the white chess pieces were on the other side, and the pink figure floated in the middle.
For Luo Zheng and the other three hundred cultivators, this was the final stage of the Illusory Battlefield.
But for her, it was to predict the fate of this round of evolution amidst the variables and fixed numbers!
The great crisis that erupted in this round of evolution was a magnificent game of chess with the world as the chessboard and trillions of creatures as the chess pieces.
Even she had no idea where the danger would come from …
However, gathering the most outstanding prodigies in the future and playing a game of chess was a form of deduction in advance.
She wanted to see clearly whether the danger of this round of development came from the variable or from the fixed number …
At this moment, countless eyes in the universe were staring at the chessboard. From the 128 Empyreans to the ordinary martial artists, all of them were paying close attention …
There were even some cultivators who saw this as an excellent opportunity to set up a gambling house. They lined up the three hundred cultivators one by one and began to place their bets.
The pink figure stretched out her hand and casually grabbed!
A giant hand appeared from the endless clouds. It was a giant jade hand.
Although that hand was huge, everyone could tell that it was the hand of a young girl. Her fingers were delicate and fair, slender and straight like scallions.
The index finger of this hand pressed towards a white stone, and the nail of the middle finger clasped the bottom of the white stone!
The person standing on the white chess piece was a sky-pride from the Shaye Clan!
When he saw the huge index finger pressing down on him, he was shocked. However, when the index finger pressed down on him, it penetrated his body and pressed down on the chess piece.
After the giant jade hand clasped the white stone, it pressed down on the huge chessboard!
Smack!
With a crisp sound, the chess piece was placed!
The Shaye Clan cultivator was still standing steadily on the white stone …
He also understood that his opponent would be a martial artist among the Black Chess pieces!
Soon, his guess was confirmed. When the giant jade hand retracted into the clouds, it reached out from the other side and clasped another black stone.
This time, the giant jade hand clasped the chess piece that Xi Youqin was on!
Xi Youqin was slightly taken aback …
She did not expect that the first black stone would be her.
Smack!
The black stone was placed and left the hand.
"The two of them can fight to determine victory and defeat, not life and death …" The mysterious girl's voice rang in everyone's ears again.
"I'll be the first one!"
Xi Youqin looked gloomy …
She did not like to fight with others. The number of times she had fought with others in the Illusory Battlefield this time was probably ten times more than the number of times she had fought in her entire life!
Xi Youqin had fought very hard in the previous two stages.
However, she had her own motive, which was to work hard to see Luo Zheng.
Now that Luo Zheng was by her side, part of her long-cherished wish had been fulfilled. It was meaningless for her to fight with others. Feeling gloomy, she turned around to look at Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng smiled at Xi Youqin. "You can do it, Youqin!"
Xi Youqin pursed her lips and nodded reluctantly …
She did not know that her master, Indefinite Old Man, was in a corner of the universe, watching her nervously with a group of senior sisters.
"Youqin doesn't seem to have any fighting spirit …"
"Sigh, her competitive spirit seems to be all about love. She has little interest in martial arts!"
"It's so unfair. God gave her such amazing talent but did not give her the heart to pursue martial arts …"
After some thought, Xi Youqin stepped down from the black chess piece. Although there was still some resistance in her heart, with a flick of her ten fingers, seven flying swords of different colors slowly floated out from her body, and the seven swords slowly spread out...
She stared at the Xaye Clan's warrior not far away and pursed her lips. "Okay, you can start now …"
Seeing this, Luo Zheng could not help but shake his head.
Xi Youqin did not improve at all. She had no fighting spirit at all. She was no different from her past self.
He really could not figure out how she had reached this stage in the Illusory Battlefield …
However, Luo Zheng did not know that in the first stage of the Illusory Battlefield, Xi Youqin was a completely different person than she was now!
At that time, in order to find Luo Zheng, Xi Youqin shuttled through one main city after another. Her aura was not strong to begin with, and she looked like a delicate human girl. Naturally, she was attacked countless times.
At that time, her mind was extremely steady, and her fighting spirit was overflowing. Anyone who stopped her from finding Luo Zheng would become her mortal enemy. Thus, she was like a God of Death, and the number of warriors she had killed was unknown.
However, at that moment, all she wanted was to stay by Luo Zheng's side. How could she have any fighting spirit?
Now that she was forced onto the chessboard, she had no choice but to make a move. It was like forcing a duck onto a perch …
As for the Xaye Clan warrior, when he saw that his first opponent was a delicate human girl who had no fighting spirit, a satisfied smile appeared on his face.
His Xaye Clan had been silent for too long. This ancient clan could only be considered a second-rate clan in the universe …
If he took first place in the Illusory Battlefield and received the Godhead Compass, he would become a Dao Child in the future and lead his clan to glory again.
Thinking of this, the Xaye Clan warrior lightly shook his shoulders.
Two thin totems rose from the back of his shoulders. Then, traces of ancient aura spread out from the totems.
The Xaye Clan was also an ancient clan. They worshipped their ancestors.
Every generation of the Xaye Clan was accumulating power for the next generation …
As the son of the 36330th Great Chieftain of the Xaye Clan, he could obtain great power from his ancestors!
"In the first battle, you only need to show 20% or 30% of your power."

Chapter 1316
In the sacred land of the Shaye Clan, a giant totem stood tall.
The dreamscape was built at the bottom of the giant totem. Naturally, the picture that was displayed in the dreamscape would appear in front of the totem.
At this moment, hundreds of thousands of members of the Shaye Clan were sitting quietly around the totem, looking at the huge picture in front of them.
The Shaye Clan was considered a second-rate clan in the universe. They had only produced one Heavenly Venerate and had never produced a True God. The entire Shaye Clan relied on this Heavenly Venerate to hold on and try to become a first-rate clan.
However, compared to the first-rate clans such as the humans, demons, and demons, the Shaye Clan was very weak.
Today, however, the most outstanding genius of their clan had reached the pinnacle of the Dreamscape War.
For the Shaye Clan who had been frustrated for a long time, this might be a chance for them to rise again. This genius was the most precious seed that would grow into a towering tree sooner or later. He would become the new protector of the Shaye Clan and the first True God of the Shaye Clan.
"Yodi, his first opponent is a human woman."
"Yodi's luck is not bad. He didn't meet those Daoists."
"This woman looks like she has no fighting spirit. Hehe, Yodi has met a pushover."
The hundreds of thousands of people discussed like buzzing bees. The entire area around the giant totem was in chaos.
Under the giant totem, the Shaye Clan's Chieftain raised a long spear in his hand and held it horizontally. It was a gesture to keep quiet.
Immediately, all the Shaye Clan members stopped talking. Then, the Chieftain roared, "Ancestors, bless the Shaye Clan."
"Ancestors, bless Yodi!"
"May the souls of the ancestors live on forever."
"Buzz …"
All the Shaye Clan members roared at the same time.
The giant totem was the Shaye Clan's ancestral totem. After the warriors of the Shaye Clan died, their physical bodies would be burned but their souls would live on forever.
They poured the souls of their ancestors into the ancestral totem. The ancestral totem could not protect the soul from destruction but with the infusion of countless ancestors' souls, the ancestral totem contained the will of each generation of ancestors.
"Buzz …"
At the same time, on the giant chessboard …
The two totems behind the Shaye prodigy named Yodi were also emitting a faint glow.
Holding the black spear, a faint smile appeared on Yodi's face. "Father, you're too nervous. I don't need to summon the power of my ancestors in the first battle!"
When the ancestral power was summoned, all the totems owned by the Shaye warriors would be able to transmit a powerful ancestral power!
However, the ancestral power was not directed at Youdi alone. It was directed at the warriors of the Shaye Clan!
It was only during times of war that the Shaye Tribe would summon the power of their ancestors. But now, they were summoning the power of their ancestors for the sake of Yodi. It was a huge waste because Yodi was the only one fighting. The power of their ancestors was meant for the entire Shaye Tribe. But even so, it was still a matter of great importance.
"Since that's the case, we must show our might in the first battle!"
You Di gently waved the black spear in his hand. Then he smiled apologetically at the delicate human girl opposite him, and his figure shot out!
The chessboard covered a vast area.
Just now, when the huge jade-like hand landed, the white piece landed on the "Heaven's Origin", and Youdi stood in the center.
Xi Youqin landed on one of the 'stars' and was still a few thousand feet away from Yodi.
However, Yodi's body suddenly moved and the distance between them was only a step away!
A slender afterimage dragged forward and suddenly disappeared at a certain point. When Youdi's figure reappeared, he was only ten feet away from Xi Youqin!
The black spear in Yodi's hand was ten feet long!
"Whoosh!"
The spear suddenly pierced forward!
However, in Xi Youqin's eyes, what she saw was a giant black python. The scales on the python's body shone with a black luster. It coiled and opened its bloody mouth to devour her!
The four sacred beasts worshipped by the ancestors of the Shaye Tribe were the Blackswamp Python, the Phantom Porcupine, the Mystical Eagle, and the Sleeping Antelope.
Outsiders did not know much about these four sacred beasts, but they were very important to the Shaye Tribe!
Xi Youqin was a little absent-minded to begin with …
Fighting with others was something she detested to begin with!
However, when she saw the giant python's mouth open 180 degrees and reveal its ghastly fangs, she could not help but panic. With a thought, seven flying swords circled in front of her!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The seven flying swords instantly formed a defensive sword array and blocked in front of Xi Youqin!
However, the most basic sword array could not stop Yodi's spear.
The spear and the spear were similar in appearance, but the biggest difference was that the spear had a hard shaft, while the spear had a slender body.
Looking at the slightly clumsy sword array, Yodi's face showed a slight smile. He thought that he would need to use thirty percent of his strength to defeat this girl, but he did not expect that he had overestimated her. He did not know how this human girl made it into the top three hundred. Her strength was probably not even on par with a core disciple of a tenth-grade Holy Land!
This battle was too easy …
The black spear in his hand shook slightly and turned into a black python that slithered left and right through the flaws in the sword formation. It instantly drilled through the sword formation and headed straight for Xi Youqin!
"Ah …"
Seeing that her sword array could not stop her opponent, Xi Youqin's face became even more panicked.
In a moment of desperation, a purple mist spread out from under her feet. She circulated the Purple Jade Cultivation Technique, and her speed increased by more than ten times as she retreated!
Luo Zheng, who was standing on the chess piece, looked at the scene in front of him with a speechless expression …
He only knew that Xi Youqin had made it into the second stage, but Luo Zheng had never seen her strength. However, when facing the Shaye Tribe member, she only knew how to run away. He did not understand how Xi Youqin obtained so many fantasy points!
Luo Zheng was not the only one who had this thought …
As the two fought, the 300 sky-prides on the chess piece, as well as the warriors in the entire universe who were watching the battle in the Illusory Space, were somewhat puzzled.
How did this woman get to this point?
The group of women from the Extreme Purple Realm was even more puzzled …
Xi Youqin came from the Extreme Purple Realm, and now she was representing the Extreme Purple Realm!
Although their junior sister usually had many shortcomings, her talent was unquestionable. With a first-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body, even if she was only at the Divine Ocean Realm, she could unleash extremely powerful combat strength.
"What's wrong with Youqin …"
"Junior sister seems to have stage fright!"
"But if this continues, she will lose in three moves!"
Those women were also very anxious. In the end, they all looked at an old woman behind them. It was the Indefinite Tower Master.
Indefinite Tower Master saw this scene and shook her head slightly. However, she did not look anxious. Instead, she said indifferently, "It was a rush. Perhaps Youqin needs some time to adapt …"
"This woman's sword formation is not very good, but her speed of escape is top-notch!"
Youdi's spear easily broke Xi Youqin's sword formation, but Xi Youqin had already retreated thousands of feet away!
Seeing this, Youdi sneered. A brown halo was emitted from the totem behind him …
"Yo …"
Then, a loud and clear cry of an eagle rang out!
When he stimulated the power of his ancestors, he had the speed of an eagle …
He suddenly spread out his hands and transformed into an eagle. He circled in the air and once again approached Xi Youqin. The black spear approached again, and at the same time, he had a leisurely look on his face. "With such strength, I think it's better for you to admit defeat …"
Xi Youqin, who had retreated thousands of feet, looked at the eagle in the air that was swooping toward her. A faint trace of fierceness appeared in her calm eyes.
She was not mentally prepared before …
Her first goal in this Illusory Battlefield was to see Luo Zheng. Now that she had achieved her goal, she did not want to fight anyone else.
But now, no matter how she retreated, this person in front of her would eventually defeat or even kill her.
It was useless to keep retreating. Moreover, at this moment, she remembered her master's instructions. It did not matter whether she could get a ranking or not. She only hoped that she could do her best.
She could not be eliminated in the first round …

Chapter 1317
A murderous look suddenly appeared on Xi Youqin's elegant face!
The Little Purple River Sword Array …
Her master had told her that the Extreme Purple Realm was an independent entity and did not belong to the universe.
This was once the cultivation place of a True God, and it was also a world that he created himself. However, the Rules of Heaven Way could not be fully developed here, so the Extreme Purple Realm could only be regarded as a broken world. That was why the Roaring Token and the Dreamy Space, which contained the law of karma, could not be transmitted to the Extreme Purple Realm!
In the Extreme Purple Realm, there was a pass-through sword array inheritance called the Myriad Purple and Red Sword Array …
This Myriad Purple and Red Sword Array was indeed powerful, but any woman with an Extreme Purple Yin Body could cultivate it.
However, in addition to this pass-through sword array, there were three Ultimate Killing Sword Arrays in the Extreme Purple Realm!
One of them was the Little Purple River Sword Array, which could be cultivated by a third-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body. There were 4,200 people in the Extreme Purple Realm who could cultivate this array.
The second one was the Yin and Yang Sword Array, which could be cultivated by a second-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body. There were more than 80 people in the Extreme Purple Realm who could cultivate this sword array.
The third one was the God-slaying Sword Array, which was the legacy of that True God. So far, only Xi Youqin was qualified to inherit this legacy. In the entire Extreme Purple Realm, only Xi Youqin could cultivate it, and she was the only one who had successfully cultivated it!
Since Xi Youqin had entered the Dreamy Battlefield, she had never used these three extremely powerful sword arrays!
Previously, she had used the Myriad Purple and Red Sword Array to fight against the enemy …
Xi Youqin did not like to compete with others. Since she was young, she had been quite resistant to weapons. Although she was born in a martial arts family, she was interested in needlework, needlework, and cooking instead.
Just now, she was very ignorant and very passive.
However, after being forced to retreat by You Di, she understood that if she lost, she would be eliminated as well. Not only would her master be disappointed, but she would also not be able to accompany Luo Zheng here …
In fact, in this battle, even if she lost once, she would not be eliminated. It was just that Xi Youqin did not know that.
When the purple river spread out, the eyes of the many Heavenly Legends on the chess pieces suddenly lit up …
"That's more like it …" One of the sky-pride sitting on the chess piece commented as he looked at the purple river.
Previously, Xi Youqin had been running away without any intention to fight. These three hundred people were all people with great backgrounds. With the strength that Xi Youqin had displayed, she was not worthy to stand with them!
However, they did not know that Xi Youqin did not want to stand with them at all.
When the Small Purple River Sword Formation was activated, the seven flying swords entered the purple river.
As Xi Youqin made a mysterious hand gesture, the rolling purple river came pouring down from the sky. The purple river flowed straight down and formed a magnificent waterfall. However, the waterfall was not formed by water, but by purple sword shadows!
Under Xi Youqin's guidance, the purple swords appeared in front of her. With a flick of her fingers, all the purple swords went straight for Youdi!
Eudy had intended to end the battle as soon as possible.
Challenging such a weak human girl gave him almost no sense of accomplishment at all.
However, in the face of the sudden appearance of the purple river, his eyes widened as a sense of danger filled his heart!
Under such extreme circumstances, he did not hesitate at all. With a light wave of the black spear in his hand, the two totems behind him suddenly flew out and hovered slowly in the air!
"In the name of my ancestors, bless me!"
Suddenly, countless light spots floated out of the two totems in the air!
These light spots were the souls of the countless ancestors of the Shaye Clan. These souls were the accumulation of the Shaye Clan for generations.
When the light spots floated out, Eudy's figure also glowed with a sharp light. With the black spear in hand and the blessing of the ancestors, Eudy's aura underwent a subtle change!
At this moment, Eudy was no longer Eudy. He was the amalgamation of the countless ancestors of the Shaye Clan!
At this moment, Eudy's physical body and strength had been strengthened to the extreme. He could even fight against a Demon Race martial artist!
"It's just a sword formation. I can break it!"
Eudy took a step forward and did not dodge. Instead, he charged toward the Little Purple River Sword Formation!
The river formed by the purple swords created wave after wave that surged toward Eudy.
Eudy was like a rock in the river, but he was not afraid at all. Although the purple swords were sharp, they instantly shattered when they collided with him …
With this charge, Eudy once again advanced toward Xi Youqin!
"Eudy's ancestor power can already sense the Heavenly Soul ancestor!"
"Before he entered the Illusory Battlefield, it was difficult for him to do so. I didn't expect that the training in the Illusory Battlefield would allow him to advance further!"
"That human girl's sword formation is indeed profound, but it's still far from our Eudy's. Haha …"
However …
A strange expression appeared on Xi Youqin's face.
"Master told me before that the Little Purple River Sword Formation cannot be broken with brute force …"
"The Little Purple River Sword Formation is not a transformational sword formation. Ordinary sword formations usually have 27 transformations, 81 transformations, and even more than 1,000 transformations. However, the Little Purple River Sword Formation only has three transformations. It's precisely because it lacks transformations that it has no loopholes."
"This guy is charging at me just like that …"
Xi Youqin pursed her lips. With a thought, she suddenly waved her finger!
Change the formation …
The Little Purple River Sword Formation had three transformations.
The first was the "Formation Charge," which used the purple swords to charge forward.
This was the first move of the sword formation. Apart from the Little Purple River Sword Formation's power, the Formation Charge was nothing special.
However, when the second transformation began, its murderous intent immediately became apparent!
"Spiral Trap!"
Buzz buzz buzz …
The purple river that had been surging forward suddenly changed direction!
The purple river formed a whirlpool that kept spinning around Eudy!
When Xi Youqin changed the formation, Eudy instantly had a bad feeling!
With the support of his ancestor's power, his state of mind had undergone a drastic change. At this moment, he should have been invincible. However, the purple river had turned into a whirlpool and trapped him firmly within.
"I don't believe that I can't break this sword formation!"
Eudy roared again. The two totems floating in the air spun faster and faster, and the light on his body grew brighter and brighter!
However, after being trapped in the purple river, Eudy realized that the sword formation could be both hard and soft. He couldn't break free at all and was easily trapped by Xi Youqin.
Seeing this, many prodigies looked at Xi Youqin and her sword formation with a faint trace of fear in their eyes.
In their eyes, Eudy from the Shaye Clan was a prodigy, but he definitely wasn't a very powerful one …
However, Xi Youqin had easily trapped him within her sword formation. The power of the sword formation itself was not to be underestimated.
Xi Youqin stood outside the sword formation and said softly to Eudy, "You … can admit defeat now."
It wasn't difficult for Xi Youqin to defeat this person in front of her, but she didn't look happy at all. It was meaningless to her.
"I haven't lost!"
However, this was an unacceptable result for Eudy.
Due to their worship of their ancestors, the warriors of the Shaye Clan valued their honor very much!
After all, in their hearts, their ancestors would always be by their side …
Xi Youqin blinked and formed another mysterious sword technique with her fingers. She said helplessly, "Then let's change the formation again …"
When the Little Purple River Sword Formation changed for the third time, a piercing murderous intent erupted from the purple river!
The seven flying swords suddenly appeared and transformed into a giant sword shadow that slashed toward Eudy's head …

Chapter 1318
Xi Youqin did not hesitate anymore.
Xi Youqin was very familiar with the Little Purple River Sword Formation.
After the third change, she used seven flying swords to attack together. It was called the Seven Stars Sinking.
Four years ago, her master had told her that the power of the Seven Stars Sinking was enough to fight against the prodigies of the major clans.
"Master, what's a prodigy?"
"It's a martial artist from one of the major clans."
"Is he more powerful than Luo Zheng?"
"…"
Her master rolled his eyes and did not say anything.
According to the Demi-Immortal Tribe's estimation of Luo Zheng's talent, he was qualified to be a prodigy. However, his actual strength could only be determined through a battle.
Four years ago, Xi Youqin's Little Purple River Sword Formation was enough to fight against a prodigy. Now, she had even mastered the God-Slaying Sword Formation.
It was a pity that Xi Youqin was muddle-headed when it came to martial arts.
She did not even realize her own potential and strength.
However, Xi Youqin understood now.
She could either choose to avoid the battle or end it quickly.
Now, Xi Youqin felt that she could not avoid the battle. She could only choose to kill her opponent as quickly as possible.
Poor You Di from the Shaye Clan. He did not even have time to unleash his full strength before he was trapped and killed by Xi Youqin's Little Purple River Sword Formation.
In fact, there was a huge gap between You Di and Xi Youqin's true strength.
Even if You Di used his most powerful technique, Ancestor's Sacrifice, he could only create a small obstacle for Xi Youqin.
The first battle between Xi Youqin and You Di was the opening battle. It was also the most eye-catching battle.
It was a pity that this battle was far from being exciting.
After all, Xi Youqin's clumsy way of avoiding the battle had surprised many people. Then, she suddenly became powerful and easily killed the prodigy from the Shaye Clan.
Even Luo Zheng couldn't understand what was going on. More people were confused.
Perhaps only Wuding Old Man could understand Xi Youqin's strange behavior and guess what Xi Youqin was thinking.
Of course, to the Shaye Tribe, this was like a bucket of cold water being poured over them from head to toe. How could they have known that the delicate human girl, who had been dodging everywhere at the beginning, would defeat Youdi so easily?
Under the giant totem, the countless clansmen were silent. Even the light of the ancestors on the giant totem had dimmed a little. In theory, the souls of the ancestors had turned into some kind of will, silently watching their descendants. They could also feel that the countless clansmen were in low spirits …
Soon, a white chess piece appeared in the sky, and Youdi, who was originally dead, appeared on the chess piece again.
Previously, the mysterious woman had announced the rules. At this stage, the opponents would not be directly eliminated. Instead, cultivators would be randomly selected to fight. You Di had a chance.
Xi Youqin did the same. Her figure disappeared from the black chess piece and returned to another black chess piece. She slowly controlled the black chess piece and moved it closer to Luo Zheng. Two chess pieces, one black and one white, landed on the chessboard …
"What a powerful technique! My Youqin already has such strength! " Luo Zheng praised when Xi Youqin came closer. With Luo Zheng's current vision, he could naturally see the sharpness of the Little Purple River Sword Array. He was even a little confused. How could such an inheritance exist in the small Extreme Purple Realm?
Xi Youqin was slightly taken aback when she heard Luo Zheng's words. However, a sweet smile appeared on her face.
Luo Zheng rarely acknowledged her in the past. She was happy enough to receive Luo Zheng's praise. She raised her nose slightly and showed a hint of pride on her face. "This is not my true strength!"
"Oh?" Luo Zheng was a little surprised. "Youqin's true strength is?"
She smiled coquettishly and said, "You'll find out soon enough!"
After the first two chess pieces were placed, the mysterious woman said indifferently, "The winner of the first round is Xi Youqin from the Extreme Purple Realm …"
As soon as she finished speaking, a jade-like hand reached out from the sky and grabbed a white chess piece and placed it on the chessboard. On the white chess piece was a demon prodigy. At the same time, a black chess piece was placed. It was a prodigy from the Human Alliance!
When Luo Zheng was at the Misty Cloud Palace, he had not seen this prodigy. This prodigy was the successor of Celestial Venerate Huan Hai. He did not cultivate in the Thirteen Palaces.
The second battle was far more exciting than the first battle.
Both prodigies unleashed their full strength. They fought back and forth, from a corner of the chessboard to the sky. With a single punch, cracks appeared on the chessboard of heaven and earth. However, those cracks repaired themselves in the blink of an eye …
In the end, the victor was the demon prodigy.
After these three hundred cultivators fought against each other, there were bound to be winners and losers between the races!
The cultivators from the three great factions of the human race were very calm. This was not enough to determine the final result.
However, the demon prodigy laughed maniacally after defeating the Human Alliance prodigy. "Mere humans call themselves the masters of the universe. They're nothing more than a collection of ants. There's nothing to fear!"
It was a publicly acknowledged fact that the human race was flourishing.
Whether it was the demon race or the demon race, no single race could stand up to the human race.
However, the three great factions of the human race were at odds with each other. If the three factions could set aside their prejudices and unite, the other races in the universe would have no room to grow at all.
No one knew what the demon prodigy was thinking by taunting them in such a manner.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Lie Qianhan, and the others looked indifferent. In their eyes, this heaven's pride of the Demon Clan was just a clown. However, they were not the only ones who saw this scene. Among the three Holy Lands of the Human Clan, there were nearly thirty to forty thousand tenth-grade Holy Lands, and all of them had Illusory Spaces. When the warriors of the Human Clan saw this scene, they were extremely indignant. At this moment, countless warriors were angrily demanding them to defeat the heaven's pride of the Demon Clan. However, their passionate voices could not reach the Illusory Battlefield.
However, in the third round, the demon prodigy was chosen again. He had actually fought twice in a row. No one knew what the mysterious woman's rules were. Coincidentally, the demon prodigy's opponent was also a human prodigy from the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm!
In the end, the human prodigy from the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm was defeated by the demon prodigy …
After defeating the human prodigy twice in a row, the demon prodigy became even more arrogant. He taunted the human race again before returning to the chessboard.
At this moment, some of the human prodigies showed anger on their faces. They swore in their hearts that they would trample the demon prodigy under their feet.
Next was the fourth round, the fifth round …
In this round after round, the black and white chess pieces were placed down one by one, and the number of chess pieces left on the chessboard increased.
However, it was still not Luo Zheng's turn!
When it was the 22nd round, the huge jade-like hand picked the demon prodigy again and knocked him onto the chessboard.
This time, it was a coincidence that the hand picked a human prodigy again!
And the person who fought was Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
He was the legendary number one cultivator in the Divine Ocean Realm of the universe. He was the main character in this round of the Battle of the Worlds. He was the Daoist of the Xuanyuan Clan!
When Xuanyuan Chenfeng appeared, all the human prodigies in the Illusory Battlefield began to boil with excitement.
At the same time, the atmosphere on the demon race's side began to turn solemn.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's reputation was not limited to the human race.
As a Daoist, he could cultivate the Divine Path and had the possibility of becoming a True God!
There were only a few Daoists in the entire universe, and Xuanyuan Chenfeng was known as the most powerful one among them. One could imagine how much attention his appearance would attract!
After the black and white chess pieces were placed, Xuanyuan Chenfeng left the chessboard.
No emotion could be seen on his face. He just slowly walked towards the other side of the chessboard, neither too fast nor too slow.
As for the demon prodigy who kept provoking Xuanyuan Chenfeng, he seemed to understand how powerful Xuanyuan Chenfeng was. At this moment, his face was full of caution. As a prodigy of the demon race, perhaps he did not intend to defeat Xuanyuan Chenfeng, but at least he could not lose too badly.
However, the demon prodigy did not expect that when he fought with Xuanyuan Chenfeng, there was no question of whether he would lose badly or not.

Chapter 1319
Facing the demon warrior, Xuanyuan Chenfeng only casually slapped out an ordinary handprint.
As soon as the handprint was slapped out, he turned around and left, not giving it a second look.
There were countless types of handprints in the universe, such as the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region's Demon Subduing Seal, Inaction Seal, Vajra Seal, and so on.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's handprint was the most ordinary. It was condensed from the True Essence in his body, and any warrior could condense it with True Essence.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was Xuanyuan Chenfeng after all!
Just this casual palm print contained the aura of the Great Dao!
When the demon warrior saw the handprint coming down on his head, he tried his best to dodge, but when he wanted to take a step forward, he found that he seemed to have fallen into a trance, unable to move or resist!
This was the power of Dao!
The 3,000 Divine Daos were the 3,000 Great Daos!
Every Dao formed its own system, and they were all different.
The Divine Dao that Xuanyuan Chenfeng cultivated had always been a secret in the universe.
This was because the Xuanyuan clan was very unique. This clan was the leader of the four great clans of the Gods No Thought. The other three clans, such as the Ji Clan or the Ji Clan, often had their Dao Children cut off from generation to generation.
In other words, the other three great clans often only had geniuses and no Daoists.

It was still only one move …
Strictly speaking, it was only two moves!
Because Luo Zheng's punch was only half a move. He had changed his move at the last moment, changing his fist into a spear. It could be considered as using half a move to deceive, and then using the spear to kill!
But at this moment, no one thought that the problem was just one move or half a move. In their opinion, Xuanyuan Chenfeng relied on a big handprint, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao relied on a sword, and Luo Zheng relied on a spear. That was enough.
The problem was that Luo Zheng had the Holy Spear in his hand.
How could the Holy Spear of Massacre of the Yao Night Clan be in Luo Zheng's hands?
"That's the Holy Spear of Massacre!"
"How could it be in Luo Zheng's hands?"
"The Holy Spear of Massacre is the most precious treasure of the Yao Night Clan, and the King of the Yao Night Clan is by Luo Zheng's side. This spear … could it be that the King of Massacre of the Yao Night Clan passed it on to Luo Zheng?"
"Hmph, I heard that the King of Massacre is only a soul now. Where did he get the Holy Spear of Massacre? This spear is clearly kept in the Yao Night Clan … "
…
In the Yao Night Clan …
Yao coldly stared at the Holy Spear of Massacre in Luo Zheng's hand. A ruthless expression appeared on her face, and her gaze fell on Ming Dao, who was standing to the side.
"My, my King …" Ming Dao fell to his knees with a plop.
Yao and Ming Dao were from the same faction, and it was because of their bloodline inheritance that she had supported Ming Dao's father, the Realm Lord of Broken Shadow, to become the Heavenly Sovereign of Broken Shadow …
"This Holy Spear of Massacre was accidentally stolen by Luo Zheng!" Ming Dao hurriedly explained.
Yao shook her head and sighed. "It's just a projection of the Holy Spear. It's not that important, but you failed to live up to expectations …"
The Holy Spear of Massacre that she had lost in the Illusion Battlefield wasn't the real Holy Spear of Massacre, so Yao didn't feel any heartache. However, Ming Dao's Illusion Points had all been stolen, and he hadn't been able to enter the top 100. Yao was very disappointed.
In the Illusion Battlefield, the two factions of the Yao Night Clan that supported her, the King of Punishment, were almost completely annihilated. On the other hand, the Daoist nurtured by the King of Life's faction had a smooth journey. He should be able to obtain a decent ranking.

Chapter 1323
After Luo Zheng took out the Holy Spear of Slaughter, many cultivators looked solemn.
The Holy Spear of Slaughter was known as the most powerful weapon in the universe. It was one of the top three supreme artifacts on the Universe's Myriad Spirit Stele. It was the symbol of the Fiend Night Clan …
However, wasn't Luo Zheng rumored to be a sword cultivator?
Why had he suddenly switched to using a spear?
Luo Zheng hadn't stayed in the Upper Realm for long, but many people had already dug up news of his time in the Human Arrival Alliance. News of Luo Zheng's performance in the Torch Flame Inheritance had also gradually spread.
This could also be news deliberately released by the Human Arrival Alliance to prove to the other races in the universe that Luo Zheng was from the Human Arrival Alliance.
However, Luo Zheng didn't care about what others thought of him.
When he stabbed out with his spear, the Holy Spear of Slaughter had already returned to his Sumeru Ring …
Many people in the universe had heard of Luo Zheng's name, but this was the first time they had seen Luo Zheng in action.
Luo Zheng had also killed the Demon Clan's prodigy, Nu Feng, with a single strike. However, the Holy Spear of Slaughter was very famous in the universe. Many people would attribute the credit to the Holy Spear of Slaughter rather than his true strength.
When Luo Zheng returned to the sky and stepped on a black chess piece, a white chess piece slowly floated towards Luo Zheng.
A woman with long and slender legs stood on the white chess piece. She gave off a quiet and elegant aura. Compared to the other women from the Demon Night Clan, she was not as lively and lively. However, she was much more dignified and calm.
When she floated next to Luo Zheng, she stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Friend, please return the Holy Spear of Slaughter to the Fiend Night Clan. It's a sacred artifact of the Fiend Night Clan."
When Luo Zheng heard this, a faint smile appeared on his face …
He knew that the Holy Spear of Slaughter was very important to the Fiend Night Clan. The problem was that this was the Illusory Battlefield.
The Holy Spear of Slaughter was not the real thing. It was just a projection. However, Luo Zheng could use the mark to bring the Holy Spear of Slaughter out of the Dream Battlefield. He wanted to give Xun a surprise when he returned.
However, the woman in front of him actually wanted him to return it? How could he choose to return it?
Not only did he not return the spear, he instead looked to the woman from the Demon Night Clan with a smile on his face. "Just because this spear is your clan's sacred item, I should return it to you?"
Not only did the woman not refute Luo Zheng's words, she even nodded her head heavily. "This spear is a sacred object of our Demon Night Clan. It is only natural for us to return it."
"Are you an idiot? The Holy Slaughter Spear is only a projection from the Illusory Battlefield. It's not the real Holy Slaughter Spear. "Xi Youqin was unable to bear it any longer. She stepped on a chess piece and blocked the woman from the Demon Night Clan.
The woman from the Demon Night Clan smiled faintly. "Of course I know that. However, I also know that you've been to the Karma Hall. The Holy Slaughter Spear has already been marked and can be brought out … Do you really intend to give it to Xun?"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly when he heard the woman from the Demon Night Clan's words.
The woman's reaction was extremely strange.
Everyone from the Demon Night Clan would refer to the three kings as' Our Kings'.
Even the Demon Night Clan's members who were hostile to Xun did not dare to be negligent in addressing them as' Our Kings'.
However, this woman from the Demon Night Clan was calling Xun by her real name?
Who exactly was she?
When he thought of this, Luo Zheng smiled coldly. "I took the Holy Slaughter Spear from your Demon Night Clan's cultivators. Naturally, I intend to give it to Xun and return it to you. Who do you think you are?"
The woman from the Demon Night Clan was not angered when she heard Luo Zheng's words. Instead, she stared at Luo Zheng with a faint smile on her face. "There will be a time when we fight … At that time, you will definitely return it to me."
Then, she stepped on the white chess piece and fluttered to the other side.
Hearing the woman's last words, Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression. Who was this woman? What did she mean by that?
"That woman is Qing Yu, a Daoist from the Demon Night Clan," one of the human prodigies explained to Luo Zheng.
Qing Yu?
Luo Zheng had never heard of such a person in the Demon Night Clan before.
However, he had never been to the Demon Night Clan, so he did not know much about the geniuses in the clan.
At this moment, the human cultivator continued, "Actually, this Daoist Qing Yu is very strange. I've never heard of such a Daoist in the Demon Night Clan with a fully bloomed Mahayana Lotus Flower. She seemed to have appeared out of thin air."
"Appeared out of thin air?" Luo Zheng frowned slightly as he stared in the direction Qing Yu had left.
"Hehe, the cultivation of a Daoist requires an enormous amount of resources and resources. I haven't even heard of such a Daoist in the Nightfiend Clan for many years. Now that he has suddenly appeared, he must have appeared out of nowhere …" The human prodigy was quite knowledgeable, and he helped Luo Zheng analyze the situation.
'No matter who she is? Does she intend to force me to return the Sacred Spear of Slaughter to her?
That's not realistic either … '
'A battle on this chessboard won't endanger my life. How is she going to force me to return the Sacred Spear of Slaughter?' Luo Zheng could not figure it out.
As he thought about it, his gaze fell on Qing Yu as he quietly pondered. This matter was probably related to Xun, so it was best to wait and see.
That huge jade-like hand was still grabbing the chess pieces one by one and pressing them onto the Heaven and Earth Chessboard. Round after round of competition was still unfolding.
Three hundred sky-prides might not be a lot, but it was still very time-consuming to go through all the battles.
Ever since Luo Zheng had stepped onto the chessboard, he had not had a chance to participate in any of the subsequent battles. On the other hand, Xi Youqin had fought twice, and each time, she had won with the Little Purple River Sword Array. Against prodigies, Xi Youqin could handle them with the Little Purple River Sword Array.
At the thirty-fourth round, there was finally a warrior among the three hundred warriors who had been eliminated.
It turned out that the black chess piece on the chessboard had sealed the white chess piece's eyes and directly 'devoured' it. Under such circumstances, the white chess piece would be eliminated. At the same time, the prodigy on the white chess piece could only fight with all his might. If he won, he would still be able to stay on the chessboard. If he lost, he would be eliminated.
In the end, the prodigy on the white chess piece was defeated and became the first prodigy to be eliminated.
Even though Nu Feng was constantly defeated, because the chess pieces under his feet were all living chess pieces, he was not eliminated.
In other words, when a chess piece was placed on the chessboard, if he was still a living chess piece, winning or losing didn't matter, and he would not be eliminated.
But if it was his own chess piece … it was a dead chess piece, or even a dead chess piece. Under such circumstances, he could only fight with all his might. He could only win and not lose. If he lost, he would be eliminated …

Chapter 1324
The elimination of the first cultivator didn't attract much attention in the eyes of ordinary cultivators.
It was very normal for cultivators to be eliminated from a competition.
Many cultivators even began to question the rules of this chessboard. Using this method to eliminate cultivators seemed too baseless and it was impossible to truly and effectively determine the strongest cultivator.
For example, Nu Feng of the Demon Race had been defeated three times in a row and still hadn't been eliminated. This was inevitably unfair.
However, they didn't realize that this Great Era War was just a preview of the universe's future. There was no such thing as rules.
On the contrary, the many Heavenly Venerates in the universe attached great importance to this.
The first prodigy to be eliminated …
His name was Guan Quan.
He was a member of the Celestial Tang Clan.
The Celestial Tang Clan was a small and unknown clan. It couldn't even be considered a second-rate clan, yet it had given birth to a prodigy. This clan only controlled three Great Realms, and these three Great Realms were extremely barren and marginal Great Realms. Therefore, no great faction was willing to invade such a clan.
"Could it be … that the crisis of this Great Era will be born from such a small and unknown clan?"
In the Human Alliance, Heavenly Venerate Cloudfox muttered to himself as he looked at the black and white chess pieces.
"A small Celestial Tang Clan can be destroyed by a few tenth-rank Sacred Lands of the Human Alliance if they join forces. Do they have the ability to trigger the crisis?" Cloudfall asked in confusion.
Heavenly Venerate Cloudfox smiled faintly. "A small clan like this is nothing to be afraid of. However … they may not have the ability to trigger the crisis, but the crisis may erupt from their side …"
What form would this crisis take? So far, no one knew …
It could be a silent, world-ending plague that even Celestial Venerables could not resist. It could also be a self-destruction caused by the fall of all the stars in the heavens. It could also be a war disaster started by some ambitious person from a great power.
However, many Heavenly Venerates began to speculate and their gazes fell on the Celestial Tang Clan.
If they calculated according to this clan, they might be able to deduce a result. Based on this result, they would be able to set up defenses in advance and try to overcome this crisis.
The chess pieces on the Chessboard of Heaven and Earth were still falling down one by one, little by little, forming a piece.
The white chess pieces, named Fixed Number, were very stable and occupied a large territory. The black chess pieces, named Variable Number, were cut into pieces by the white chess pieces …
As everyone became more familiar with the rules, the way they fought also changed.
These warriors had a rough estimate of their own strength.
If he encountered a strong opponent and was unwilling to reveal his trump card, he would take the initiative to admit defeat. In any case, he wouldn't be eliminated if he admitted defeat in such a situation. If he revealed his trump card too early, he would probably be targeted by others in the life and death battle …
It was also because of this that the round after round of battles sped up.
After both sides were taken off the chessboard, they quickly tried to guess each other's strength. They even tested each other before deciding which side would surrender …
This method clearly did not go against the original intention of the chessboard. However, the mysterious girl did not stop them. She continued to focus on using the three hundred prodigies as chess pieces, constantly placing them on the chessboard.
Every time a white piece or a black piece was about to be "hugged and eaten", it was the crucial moment of the killing move. Naturally, the warriors on both sides would not be perfunctory anymore.
If he lost now, he would be eliminated immediately!
Naturally, he would give it his all …
As more and more pieces were placed on the chessboard, the speed at which the martial artists were eliminated also increased.
This was because when they were "hugged and eaten", one of them would definitely be eliminated …
During this period, Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin were each taken off the chessboard twice.
Luo Zheng still relied on that Saint Spear. His opponent took the initiative to admit defeat once, and the other time, after exchanging blows, his opponent no longer put up any unnecessary resistance and admitted defeat again …
As for Xi Youqin, no one could break the Minor Purple River Sword Array. Every time she used the Minor Purple River Sword Array, her opponent would also take the initiative to admit defeat.
Just like this, close to three hundred chess pieces were placed on the chessboard. These three hundred prodigies had also exchanged blows three hundred times.
Of course, most of the exchanges were perfunctory and not very spectacular … Moreover, there were also some warriors who had never even entered the chessboard before.
At this moment, the white chess pieces on the chessboard had formed a large dragon, and the situation was extremely stable.
However, although this large dragon was stable, it wasn't completely dead yet. At the top of the large dragon, there was a battle with the black chess piece. The crucial point of this battle was probably only around the third to fourth moves before a chance was born.
The martial artists who were well-versed in the chessboard could already see that under this situation, it would probably be a battle between two Dao Disciples! After all, this battle directly determined the overall situation of the black and white chess pieces. If the black chess piece lost, the originally unfavorable situation would be lost. If the white chess piece lost, the large dragon would be killed by the black chess piece, and the situation would be reversed.
The next few moves were the most crucial ones!
"Pa …"
The black and white chess pieces landed on the top of the large dragon.
Two martial artists entered the arena, fought, and admitted defeat …
"Pa pa …"
Another two chess pieces landed, and another two martial artists entered the arena. One was Lie Qianhan of Deities No Thoughts, and the other was a prodigy from a small clan. That prodigy directly admitted defeat and admitted that he wasn't a match for Lie Qianhan …
At this moment, the last move of the battle of the calamity appeared.
Luo Zheng looked at the situation on the chessboard, and his eyes flashed slightly. With the appearance of the battle of the calamity, these two moves were especially crucial. Moreover, this was also a life and death game. The loser would be directly eliminated if the large dragon was killed.
In that case, who would be the cultivator that would be chosen to enter?
The mysterious girl's chess skills weren't very good, and most of the martial artists could see this. At this moment, everyone held their breath because they all knew that whoever was chosen to enter the Chessboard of Heaven and Earth would most likely be eliminated. In this case, their opponents would definitely not be weak.
"Huff …"
A jade-like hand directly grabbed a purple-haired girl.
Luo Xi Xuan of the Demi-Immortal Clan!
Luo Zheng wasn't familiar with this girl. He had never been to the Demi-Immortal Clan. Although the old patriarch had sent someone to ask him to go, he couldn't go. However, Luo Xi Xuan had fought twice before, and each time, her opponent directly admitted defeat.
Hearing the introduction of others, Luo Zheng learned that this person was a Dao Child of the Demi-Immortal Clan!
There were many martial artists from the Demi-Immortal Clan who had entered the three hundred, about nine of them. Moreover, the Demi-Immortal Clan had two Dao Children.
One was Luo Xi Xuan, and the other was Jiang Zhengyi.
The Dao Children entered the arena.
Many of the martial artists' faces showed a touch of seriousness.
This was a life and death battle. No one was willing to be eliminated. If they admitted defeat, they would be directly eliminated. Who would be willing to collide with these Dao Children so early?
Therefore, most of the martial artists were praying in their hearts that they wouldn't enter the arena.
After the Dao Child of the Demi-Immortal Clan was knocked into the chessboard.
That jade-like hand directly moved towards Luo Zheng.
Looking at the direction of the jade-like hand, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. "It's me?"
However, in the end, that finger directly passed over Luo Zheng's head and grabbed a martial artist not far behind Luo Zheng. Hua Tianming!
"So it's Hua Tianming," Xi Youqin said softly.
"Brother Tianming …" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
This wasn't good news …
He knew that Hua Tianming's strength had soared by leaps and bounds and had undergone a qualitative change.
However, although that Dao Child was also a Divine Sea Realm martial artist, his Mahayana Lotus had been completely opened. If he didn't fall, his chances of becoming a True God were quite high. More importantly, he had begun to comprehend the Divine Dao. His understanding and application of the Dao were far beyond that of ordinary people!
Facing Luo Xi Xuan, Hua Tianming's odds were against him.
Seeing the expression on Luo Zheng's face, before Hua Tianming's black piece was placed, he smiled slightly at Luo Zheng. He was still calm and confident, and his expression was full of heroic spirit.
At this very moment …
In a tenth-rank Holy Land in Gods No Thought, there was a burst of uproar.
Hua Tianming had ascended to a tenth-rank Holy Land in Gods No Thought, and he had become the treasure of this Holy Land.
Their Holy Lord had never dreamed that a martial artist from his own Holy Land would be able to stand in the top 300 …
The entire Gods No Thought was the world of the four Great Clans. These tenth-rank Holy Lands were merely vassals of the four Great Clans. Those who qualified to enter the top 300 in Gods No Thought were the Dao Children of the Xuanyuan Family, Ji Family, Ji Family, and Lie Family. As for the other tenth-rank Holy Lands, they were basically annihilated.

Chapter 1325
When Hua Tianming had first made it into the top ten thousand, the sacred leaders had been overjoyed.
As one of the great factions of the human race, Gods No Thought was essentially one of the four great divine nations. The four great clans were the emperors of Gods No Thought.
The subordinate sacred lands could only be considered the subjects of the four great clans. In terms of structure, they were completely different from the Human Alliance.
Because of this, all the resources would be given to the four great clans, and all the resources would be distributed by the four great clans.
The fact that a tenth rank sacred land could nurture a genius in the top ten thousand was completely beyond the sacred leader's expectations. In the future, he would definitely be able to use this to obtain more resources and rewards from the four great clans.
However, they never would've thought that Hua Tianming wouldn't stop at the top ten thousand.
Instead, he'd entered the second stage.
He'd entered the final stage of the game and become a variable on the chessboard of heaven and earth.
The cultivators of the tenth rank sacred lands were overjoyed by this scene.
It even attracted the attention of the sacred leaders of the other two tenth rank sacred lands.
Unfortunately, Hua Tianming hadn't placed the black piece at his feet from beginning to end. This made them look forward to Hua Tianming's next move even more.
However, they hadn't expected Hua Tianming to be eliminated as soon as he made his move.
Even more unexpected was that Hua Tianming's opponent would be Luo Xi Xuan!
Luo Xi Xuan of the demi-immortal race, the darling of the demi-immortal race, the daughter of lightning …
All of the prodigies present had their own legends.
Luo Xi Xuan's background was particularly bizarre.
There was a realm in the universe called the Dark Thunder Realm. This realm was filled with the violent power of lightning.
No one had set foot in it for countless years.
It wasn't that they were afraid of the power of thunder and lightning. In fact, the more peculiar this kind of ominous land was, the greater the possibility of obtaining an opportunity. Often, there would be countless cultivators who would rush forward one after another in order to obtain an opportunity to become stronger.
The Divine Fire Realm was one such example. The Divine Fire Realm wasn't suitable for living beings at all, but many cultivators would risk their lives to enter it in order to obtain the treasures of heaven and earth there.
Risk and reward were often directly proportional.
However, the Black Thunder Realm was a little special. There was a Black Thunder Sacred Beast sealed in this realm.
It was said that this Profound Thunderbeast was sealed eighty million years ago by six Celestial Sovereigns working together to seal it. Moreover, they relied on one of the Celestial Sovereigns' heavenly wills, the "Demon Sealing Mandate" to seal this Profound Thunderbeast.
It was precisely because of the existence of this seal that no one could enter the Dark Thunder Realm.
But twenty-two years ago, the seal broke.
It must be known that the Demon Sealing Mandate of Heaven was set up using the Demon Sealing Mandate of Heaven. In theory, this seal represented the strongest seal in the universe. After all, it was set up using the laws of the Heavenly Dao.
The breaking of the seal meant that the Profound Thunder Sacred Beast had broken free from the seal, and it also meant that it had obtained power that surpassed the Heavenly Dao!
At that time, this matter shook the entire universe.
It even made quite a few people cry out in alarm. The crisis of the great era battle had already arrived ahead of time!
The danger was naturally the Black Thunder Sacred Beast.
This crisis quickly attracted the attention of many races in the universe.
Whether it was the human race, the demi-immortal race, or the demon race's Celestial Venerables, they all headed to the Dark Thunder Realm to investigate the situation.
However, after repeated investigations, he discovered something unexpected.
The seal of the Dark Thunder Realm had indeed been broken.
But the Black Thunder Sacred Beast was still sealed inside. Only a very small part of it had been broken, so small that it was negligible …
However, as they continued to investigate, they discovered something shocking.
This seal had been broken by a three-year-old girl!
This little girl had actually broken the seal on her own and walked out of that damned place filled with all kinds of Thunder Laws!
At that time, Luo Xi Xuan was only three years old.
After realizing that this girl was extraordinary, many races began to fight over her.
In the end, she fell into the hands of the demi-immortal race, and they named her Luo Xi Xuan.
Her mysterious origin caused a heated discussion in the universe, but in the end, it gradually died down.
Because after she entered the demi-immortal race, there was no more news about her …
But a year before the Illusory Battlefield opened, the Heavenly Dao Clan announced that they already had two Dao Children. One was Jiang Zhengyi, and the other was Luo Xi Xuan.
Although no one had seen this girl's strength, no one doubted that she was a Dao Child.
In this world, there were always some people who were born extraordinary.
Luo Xi Xuan was one of those extraordinary people.
"Ai, why is Hua Tianming's first opponent from the demi-immortal race, and it's Luo Xi Xuan!"
"This Luo Xi Xuan is only rumored to be strong, but she may not be able to defeat Hua Tianming …"
"Haha, the demi-immortal race … any random genius is enough to become a top disciple of our Holy Land, and Luo Xi Xuan is a Dao Child of the demi-immortal race, the most powerful character of the younger generation, how could she be weak?"
In the tenth rank Holy Land, many cultivators couldn't help but sigh.
Now that Hua Tianming had advanced into the top three hundred, wouldn't it be good if he could enter the top ten and obtain a Divine Spark Plate?
But now, it was a pity, why was he so unlucky?
Luo Xi Xuan gently jumped down from the chess piece and looked at the chessboard under her feet. Her light purple eyes swept across the other side, landing on the handsome face of Hua Tianming, and then a faint smile appeared on her face.
"Demi-Immortal, Luo Xi Xuan," she said faintly.
At this moment, Luo Xi Xuan didn't look special at all, as if she was just an ordinary person, but she also gave off the feeling of a girl next door.
At the same time, Hua Tianming also stepped forward, slowly walking down from the black chess piece, and then took out the long sword on his back.
The long sword was wrapped in a layer of linen, but Hua Tianming placed the sword on the ground, and then slowly unrolled the linen that had been rolled into a ball …
Some cultivators would take advantage of this opportunity to attack.
But Luo Xi Xuan wouldn't.
Hua Tianming also knew that she wouldn't.
After completely unraveling the linen, a sword appeared. It looked like a very ordinary sword, and the blade was still hidden in the scabbard, as if he hadn't pulled it out.
Holding the sword hilt, Hua Tianming stood up, "Cloud Hall, Hua Tianming."
"Hm?" Luo Xi Xuan tilted her head, a puzzled look in her eyes.
What the hell was the Cloud Hall?
At the same time, the Ninth Palace Master in the Jade Pure Sacred Ground stared at Ning Yudie. Hearing it from Hua Tianming's mouth once again confirmed Ning Yudie's previous words.
Ning Yudie just smiled slightly, but her expression was as if it was only natural.
Ever since Hua Tianming showed his talent in the battle with the Tian Xie Sect, he had been put in an important position by the Cloud Hall.
Ning Yudie naturally understood this person's character. As a swordsman, he was a very principled person. He had never left the Cloud Hall, and in theory, he had always been a member of the Cloud Hall …
At that time, Ning Yudie had also secretly observed Hua Tianming. If there were no major accidents, when Hua Tianming's cultivation was enough, he would be promoted to the position of Palace Master.
However, Hua Tianming's ambitions were not in the Cloud Hall, nor in the Central Domain. He had greater ambitions.
Ever since he left the Central Domain, he had been missing. Ning Yudie only found out when she came here that Hua Tianming had ascended and had such strength.
As for the tenth-grade Sacred Ground in [Gods Without Thought], when they heard Hua Tianming's answer, they were naturally in an uproar, and many were dissatisfied.
However, it was useless to be dissatisfied. They were not of much help to Hua Tianming. At most, they could only be regarded as borrowing their ascension channel.
Hua Tianming ignored Luo Xi Xuan's doubts …
He placed one hand on the scabbard and slowly pushed it open, revealing the bright blade within.
"This sword was actually not prepared for you …" Hua Tianming pulled the scabbard out halfway and said with a smile, "So, if you have the strength, you can force me to draw this sword.
After Hua Tianming said this, everyone was stunned.
Did he mean that he did not intend to draw his sword in front of Luo Xi Xuan?
Moreover, not only did he say this, but he also did it. The sword that he had pulled out halfway was slowly retracted!
He was facing a Dao Child! And a Dao Child of the Demi-Immortal Clan!
"This guy, where did he get the confidence to say this to Luo Xi Xuan?"
"What Cloud Hall? What kind of broken faction is it? There doesn't seem to be such a faction in the universe! "
"This guy is doomed. He's probably going to be tortured to death by Luo Xi Xuan …"

Chapter 1326
In the eyes of many cultivators, Hua Tianming's actions were tantamount to extreme arrogance.
However, Luo Zheng just smiled and nodded slightly. He was very clear about Hua Tianming's nature.
This guy didn't know what arrogance was, but since he said so, he must be confident.
Although a Dao Child was an extremely powerful existence, no one stipulated that a Dao Child was necessarily invincible.
Becoming a Dao Child only meant mastering the Divine Path and comprehending the mysterious Dao Essence contained in the Great Dao. Although the Dao Essence was incomparably powerful and surpassed the limits of the Heavenly Dao, it didn't mean there was no way to break it …
Back when Hua Tianming was just a Xiantian Secret Realm cultivator, he was also like this. Who would have thought that ten years later, he would still be like this.
From this, it could be seen that many people's personalities were innate. Even if they experienced tempering, it only made them sharper, but in the end, their nature could not be changed.
However, when Luo Xi Xuan heard Hua Tianming's words, she showed a faint smile.
She wasn't angry …
On the contrary, when she looked at Hua Tianming, there was a strange look in her eyes.
There were many geniuses in the Demi-Immortal Clan. It could be said that any cultivator who could join the Demi-Immortal Clan would be a Core Disciple in any Tenth Grade Holy Land, but in the Demi-Immortal Clan, they could only be the tail of a phoenix.
Luo Xi Xuan had never fought, or rather, she had never fought with a cultivator of the same generation …
However, all the cultivators of the same generation in the Demi-Immortal Clan would always show a cautious look when they saw her, and there was even a hint of fear hidden within that caution.
She didn't like this kind of look.
After growing up, Luo Xi Xuan also understood her background. Although from the outside, she was very strong and had amazing perception, and even obtained the title of Golden Rakshasa in the Demi-Immortal Clan.
However, she knew that when others looked at her, they treated her like a monster.
That caution and politeness were mixed with a sense of alienation, making it difficult for her to get close to others.
Therefore, the only person who accompanied her as she grew up was her Honoured Master, followed by endless loneliness. In just twenty years, this little girl had lived alone as a companion, but in her heart, she longed to interact with others normally.
She even often wished that she was an ordinary person. How good would it be?
Like an ordinary girl, she grew up in a boudoir and was doted on by her parents. After she got married, she married a loyal and diligent husband and lived a peaceful life that was not long.
Unfortunately, her fate was destined to be extraordinary from the very beginning.
After being sent to the Demi-Immortals, no one spoke to her. Instead, she was placed in the Thunderclap Realm, where she was nourished by the Extreme Yin Sage Lightning. Slowly, she grew up surrounded by boundless lightning ….
If other experts had heard Hua Tianming's words, they would have snorted disdainfully. Since he didn't intend to draw his sword, then he shouldn't blame others for eliminating him.
However, after Luo Xi Xuan thought about it for a while, her brush-like eyelashes blinked and a very strong interest appeared on her face as she smiled and said, "Is that so? You want me to force you to draw your sword? That sounds interesting! "
When Hua Tianming heard this reply, he was slightly taken aback. No matter what, this was not interesting at all.
However, he still gently waved the scabbard and said indifferently, "You can make your move now …"
"Yes!"
Luo Xi Xuan nodded vigorously. When she took a step forward, a flash of lightning instantly flashed in her eyes.
"Pa!"
Accompanied by the crisp sound of lightning, her long purple hair suddenly straightened and fluttered wildly like the hair of a Demonic Beast ….
At the same time, the lightning around Luo Xi Xuan surged like liquid.
It was actually golden lightning!
As the lightning slowly intertwined, it formed a page, a page as tall as a person.
And it was within this thunder page that revealed an extremely powerful Dao. This was the Dao of the Profound Thunder Divine Way!
Other cultivators might have been intimidated and suppressed by the Dao of the Profound Thunder Divine Way ….
However, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly narrowed and his eyelids began to twitch violently.
"This is … the Sanskrit page!"
When Qinglong had just awakened and communicated with him, he had asserted that his Weapon Body was probably the only one in the universe.
However, Qinglong had not explained the reason behind it ….
After communicating with him, Luo Zheng gradually came to understand that his Weapon Body might not have been due to the Divine Flame Cauldron.
The Divine Flame Cauldron was admittedly a strange cauldron passed down from the God Realm, and the Divine Flame it contained was of great help to Luo Zheng. However, what truly made him was that Sanskrit page.
So far, every time Luo Zheng was tempered by the True Fire, the Sanskrit text would transform into countless golden vortexes that helped Luo Zheng absorb the power of the True Fire and temper his body ….
It was also through this absorption and tempering that Luo Zheng was able to reach this step today.
But where did that golden Sanskrit page come from? Why did it have such an effect? Luo Zheng had no way of knowing, nor could he get an answer from Qinglong.
The golden page condensed from golden lightning in front of him was very similar to the page Luo Zheng had absorbed in the past.
"This woman also has such a page, but her page is formed from lightning." Luo Zheng's gaze was fixed on the Sanskrit page, his eyes constantly flickering. If there was a chance, he would naturally think of a way to inquire about it.
However, the battle between her and Hua Tianming was an elimination battle, and only one person could survive. If she lost, Luo Zheng was afraid that he would have to go to the Heavenly Immortal Clan.
"What a strong Profound Thunder Dao Connotation …."
Even Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who had always had an indifferent expression, had a grave expression on his face when he felt the Dao Essence.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng was a great master, and the Divine Dao he cultivated was very special. Even when facing the other Dao Children, it was difficult for him to be so dignified.
"One move, humph …. That human warrior should not be able to withstand one move in front of Luo Xi Xuan."
"That guy is probably the same as Nu Feng of the Demon Clan. No matter whether he can win or not, he should brag first …."
"Unfortunately, he will lose in the end. Bragging like this will not do him any good."
Many warriors had such an evaluation ….
Luo Xi Xuan slowly stepped forward, and the Sanskrit page was like a door, standing in front of her. When she stepped past, all the Sanskrit characters were like a curtain, sticking to her body. The difference was that the curtain would slide to the sides, but the Sanskrit characters stuck to the surface of Luo Xi Xuan's body one by one.
"Zi zi …."
Luo Zheng's Sanskrit characters turned into a golden vortex.
Luo Xi Xuan's Sanskrit characters released fine lightning, like small spiders, lying on her face, neck, arms, and body ….
Then Luo Xi Xuan smiled gently at Hua Tianming, raised her arm slightly, and stretched out her index finger towards Hua Tianming, and then pointed it to the ground.
"Pa …."
"Ten Directions, Thunder Annihilation …."
When she finished speaking, a smile appeared on her face again, and at the same time, she said, "If you don't draw your sword, I'm afraid you won't have the chance to do so …."
Then, on the chessboard of heaven and earth, countless purple lightning balls appeared one after another.
These lightning balls seemed to have the power to destroy the world, and the moment they appeared, they quickly gathered towards Hua Tianming!
Facing these rolling lightning balls, Hua Tianming's eyes showed no fear. He just gently stabbed the long sword in the scabbard towards the ground.
"Your Thunder Element Law … is very strong, and the Dao Connotation it contains has already surpassed the scope of this chessboard of heaven and earth." As he spoke, a faint smile appeared on his face, "I know that the essential difference between a Dao Child and a Heaven's Pride is Dao Connotation. If I take away your Dao Connotation, your strength will be no different from an ordinary Heaven's Pride …."
"For others, Dao Connotation is almost impossible to block," Hua Tianming shook his head slightly, "but for me, it has almost no effect."
After speaking, Hua Tianming leaned on the long sword, and a little bit of Sword Intent slowly floated out from the scabbard.
Hua Tianming did not activate Sword Intent at all, but Sword Intent overflowed by itself ….
When Luo Zheng saw the Sword Intent, he finally understood where Hua Tianming's confidence came from.
He also did not expect that Hua Tianming would actually comprehend Ultimate Sword Intent in these few years.
Ultimate Sword Intent had become a kind of limit in the chessboard of heaven and earth. Advancing just a little bit was surpassing this chessboard of heaven.
Therefore, to a certain extent, Hua Tianming could also be regarded as a Dao Child ….
