Chapter 1327
After Luo Zheng took out the Sacred Massacre Spear, many warriors had solemn expressions on their faces.
The Sacred Massacre Spear was said to be the most powerful weapon in the universe, the top three supreme divine weapons on the Universe Myriad Spirit Stele, and the symbol of the Demon Night Clan …
But wasn't Luo Zheng a sword cultivator?
Why did he suddenly change to a spear?
Luo Zheng hadn't stayed in the Upper Domain for long, but many people had already dug up the things that happened in the Alliance. Luo Zheng's performance in the Torch Inheritance had also gradually spread.
This could also be deliberately released by the Alliance to prove to the other races in the universe that Luo Zheng came from another race in the Alliance.
But Luo Zheng didn't care what others thought.
When he stabbed with the spear, the Sacred Massacre Spear returned to his Sumeru Ring …
Many living beings in the universe had long heard of Luo Zheng's name, but this was the first time they saw Luo Zheng fight with their own eyes.
Although Luo Zheng had also killed Nu Feng, the prodigy of the Demon Race with a single spear strike, the Sacred Massacre Spear was very famous in the universe. Many people attributed the credit to the Sacred Massacre Spear instead of his true strength.
When Luo Zheng returned to mid-air again and stepped on a black chess piece, a white chess piece floated slowly towards Luo Zheng not far away.
On this white chess piece stood a Demon Night Clan lady with slender legs. This lady had a quiet and elegant aura. Compared to the other Demon Night Clan ladies, she was less lively and lively, but she was more dignified and quiet.
When she floated beside Luo Zheng, she stared at him and said, "Friend, please return the Sacred Massacre Spear to the Demon Night Clan. This is a sacred item of the Demon Night Clan."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed an expression that seemed like a smile yet not a smile …
He knew that the Sacred Massacre Spear was very important to the Demon Night Clan, but this was the Dream Battlefield.
This Sacred Massacre Spear wasn't the real thing. It was just an illusory projection. However, Luo Zheng could very likely bring the Sacred Massacre Spear out of the Dream Battlefield through that mark. He wanted to give Xun a surprise when he returned.
However, this lady before him actually wanted him to return it? How could he choose to return it?
Not only did he not return it, but he also stared at the lady with a smile. "Just because this spear is a sacred item of your clan, I should return it to you?"
Not only did the lady not refute Luo Zheng's words, but she also nodded her head. "This spear is a sacred item of the Demon Night Clan. I should naturally return it."
"Are you an idiot? This Sacred Massacre Spear is just a projection from the Dream Battlefield. It isn't the real Sacred Massacre Spear. "Xi Youqin couldn't help but step on a chess piece to block the lady from the Demon Night Clan.
The lady from the Demon Night Clan smiled faintly. "Of course I know that. However, I also know that you've been to the Karma Hall. This Sacred Massacre Spear has already been smeared with a mark, so you can bring it out. Do you really intend to give it to Xun?"
Luo Zheng slightly furrowed his brows when he heard the lady from the Demon Night Clan.
The lady's reaction was very strange …
All the members of the Demon Night Clan would address the three Kings as' Our King '.
Even the members of the Demon Night Clan, who were hostile to Xun, didn't dare to be disrespectful.
However, this lady from the Demon Night Clan called Xun by her real name?
Who was she?
Luo Zheng sneered coldly. "I took this Sacred Massacre Spear from the hands of a warrior from your Demon Night Clan. Of course, I intend to give it to Xun and return it to you. Who do you think you are?"
The lady from the Demon Night Clan wasn't angry when she heard Luo Zheng's words. Instead, she stared at Luo Zheng with a faint smile. "There will be a time when we fight. At that time, you will return it to me."
Then, she stepped on the white chess piece and fluttered to the other side.
Luo Zheng was puzzled when he heard that there was a hidden meaning in the lady's words. Who was this lady? What did she mean?
"This lady is the Dao Child of the Demon Night Clan — Qing Yu." A Human Clan's genius explained to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had never heard of such a person in the Demon Night Clan.
However, he had never been to the Demon Night Clan before, so he didn't know much about the geniuses in the clan.
At that moment, the Human Clan's warrior continued, "Actually, this Dao Child called Qing Yu is very strange. I've never heard of her before. The Demon Night Clan has a Dao Child with a Mahayana Lotus in full bloom. This lady seemed to have appeared out of nowhere."
"Appeared out of nowhere?" Luo Zheng stared in the direction that Qing Yu had left, and his brows furrowed slightly.
"Hehe, the nurturing of a Dao Child requires a huge amount of money and resources. I've never heard of such a Dao Child in the Demon Night Clan a few years ago. Now that she has suddenly appeared, she must have appeared out of nowhere …" The Human Clan's genius was quite knowledgeable, so he helped Luo Zheng analyze the situation.
No matter who she is? Is she planning to force me to return the Sacred Massacre Spear to her in battle?
That's not realistic …
The battle on this Heaven and Earth Chessboard won't be life-threatening. What does she want to use to force me to return the Sacred Massacre Spear? Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out.
As he thought about it, his gaze fell on Qing Yu, and his heart sank. He was afraid that this matter had something to do with Xun, so it was better to wait and see.
That huge jade-like hand was still grabbing the chess pieces one by one and pressing them onto the chessboard of heaven and earth. Round after round of battles continued to unfold without stopping.
Even though 300 geniuses wasn't a large number, it was still extremely time-consuming for them to battle one after another.
Ever since Luo Zheng had entered the Heaven and Earth Chessboard once, he didn't have a chance to participate in the following battles … On the contrary, Xi Youqin had participated twice, and each time, she had won with the Little Purple River Sword Formation. Facing the geniuses, Xi Youqin's Little Purple River Sword Formation was enough to deal with them.
In the 34th round, there were finally some martial artists who were eliminated.
It turned out that the black chess piece on the chessboard had sealed the living eyes of the white chess piece, and it had directly "eaten" the white chess piece. Under such circumstances, the white chess piece would be eliminated. At the same time, the warrior on the white chess piece could only give it his all. If he won, he would still be able to stay on the Heaven and Earth Chessboard. If he lost, he would be eliminated.
In the end, the genius on the white chess piece was defeated, and he became the first warrior to be eliminated.
Even though Wind Fury was continuously defeated, the chess pieces under his feet were all living, so he wasn't eliminated.
In other words, when a chess piece was placed on the chessboard, if it was a living chess piece, the outcome of the battle wouldn't matter, and he wouldn't be eliminated.
However, if it was his own chess piece … it was a dead chess piece, or even a bunch of dead chess pieces. Under such circumstances, he could only give it his all. He could only win, and he couldn't lose. If he lost, he would definitely be eliminated …

Chapter 1328
To begin cultivating the Law of Thunder, one had to place their True Essence on a weapon and shoot it out to subdue the enemy.
After reaching a certain level of mastery, one could surround their body with thunder and lightning, or even use thunder and lightning to temper their body, which was not difficult for a cultivator.
However, the power of thunder and lightning could only circulate in the internal organs without any major problems, but it could not be drawn into the brain …
The soul was fragile and could be damaged by thunder and lightning, so all cultivators who cultivated the Law of Thunder would isolate the power of thunder and lightning beneath their necks.
There were many cultivators who could control thunder and lightning in the universe, but under normal circumstances, no one would draw thunder and lightning into their mind, even if their soul was extremely strong and could withstand the torment of thunder and lightning …
But at this moment, Luo Xi Xuan had completely transformed herself into thunder …
This was different from Thunder Evasion, which used thunder and lightning as a movement technique, but only concealed oneself in thunder and lightning.
Thunder Evasion could greatly increase a cultivator's flying speed, but it did not mean that a cultivator could move forward with thunder and lightning; after all, the speed of thunder and lightning shuttled through space was immeasurable.
The Profound Thunder Realm was similar to the Divine Fire Realm in that there were many Law of Thunder creatures.
But so far, no one had ever heard of anyone capturing a Law of Thunder creature …
The reason was that these Law of Thunder creatures were almost all thunder and lightning incarnations. They came and went at the speed of a flash, so it was impossible to catch up to them.
In the records of the universe, there was an eminent monk from the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm who owned a living being of the Law of Thunder. It was said that the living being of the Law of Thunder was not captured, but was converted by the eminent monk using the Great Buddhist Dharma and became the eminent monk's mount.
"If this Luo Xi Xuan completely transformed herself into thunder, wouldn't she also have the speed of a flash?"
"If she really had the speed of thunder, it would be impossible to react …"
"I underestimated this Luo Xi Xuan."
In an instant …
Nine hundred lives and deaths in an instant.
This was the limit of speed in the universe!
Whether it was light or thunder, they were all bound to this limit!
After completely transforming into thunder and lightning, Luo Xi Xuan floated up slightly, and even though her appearance was outlined by thunder and lightning, she still looked innocent and charming.
"I'm coming …"
Just as she spoke her last word, everyone saw a flash of thunder and lightning.
A long and narrow purple thunder turned into a long dragon that stretched across the chessboard, piercing through Hua Tianming's body and reaching the other end of the Heaven and Earth Chessboard.
This speed had exceeded the limit of vision …
"Pa!"
The speed of sound was, after all, several times slower than that of thunder and lightning. Only now did everyone hear the crisp sound of thunder.
When that voice rang in everyone's ears, Hua Tianming's hair was instantly disheveled, and a wound appeared on the side of his neck. The area around the wound was charred, and fresh blood flowed out!
"It's impossible to dodge at the speed of an instant …"
"Hua Tianming probably didn't even have time to react."
"To be honest, I can resist the power of thunder, but although Luo Xi Xuan has transformed into thunder, her attacks are not as simple as ordinary thunder. In this state, she should be invincible, right?"
Some of the sky-prides and even a few Daoists looked extremely solemn when they saw this scene.
Everyone was thinking about how to break Luo Xi Xuan's move.
However, among all the martial arts in the world, only speed was unbreakable.
This speed was already so fast that it could be broken in an instant. What else could he do?
Ji Luo Xue stared indifferently at Luo Xi Xuan on the Chessboard of Heaven and Earth. A small ring appeared in her hand. That ring was formed from vital essence and contained the power of the Five Elements …
"After transforming into thunder, Luo Xi Xuan … is indeed extremely fast. However, if I use the power of the Five Elements to bind her, I have a way to restrain her, but it won't be easy …"
Although Ji Luo Xue was a sky-pride, she was one of the most powerful sky-prides. Moreover, unlike the other sky-prides, she had cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao from the beginning. Although she did not possess the essence of the Great Dao, she had comprehended most of the Five Elements Divine Dao. Her situation was very similar to Luo Zheng's; she was only one step away from stepping into the Divine Dao.
"I held back just now." After transforming into thunder, Luo Xi Xuan's voice was very ethereal, as if it was transmitted from a distant space. "Next time, if you still refuse to draw your sword, then you really won't have a chance … You won't be able to catch up to my speed. Even if you draw your sword now, it'll be useless."
The faint smile on Hua Tianming's face disappeared and was replaced by an extremely serious expression.
"Instant speed … is indeed very fast. This is the Purple Infernal Thunder Form of the Profound Thunder Divine Dao," Hua Tianming said seriously.
Upon hearing Hua Tianming's words, a trace of doubt flashed across Luo Xi Xuan's face. "You … know?"
Since ancient times, there had been very few people who had stepped into the Divine Dao, and each of the 3,000 Great Daos was different.
Only Luo Xi Xuan understood the various profound meanings of the Profound Thunder Divine Dao. She could only rely on herself to explore. Even the old patriarch of the Demi-Immortal Tribe could not give her any advice …
As for the Purple Infernal Thunder Form, it was only comprehended by Luo Xi Xuan herself. No one else knew about it.
However, how did Hua Tianming know the name of this profound meaning?
Hua Tianming did not answer Luo Xi Xuan's question. Instead, he continued, "However, you've only just become a Dao disciple. The Purple Infernal Thunder Form is ultimately unable to break through the speed of the universe. I … may not be unable to catch up!"
As soon as these words left his mouth, everyone thought that Hua Tianming had gone crazy again.
Others might not be able to recognize the Purple Infernal Thunder Form, but Luo Xi Xuan's speed after transforming into lightning was obvious to all. She had abandoned her physical body and transformed into pure lightning. Instant speed … yet someone said that he may not be unable to catch up!
Unless Hua Tianming could transform into lightning himself, it was impossible!
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with curiosity. What method would Hua Tianming use to break Luo Xi Xuan's move?
"However, you do have the strength to make me draw my sword," Hua Tianming said. He held the scabbard with his left hand and placed it on his waist. His right hand held the hilt of the sword in reverse. His body was slightly bent, but he still did not draw his sword.
Seeing Hua Tianming's actions, a happy smile bloomed on Luo Xi Xuan's face. "Very good … then, it's over!"
If Hua Tianming did not draw his sword no matter what, even if she defeated him, Luo Xi Xuan's thoughts would not be clear.
At this moment, seeing that Hua Tianming was willing to face her and seemed to be going all out, she was relieved. She wanted to make Hua Tianming accept his defeat wholeheartedly.
A bolt of lightning suddenly flashed across the Heaven and Earth Chessboard.
However, she did not immediately rush towards Hua Tianming. Instead, she circled around the Heaven and Earth Chessboard and flickered. Every time she flickered, a purple lightning mark would be left in everyone's eyes. Then, everyone heard the rumbling of thunder.
She dashed left and right, but was unwilling to get close to Hua Tianming …
"She can finish off Hua Tianming in one move. What is Luo Xi Xuan doing?"
"Why do I feel like she's afraid?"
"How is that possible … Hua Tianming's posture is just the most ordinary Sword Drawing Technique. This Sword Drawing Technique is only an ordinary martial skill. Its speed is indeed fast, but how fast can it be?"
Everyone was extremely puzzled …
In fact, Luo Xi Xuan, who had turned into a bolt of lightning, was also very depressed at this moment.
She was very strange …
With her speed, Hua Tianming's eyes would not be able to follow her.
However, from the beginning, she had a strange feeling that Hua Tianming was staring at her. No matter how fast she was, this guy's gaze would always be able to catch up to her!
Therefore, she did not immediately rush up to end the battle because she instinctively felt a trace of danger.
However, as she circled around the chessboard, even if she circled behind Hua Tianming and Hua Tianming's back was completely facing her, she still felt that gaze.
This gaze made Luo Xi Xuan feel a little uneasy, and also made her feel very uncomfortable …
"Pa …"
"Pa …"
"Pa …"
Under the constant shuttling, Luo Xi Xuan also felt a trace of irritation.
The Purple Abyss Thunder Form also consumed a lot of True Essence. After turning her body into lightning, she needed a constant supply of True Essence. The True Essence sea in her body was like a receding tide, rapidly decreasing.
She had to make a decision immediately …
Thinking of this, she suddenly moved three times on the Heaven and Earth Chessboard. The three purple lightning bolts formed a triangle and surrounded Hua Tianming.
However, she did not rush in from the front. Instead, she circled behind Hua Tianming, shuttled through the air, and pounced towards the back of his head …
"Pa!"
Killing Hua Tianming would only take an instant.
However, at this moment, Hua Tianming pulled out the sword in his hand …
A faint light emitted from under his feet. He stepped on the light, and a snow-white sword light flashed in his hand.
"Absolute Step …"

Chapter 1329
When Hua Tianming had first made it into the top 10,000, the sacred land's Sacred Lord had been overjoyed.
As a major faction of the Human Race, Deities Without Thought was essentially four massive divine nations, and the four great clans were the emperors of Deities Without Thought.
The subordinate sacred lands could only be considered subjects of the four great clans. In terms of structure, they were completely different from the Human Dao Alliance.
Because of this, all the resources would be tilted to the four great clans, and all the resources would be distributed by the four great clans.
The fact that a Rank 10 sacred land had nurtured a genius in the top 10,000 had completely exceeded the Sacred Lord's expectations. In the future, he would definitely be able to use this to obtain even more resources and rewards from the four great clans.
However, they had never imagined that Hua Tianming wouldn't stop at the top 10,000 …
He had entered the second stage …
He had entered the final stage of the game and had become a variable on the chessboard of this world.
This scene made many of the martialists in the Rank 10 sacred lands burst with joy.
Even the Holy Lords of the other two Rank 10 Holy Lands were attracted over.
Unfortunately, the black chess piece under Hua Tianming's feet hadn't landed since the beginning. This made them look forward to Hua Tianming's move even more.
However, they had never imagined that Hua Tianming's first move would be an elimination game.
They had never imagined that Hua Tianming's opponent would be Luo Xixuan!
Luo Xixuan of the Sky Immortal Clan, the favored child of the Sky Immortal Clan, the daughter of lightning …
The sky-prides here all had a legendary story to tell.
Luo Xixuan's background was especially bizarre.
There was a realm in the universe called the Mysterious Thunder Realm. This realm was filled with the violent power of lightning.
For countless years, no one had ever set foot in it.
It wasn't that they were afraid of the power of lightning. In fact, the more strange and dangerous a place was, the higher the possibility of obtaining opportunities. Often, countless martialists would seek opportunities to become stronger in order to find them.
For example, the Divine Flame Realm was like this. The Divine Flame Realm wasn't suitable for living beings, but many martialists risked their lives to enter it in order to obtain the treasures in the Divine Flame Realm.
The risks and rewards were often directly proportional.
However, the Mysterious Thunder Realm was special. A Mysterious Thunder Sacred Beast was sealed in this realm.
It was said that this Mysterious Thunder Sacred Beast was jointly sealed by six Empyreans 80 million years ago. Moreover, they relied on an Empyrean's Mandate of Heaven, the 'Demon Sealing Mandate', to seal this Mysterious Thunder Sacred Beast.
It was precisely because of the existence of this seal that no one could escape into the Mysterious Thunder Realm.
But twenty-two years ago, the seal shattered.
One had to know that the Demon Sealing Mandate was set up using the Demon Sealing Mandate. In theory, this seal represented the strongest seal in the universe. After all, it was set up by borrowing the rules of the Heavenly Dao.
The shattering of the seal meant that the Mysterious Thunder Sacred Beast had broken free of the seal. It also meant that it had obtained a power that surpassed the Heavenly Dao!
At that time, this matter shook the entire universe.
It even caused many people to cry out in alarm. The crisis of the Great Era War had arrived in advance!
The crisis was naturally this Mysterious Thunder Sacred Beast.
This crisis quickly attracted the attention of many races in the universe.
Whether it was the human race, the Empyrean race, or the demon race's Empyreans, they all immediately went to the Mysterious Thunder Realm to investigate the situation.
But after investigating again and again, they discovered an accident.
The seal of the Mysterious Thunder Realm had indeed been broken.
But the Mysterious Thunder Sacred Beast was still sealed inside. Only an extremely small part of the seal had been broken, so small that it could be ignored …
But as they continued to investigate, they discovered an astonishing matter.
This seal had been broken by a three-year-old little girl!
This little girl had actually broken the seal on her own and walked out of that damned place filled with all sorts of lightning laws!
At that time, Luo Xixuan had only been three years old.
After realizing that this woman was not ordinary, many races began to fight over her.
In the end, she had fallen into the hands of the Empyrean race, and the girl had been named Luo Xixuan.
Her background was mysterious, and it caused a heated discussion in the universe. However, it gradually died down in the end.
Because after she had entered the Empyrean race, there had been no news of her …
But a year before the Dreamrealm Battlefield had opened, the Heavenly Law Clan had announced that there were two Dao Children in the clan. One was Jiang Zhengyi, and the other was Luo Xixuan.
Although no one had seen how strong this girl was, no one suspected that she was a Dao Child.
In this world, there were always some people born extraordinary.
Luo Xixuan was one of those people born extraordinary.
"Sigh, why did Hua Tianming's first opponent turn out to be someone from the Empyrean race? And it's even Luo Xixuan!"
"This Luo Xixuan is only as powerful as the rumors say, but she might not be able to defeat Hua Tianming …"
"Haha, the Empyrean race … Any random genius can become a supporting disciple of our Holy Land. Luo Xixuan is a Dao Child of the Empyrean race, the most powerful person in the younger generation. How could she be weak?"
Many cultivators in the Rank 10 Holy Land couldn't help but sigh.
Now that Hua Tianming had entered the top three hundred, wouldn't it be great if he could enter the top ten and obtain a Godhead Disk?
But now, it was a pity. Why was he so unlucky?
Luo Xixuan lightly jumped down from the chess piece and looked at the chessboard beneath her feet. Her faint purple eyes swept across the opposite side and landed on the heroic face of Hua Tianming. A faint smile appeared on her face.
"Empyrean Realm, Luo Xixuan," she said indifferently.
At this moment, Luo Xixuan didn't look special at all. It was as if she was just an ordinary person, and she gave off the feeling of a girl next door.
At the same time, Hua Tianming also stepped forward and slowly walked down from the black chess piece. Then, he took out the longsword on his back.
The longsword was wrapped in a layer of sackcloth. Hua Tianming placed the sword on the ground and slowly untied the sackcloth.
Some cultivators would take advantage of this opportunity to attack.
But Luo Xixuan wouldn't.
Hua Tianming also knew that she wouldn't.
After completely untying the sackcloth, a sword appeared. It looked like a very ordinary sword. The sword was still hidden in the scabbard, and he didn't pull it out.
Holding the sword hilt, Hua Tianming stood up. "Cloud Hall, Hua Tianming."
"Hmm?" Luo Xixuan tilted her head, her eyes revealing a puzzled look.
What the hell was Cloud Hall?
At the same time, the Nine Palace Masters in the Jade Clear Holy Land stared at Ning Yudie. Hearing it from Hua Tianming's mouth, it once again verified Ning Yudie's previous words.
Ning Yudie just smiled slightly, but her expression was as if it was natural.
Ever since Hua Tianming showed his brilliance in the battle against the Heavenly Evil Sect, he was put in an important position by the Cloud Hall.
Ning Yudie naturally understood this person's character. As a swordsman, he was an extremely principled person. He had never left the Cloud Hall, so in theory, he had always been a member of the Cloud Hall …
Back then, Ning Yudie had also secretly observed Hua Tianming. If there were no major accidents, when Hua Tianming's cultivation was sufficient, he could be promoted to the position of Hall Master.
However, Hua Tianming's ambition was not in the Cloud Hall, nor in the Central Region. He had a greater ambition.
Ever since he left the Central Region, he had been missing. Only when Ning Yudie came here did she know that Hua Tianming had actually ascended and possessed such strength.
As for the tenth grade holy land of Gods Without Thought, when they heard Hua Tianming's answer, there was an uproar and many people were dissatisfied.
However, dissatisfaction was useless. They were not of much help to Hua Tianming. At most, they could only be considered to have borrowed their ascension passage.

But not all of the peach blossoms avoided her.
At this moment, there was a small piece of peach blossom on top of her hair.
Before she was drawn into the Emerald Dream Realm, the peach blossoms had never appeared. But now, there were peach blossoms on her hair!
"This is that piece of land …"
Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a strange look.
If he cut off this peach blossom, he would be able to break the Emerald Dream Realm …
This was only Luo Zheng's guess. Perhaps the Dream Divine Path wasn't so easy to break.
But in this situation, Luo Zheng couldn't really do nothing. If so, he might as well choose to admit defeat.
"Bang!"
After each heavy blow, Luo Zheng was also looking for an opportunity.
This was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity for Luo Zheng. If he failed, with her speed in the Emerald Dream Realm, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to catch up.
Ji Liansheng extended her jade-like hand toward Luo Zheng. With a light hook, an irresistible force pulled Luo Zheng and sent him flying back toward her …
"Phew …"
"Alright, it's time to end this." Ji Liansheng's smile was charming and enchanting.
Then, Luo Zheng was restrained by the invisible force and floated in front of Ji Liansheng.
"Your physical body is indeed very special." Ji Liansheng smiled. "I'm afraid its strength is equivalent to a first-grade divine artifact … If Sister Luo Xue met you, she might have a headache. But you met me first …"
Hearing Ji Liansheng mention her, Ji Luo Xue blinked slightly. She looked down at the immobile Luo Zheng and felt slightly regretful.
Not to mention that she was a loyal fan of Luo Zheng's stories, she had lost to Luo Zheng once before. Naturally, she wanted to win it back from Luo Zheng herself. Otherwise, she wouldn't be able to get her way …
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng was going to lose to Ji Liansheng.
"Idiot …" Ji Luo Xue sighed softly. "Just cut off the peach blossoms behind her head …"
Ji Liansheng's Dream Divine Path was extremely powerful, but she had lost to Lie Qianhan and Xuanyuan Chenfeng in the Four Clans Tournament.
There was no other reason …
The Four Great Clans shared information with each other. They were also very clear about each other's characteristics.
Although Ji Liansheng's Dream Divine Path was powerful, her Dream Divine Path was like a special puzzle. If she couldn't figure it out, she was invincible. But once she figured it out, her Dream Divine Path's power would be greatly reduced.
Ji Luo Xue and the others understood this. Ji Liansheng wouldn't be able to get any benefits from them.
However, those who didn't understand Ji Liansheng had almost no chance of winning!
A faint blush appeared on Ji Liansheng's face. Then, she smiled sweetly and pinched lightly.
"Crack!"
Luo Zheng's right arm broke in an instant and left his body.
Fresh blood spurted out from the wound …
This Ji Lian was born outside of the dream world and was like a virgin flower that never left her boudoir. She would be extremely shy if she spoke a few words with a man, but at this moment, she was acting strangely cruel!
Seeing the fresh blood flowing out, Ji Liansheng's eyes lit up.
"This woman … is only so cruel in her dreams." Xuanyuan Chenfeng rolled his eyes …
Two years ago, the clan wanted to introduce Ji Liansheng to him.
This woman was compatible with Xuanyuan Chenfeng in terms of talent and appearance. It was just that Xuanyuan Chenfeng was antisocial and knew Ji Liansheng's nature, so he tried his best to reject her.
She looked delicate and shy, but her true nature was cruel and bloodthirsty.
However, the bloodthirsty in the Dream Realm and the bloodthirsty in the real world were two different things. She killed someone in the Dream Realm. When the Emerald Dream Realm dissipated, the person was still intact. It could be said that he was completely unharmed. Only his soul had been shattered …
Luo Zheng's arm was gently torn apart by her. The pain was indescribable.
His body had always been known for its strength. But being torn apart by this woman was probably the most serious injury he had ever suffered.
Although it was painful, Luo Zheng gritted his teeth. His gaze secretly swept past the peach blossom behind Ji Liansheng.
At this moment, he was only two steps away from Ji Liansheng. As long as Ji Liansheng didn't notice that he had noticed the peach blossom, he still had a chance …
Ji Liansheng raised her head and looked at Luo Zheng. Once again, she smiled charmingly, "Actually, you can admit defeat. I'm still very kind-hearted. I can't bear to see others bleed and cry …"
Luo Zheng endured the pain and forced a smile on his face, "As a martial artist, bleeding is unavoidable. Don't cry!"
"Is that so?" Ji Liansheng raised the corner of her mouth, "Then I really want to see you cry …"
After saying that, her finger once again made a slanting gesture …
"Cha …"
It was as if the sharpest sword had cut Luo Zheng's leg!
It forcibly separated Luo Zheng's right leg from his body …
"Luo Zheng!"
Although she knew that one wouldn't really die in this Dream Realm, Xi Youqin still couldn't bear to see this bloody scene. She angrily shouted from the chess piece.
The more the opponent wanted to torture him, the calmer Luo Zheng's expression became.
Since Ji Liansheng's hobby was to torture her opponents in dreams …
Then he had to give Ji Liansheng an impression and act out a play. He had to let her understand that he had no power to fight back and unyieldingly endure her means.
However, Luo Zheng's real purpose was the peach blossom on her hair bun.
"Again …"
Ji Liansheng's finger once again made a light stroke. Luo Zheng's second leg was also separated from Luo Zheng's body.
Seeing this scene, the people in Huanyu couldn't help but shake their heads …
Luo Zheng had a great reputation, but his strength was still inferior. If he encountered a real genius from a big clan, he would still be the target of torture. He had almost no power to fight back.
After all, since the beginning of this dream, Luo Zheng hadn't put up any effective resistance.

Chapter 1334

For Luo Zheng, there was only one chance …

Regardless of whether it was Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Jiang Zhengyi, or Ji Liansheng, they were all created by the large families with immeasurable resources, and they themselves had the destiny to fight for the world. In the upcoming crisis of the universe, they would play an indispensable role in it!
If he wanted to defeat them, he couldn't afford to make any mistakes!
He took out the sword, flipped it, and spat out the Sword Intent …
These few steps were done in one go!
In the Emerald Dream Realm, Ji Liansheng's speed and strength were both in a 'limitless' state.
To put it bluntly, as long as she didn't break this dreamland, she would be an invincible existence. Perhaps only World Overlords or even Empyreans would be able to forcefully break this dreamland apart!
But her speed was too fast, and she needed to think to keep up!
In layman's terms, he was able to "react" …
Luo Zheng's sword was extremely sudden. Before Ji Lian Sheng could react, the sword had already slashed out!
Swish!
The sword aura that flew out went straight for Ji Liansheng.
Luo Zheng didn't have the intention to kill Ji Liansheng. She was probably an immortal existence in this dreamland, and killing her was only the last choice.
He slashed the peach blossom.

The Dark Lightning Wind Divine Sword was sharp, and even though it cut the peach blossom, it only left a small imperceptible mark …

The purpose of the battle on the Heaven and Earth Chessboard was to determine the winner. Luo Zheng wasn't a bloodthirsty person.

Ji Liansheng fell to the ground. She was immediately eliminated.
Luo Zheng won by a narrow margin.

"This woman … is too sinister!"

"Brother Tianming is right." Luo Zheng nodded.
All the martial artists in the universe would display their strengths.
If they won with speed, they couldn't attack head-on. If they won with strength, they naturally wouldn't use unorthodox methods …
The battle in the universe continued. This round wasn't an elimination round, but the jade hand chose Xi Youqin and another prodigy from another race.
At this moment, in the depths of the sky, a nine-colored light began to slowly rotate.

Chapter 1335

Chapter 1336
From these 11 people, it could be seen that the race with the most potential in the universe was none other than Deities Thoughtless.

Chapter 1344

Chapter 1345

Metal melted to create water, water nourished to create wood, wood dried to create fire, fire burned wood to create earth, and earth and minerals created metal. It could be said that everything in the world was covered by the five elements, and there were only very few times when other Laws had to be utilized.
Thus, it was much easier to construct the world within one's body with the energy of the five elements than cultivators that cultivated the other Divine Daos.

Chapter 1347
The chess pieces on this Heaven and Earth Chessboard were placed by the Black and White Twins.
An ordinary chessboard had nineteen vertical and nineteen horizontal lines, which was three hundred and sixty-one points. There were one hundred and eighty black pieces and one hundred and eighty white pieces.
In theory, there were three hundred and sixty-one battles, which was enough to fill the entire chessboard.
But on this Heaven and Earth Chessboard, there were thirty-six vertical and thirty-six lines, which was one thousand two hundred and ninety-six points.
The previous three hundred martial artists had been eliminated, so there had been two hundred and ninety-nine elimination battles. Among them, the number of non-elimination battles was several times greater than the number of elimination battles! The two added up to a total of eight hundred and three battles …
But after these eight hundred and three battles, the situation on the Heaven and Earth Chessboard was still not clear!
In the previous Evolution Era, this situation had not occurred.
In the previous Evolution Era, the mysterious girl's chess game had already become a foregone conclusion. After just seven hundred and twenty-six battles, the white pieces had already gained an irreversible advantage.
The clearer the situation on the Heaven and Earth Chessboard was, the easier it was to calculate …
While the black and white pieces were still in a state of chaos, the mysterious girl had started a cycle.
In fact, the Heavenly Venerables who had been analyzing the situation on the Heaven and Earth Chessboard had been quite puzzled.
There were only five battles left between these ten people, and one of them had a bye. Then, the six of them would be able to play three more pieces.
Although there were many winning and losing moves on the Heaven and Earth Chessboard, the remaining moves weren't enough to fill it up …
When the rules were announced, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao and his sword shot into the sky and landed on a chess piece.

"I lost," the broken sword said to Dugu.
"I lost! Doesn't that mean I've been eliminated … "Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's face darkened.
"There's still a chance …"

This basin was 10 feet high and 10 feet wide. Normally, it was filled with the blood essence of all sorts of rare birds and beasts …
Generally speaking, the blood essence of these rare beasts was too violent for ordinary people to withstand. If they tried to forcefully absorb it, it would take a long time to dissolve. Or, it would contain a potent poison that couldn't be absorbed!
But this Hidden Phoenix Dragon Basin was able to neutralize all the blood essence and retain the characteristics of this blood essence!
Every year, the Lie Family would send people out to collect blood essence. The many Holy Lands under their banner would also keep some blood essence and hand it over to the Lie Family for the sake of this basin!
To be able to soak in this basin for two hours was a great honor within the Lie Family. The average person didn't have the qualifications to enter. For instance, some World Lords of great worlds might be granted this honor by the Lie Family.
Even the Lie Family Patriarch couldn't casually enter this Hidden Phoenix Dragon Basin to soak in.
In the entire Lie Family, the only ones who weren't restricted were the Lie Family Old Ancestor and Lie Qianhan …

Chapter 1351
Luo Zheng hadn't expected that he would encounter Qing Yu in his first battle.

She jumped up from the chess piece and landed firmly on the chessboard.

As she walked, she smiled gently at Luo Zheng.

The spirit embryo nurtured in the Pond of Faith of Life was indeed worthy of being smoked …

When he returned to the outside world, he could discuss with Xun. If Xun was willing to exchange the Sacred Slaughter Spear, then it wouldn't be too late.

The skin on Qing Yu's right hand was withered. It was full of wrinkles and cracks, and at the same time, it was emitting bursts of black Death Qi …
A pair of hands showed two completely different forms.

Tens of thousands of creatures cycled between life and death.
The Laws of Life didn't just contain vitality. Conversely, the Laws of Life contained numerous profundities related to death!

Unfortunately, this was the Illusory Battlefield. This method was useless.

"Cycle of life and death …"

Qing Yu was Ying, the King of Life!

On this side of the boundary was death, and on the other side was life …

Chapter 1353
There were far too many secrets hidden between life and death.
In the universe, there weren't many martial artists that specialized in the Laws of Life. This type of Law could bring someone back from the dead, but it didn't have a strong killing power.
Thus, Ying was the weakest of the three kings of the Fiend Night Clan.

In addition, they could use the power of life to nurture strange plants to fight.
However, Ying's Cycle of Life and Death was extremely powerful. Moreover, it was unique in the universe. It was a technique that Ying had comprehended endlessly from the Laws of Life.
Thus, when Soft Whisper used the Cycle of Life and Death, many Empyreans immediately confirmed her identity.
This was an ability that only the Laws of Life possessed.
This Cycle of Life and Death wasn't an offensive technique …
In that instant, Soft Whisper released a rich power of life with one hand, while her other hand released the power of life in the opposite direction. This was the rich deathly energy …
When these two powers simultaneously covered Soft Whisper, they dragged her into a state of life and death.
At the same time, Luo Zheng was also dragged into this state.
This was the 'state of life and death'!
When a living being was in this state of life and death, they would fall into a very unique state.
In theory, the living creatures were both dead and alive …
Taking a step forward meant life, taking a step back meant death …
When a living being fell into this state of life and death, they would fall into an extremely difficult state that would cause great damage to their soul.
This damage would also affect Soft Whisper. However, Soft Whisper was proficient in the Laws of Life and she had always been exploring the mysteries of life in this state of life and death. This damage wouldn't have much of an effect on her, but it would be extremely disadvantageous for her opponent.
This was like a martial artist that cultivated the Laws of Ice dragging their opponent into an extremely cold place. Both sides would face considerable pressure, but the martial artist that cultivated the Laws of Ice would naturally have it much easier.
The vigorous vitality and the rich deathly energy fused together and revolved around the chessboard of heaven and earth before finally turning into a gray fog.
As Luo Zheng stood in this fog, he could feel the quietness and tranquility within it.
From his body, a trace of laziness was born ….
To his surprise, he felt tired ….
The battle intent that had filled his heart also quietly dissipated.
This kind of negative emotion quietly attacked him, and then a feeling of impending death began to slowly spread!
Qingyu stood quietly in front of Luo Zheng. She was like a red spider lily covered in dust, bright and beautiful, but it was impossible to see her true appearance in the haze. There was a faint smile on her face, and a strange look in her eyes.

As she spoke, she walked towards Luo Zheng. This meant that Luo Zheng's mind was stable and his heart of martial arts was tenacious …
Many martial artists, after being drawn into a life and death situation, would be restrained by the fear of death!
The fear of death was inescapable for many martial artists.
After all, death meant returning to zero. It meant that everything he cared about and everything related to him would disappear!
The intensity of this fear varied from person to person.
Some people were able to see through life and death, so they would be less affected by death. Since they had nothing left to live for, they might as well die.
On the other hand, the stronger one's obsession with this world, the greater the impact would be!
… …
Luo Zheng's obsession was not weak.
Whether it was to find the Luo Yan outside of the Heavenly Dao, or to pursue greater power, or to protect his lover, Luo Zheng fought for these obsessions.
At the beginning, the fear was still manageable, and Luo Zheng's obsession could resist it.
However, as Luo Zheng spent more and more time in this life and death situation, the fear became stronger and stronger.
The lines and chess pieces on the chessboard of heaven and earth began to slowly disappear.
Under Luo Zheng's feet was an endless abyss, and he was standing on a thin line above this abyss.
The stronger the obsession, the more afraid of death.
"I can't die …"
A strange look appeared in Luo Zheng's eyes.
Qingyu stared at Luo Zheng with an indifferent look. In fact, the life and death situation was, frankly speaking, a test of obsession.
The stronger his obsession was, the more severe the backlash from his own obsession would be!
Imperceptibly, his state of mind would be completely shattered by his obsession.
On this thin line, Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and took a step toward Qingyu …
The moment he took a step, he dashed toward Qingyu …
The fear in his heart was very real. It was not an illusion, but a complete experience of life and death, which was forcibly imposed on his mind.
With Luo Zheng's current state of mind, he could not resist or escape. The only way was to kill the fear before it grew!
"Whoosh!"

But now, his heart was fiercely beating, because of himself!

Chapter 1354

The person Xun had chosen was indeed not bad.
As long as this person didn't die, he would be able to help Xun return to the throne of the King of Slaughter …
"Being defeated between life and death isn't a disgrace. On the contrary, it proves that your obsession is extremely strong. In this world, those with great obsession are usually the most successful people … So, please admit defeat," Qing Yu said indifferently.
In ancient times, there was a legend about the Heavenly Pool Mountain. It was said that one had to kneel for a thousand years before the treasure appeared.
Many people went to check it out. Most of them scoffed, not believing this legend.
However, hundreds of people stayed and knelt in front of the Heavenly Pool Mountain …
A year later, everyone chose to leave. In the end, only one person stayed.
Others mocked that person for being stupid. Rumors were just rumors. A thousand years was precious.
However, that person remained unmoved. He knelt for a thousand years.
A hundred years later, the Heavenly Pool Mountain cracked, and the treasure appeared.
This story spread widely in the universe. It was just a legend.
This legend was to tell everyone that those with great obsession would press forward even if it was a seemingly stupid goal. What if … they succeeded?
The first prerequisite for carrying out the obsession was to "live". They needed to live.
That year, when Ying created the Cycle of Life and Death, she had cleverly used this point to open up this space between life and death.
The space between life and death was the closest place to death. She used the threat of death to arouse the desire to live in the obsession!
To break through this space between life and death, one had to comprehend life and death.
However, to comprehend life and death in such a short time? And to do it in front of her? This was something that was almost impossible to do.
Her remaining opponents were all newly born geniuses in the past few decades. They had completely understood life and death. Could these little guys do it?
With this move, how could they be her opponents?
"Thump!"
Another muffled sound was heard.
Luo Zheng was close to death. Thus, the fear became stronger. The stronger the fear, the more severe the impact on Luo Zheng's mind …
Therefore, his heartbeat became more and more violent, as if a cannon had exploded in his chest!
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's body was different from ordinary people's.
If that wasn't the case, his chest would probably explode with a single heartbeat!
Luo Zheng's body staggered as he leaned against the thin line, gasping for breath.
The wounds on his body were nothing …
However, the pressure in his heart was like a heavy hammer with the weight of a hundred mountains, pressing down on his heart. It could be lifted up high and smashed down at any time.
"Don't be stubborn anymore. Give the Sacred Massacre Spear to me, and I promise you that I will help Smoky reconstruct in the spirit embryo. I can also admit defeat in this battle …" Qing Yu originally wanted to say that she could give Smoky a push from behind, but when she thought about how her cultivation was only at the Divine Sea Realm, she couldn't choose to be Yao's enemy.
As she spoke, she approached Luo Zheng. She even extended a jade-like hand to Luo Zheng, as if she wanted to support Luo Zheng.
However, her gaze was fixed on the Sumeru Ring in Luo Zheng's hand.
If she killed Luo Zheng, he would resurrect on the chess piece. Before the victor was decided, she must snatch the Sacred Massacre Spear.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly stood up, and the Thunder and Wind Dark Divine Sword drew an arc in front of him!
"Whoosh!"
The sword's edge slashed out a half-moon in the air.
The front of the half-moon just happened to brush past Qing Yu's finger …
Drops of blood sputtered out.
Qing Yu looked at the wound on her hand, and then a green light flashed. Under the cover of the Law of Life, the wound quickly healed.
"You …" At this moment, as Qing Yu sized up Luo Zheng, a trace of doubt appeared on her face.
Logically speaking, Luo Zheng's struggle should have required a strong will, and he should have suffered an even greater backlash.
However, he didn't seem to be affected by this life and death situation.
He could see through life and death so quickly?
Impossible …
When Qing Yu looked into Luo Zheng's eyes, her shapely eyebrows slightly wrinkled.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes didn't have the slightest trace of emotion. He looked like a lifeless puppet.
Qing Yu was very sensitive to the power of life.
A situation like Luo Zheng's should only appear on those lifeless puppets!
However, Luo Zheng's own power of life didn't disappear …
"Is it strange?" Luo Zheng said coldly. The Thunder and Wind Dark Divine Sword's edge glowed with a cold light. "This life and death situation is indeed strange. It can even be used as a place for cultivation."
Some Divine Ocean realm martial artists knew that they couldn't achieve good results, but they still chose to enter the Illusory Battlefield to gain experience and accept the most realistic test of life and death. It was very helpful to them.
"However, I don't need this kind of test for the time being," Luo Zheng continued coldly.
Qing Yu's face was full of doubt. "You … how did you do it?"

"What Divine Dao?" Qing Yu asked.
"Weng weng weng …"
As soon as she said that, Luo Zheng's longsword began to hum.
From the sword's body, there was a fluctuating sword intent …
"My Divine Dao is … the Emotion Severing Divine Dao!"
After saying that, Luo Zheng suddenly took a step forward. His right arm thrust the longsword's tip towards Qing Yu!
Because he thrust it out too quickly, the Thunder and Wind Dark Divine Sword's slender body was even bent by the air, like a small arch bridge. However, after this sword was completely thrust out, the originally straight body of the sword was straightened by Luo Zheng!
At the same time, the unparalleled power of the Divine Yi Sword burst out!
Seeing this, Qing Yu's face changed greatly!
The tip of her foot spun, and her entire body rapidly slid back on that thin line.
After sliding back for less than three steps, she discovered that the sword light was extremely fast. Her retreating speed was simply unable to shake it off!
Qing Yu's reaction was extremely fast. After sliding back three steps, she jumped up and flipped in the air to dodge the Divine Yi Sword.
However, Luo Zheng didn't give her the slightest chance. After flipping in the air, she once again stepped on that thin line. At this moment, Qing Yu's face was extremely unsightly.
The Emotion Severing Divine Dao …
Regardless of whether it was obsession or fear, they were all manifestations of human emotions.
This was the terror between life and death.
However, Luo Zheng had comprehended the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, which was to completely sever one's emotions!
At this moment, Luo Zheng's heart didn't have the slightest bit of distracting thoughts. His seven emotions and six desires were more or less severed, so there was no fear of death!
Many people in the Human Dao Alliance knew that Luo Zheng cultivated the Divine Yi Sword.
However, the scope of the news was only in the Thirteen Heavenly Palaces …
However, they also didn't know that Luo Zheng cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Dao!
The Divine Yi Sword was the Emotion Severing Divine Dao?
When the cultivators who had been affected by the sword cloud saw this scene, their hearts were greatly shaken.
Some people had comprehended a small portion of the Divine Yi Sword and knew that this sword was very powerful, but they didn't pay too much attention to it.
For example, Ai Anxin had comprehended quite a bit, but she paid more attention to her master's teachings …
However, she never thought that the Divine Yi Sword could comprehend the Emotion Severing Divine Dao.
After all, only a few people knew that Chen Huang had become a true divinity with the Divine Yi Sword. Even more people thought that he had died.
"Emotion Severing Divine Dao, Brother Luo Zheng actually cultivated this Divine Dao. This … isn't this the Restriction Dao!" Hua Tianming's face revealed a surprised expression. He guessed that Luo Zheng had probably entered the Divine Dao, but he didn't think that he would choose the Restriction Dao.
"This sword is quite powerful." Xuanyuan Chenfeng revealed a confident smile.
Although he was praising Luo Zheng, he was already comparing himself to Luo Zheng in his heart.

No matter what, with this guy's cultivation, he probably only had enough for two or three sword strikes!
"I'm not afraid," Xuanyuan Chenfeng said indifferently. He had absolute confidence in himself at all times.

Chapter 1355

What could strike a blow to such a guy?

The sword in his hand was faster than the previous one!
Every single sword strike contained peerlessly swift and fierce Sword Insight!

Luo Zheng's sword strike consumed a terrifying amount of Zhen Yuanyu's Zhen Yuanyu, not to mention dozens of sword strikes.

Yi Shen's sword slashed towards her crazily. There seemed to be no end to this wave of attack.

Dodging Luo Zheng's Sword of God Yi in a vertical direction was obviously very dangerous …
She had no choice but to remove the Cycle of Life and Death!
The Cycle of Life and Death had already lost its effect on Luo Zheng. If she didn't remove it, she would be the one restricting herself.
"Sou!"
A sword light flashed past her snow-white thighs and entered the chessboard, leaving behind a fine mark on the board. Although the mark was small, it had penetrated the chessboard …
"Puff!"
At the same time, blood sputtered out from her thighs. The blood flowed down her snow-white thighs, which was a ghastly sight.
For Qingyu, this kind of small injury could be healed in an instant.

"Heart Eating Insects!"
While retreating, Qingyu gently spread out her hands, and countless Heart Eating Insects suddenly appeared beside her.
These Heart Eating Insects intertwined with each other, forming a black mass. They formed a huge arrow and rushed towards Luo Zheng …
These Heart Eating Insects had caused several Heaven's Prides to suffer losses.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't have the slightest bit of carelessness.

These Heart Eating Insects were not afraid of water or fire, and it was difficult to kill them with ordinary methods.

With this sword, Luo Zheng exploded that Heart Eating Insect.

Luo Zheng was also somewhat afraid of these Heart Eating Insects. They were densely packed, and they were almost indestructible!

"Pop Pop Pop Pop Pop …"
One by one, the Heart Eating Insects exploded under Luo Zheng's sword!
When a large number of Heart Eating Insects approached Luo Zheng, he used the Eight Winding Smoke!
Although these Heart Eating Insects weren't large, they would still cause small fluctuations as they danced in the air.
Luo Zheng's body was like a piece of paper, and he always maintained a distance of three inches from these Heart Eating Insects …
Although the Eight Winding Smoke was mysterious, Qingyu's main body, or the other Daoists, could still break it. However, these small Heart Eating Insects couldn't get close to Luo Zheng.
It had to be said that the two techniques Luo Zheng cultivated just so happened to restrain Qingyu's divine ability.
After continuously dodging, Qingyu became somewhat annoyed. She suddenly waved her hand, and a wooden staff appeared in her hand.
This wooden staff was thin at the front and thick at the back. The end of the wooden staff looked like a bird's nest, and it was covered with little green leaves. These leaves emitted a powerful life force.
This was the exclusive treasure of the Fiend Night Clan's King of Life, the 32nd supreme divine artifact in the universe, the Staff of Life!
"Hmph!"
When Luo Zheng forced her into a corner like this, a trace of anger appeared on Qingyu's face.
She had originally thought that she could easily take down this little guy, but now he had dragged her into such trouble. This was truly unexpected.
She had no choice but to take out the Staff of Life!
The instant Qingyu raised the Staff of Life, her face flickered with boundless dignity.
At this moment, it was as if she had transformed into the former Ying Ying!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Within the Sattva Scepter, streaks of faint green life force floated out!

Chapter 1356

The ninth level of the Law of Life had only one profound meaning, and that was reincarnation.

Chapter 1357

…

After she finished speaking, the ten Luo Zheng on her body suddenly started to move …
Every strand of hair and every inch of muscle on their bodies were exactly the same as Luo Zheng's.

Luo Zheng hadn't become a Dao Disciple. His emotion-severing wasn't complete, so he still felt a bit strange.
But this didn't affect his combat strength …
This woman was showing something to those Celestial Venerables.
He might not understand.
But Luo Zheng only knew that he had to win!

Luo Zheng didn't dare to take this punch head on and quickly retreated.

"Youshen Shadow!"
Dozens of shadows with the same silhouette as Luo Zheng suddenly shot out from the Thunder Wind Ghost Sword and charged towards those "Luo Zheng".

Chapter 1358

None of the Heavenly Venerables were Dao Children.

Qingyu was not a Heavenly Venerable, so she could only cultivate the Law of Life to this level.

Qingyu didn't have a strong killing intent in this aura.

In order to reincarnate Luo Zheng, the prerequisite was to kill him first and then bestow him with a new life.

Chapter 1361

How could he tell that Luo Zheng had actually condensed an Instant Godhood?

But there was a prerequisite for the Instant Godhood to appear, and that was to have a Godhood Plate.

But when this Instant Godhood appeared, a part of the Chaotic Qi in Luo Zheng's body temporarily replaced it, turning into a Godhood Plate!
So far, even in the Realm of the Gods, there wasn't a proper explanation as to why the Instant Godhood was born.
To a True God, the Instant Godhood might not be of much use. After all, they already had a Godhood, but the Divine Dao they pursued was completely different.
But to Luo Zheng, it was extremely useful.
Not to mention the power in the Godhood, when he waved his sword again, the Dao of the Cold Mirror Divine Dao dyed the broken sword in his hand with a touch of icy blue light. The cold air that lingered around Luo Zheng's body turned into an ice lotus that slowly bloomed.
A touch of sword essence was formed.

Chapter 1378

"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"

However, at this moment, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's movement technique was very unique!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng seemed to be stepping on some kind of pattern as he continuously moved within the circle of light. If someone were to accurately measure Xuanyuan Chenfeng's steps, they would discover that the distance between his two steps was three feet!
Every step was three feet, no more or less than a centimeter. It was terrifyingly accurate!

No matter how he dodged, the distance between them was always an integral multiple of a foot, not a single bit more.
Almost all cultivators who had reached this level of cultivation had grasped Minute Subtlety. Most of them were able to cut off a mosquito's leg with a single sword strike without taking its life.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's accuracy was truly shocking!

However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who was originally in a rage, was exceptionally calm …
In his eyes, all colors had disappeared.

The more lines there were, the more flaws there were in the material.
This chessboard was fragile, so wherever his eyes passed, there would be many flaws!
Those chess pieces could not be destroyed, and there might be flaws in them, but Xuanyuan Chenfeng could not see them at all. This was because of the limitation of his cultivation.
In addition …
The bolts of lightning were also filled with cracks!
The movement of time and all things had their own rules, and what restricted these rules were the various laws of the universe …
Actually, some laws were discovered by cultivators, and they were carefully studied and comprehended. Just like those origin laws, they were the most important laws, and the number of people who cultivated them was also the most widespread.
At the same time, there were also some people who took other paths and started from some special laws.
For example, the movement of the celestial body, the awakening of the dreamland, and the rebirth of the netherworld.
Luo Zheng's Stellar Battle Body used the laws of the celestial body to activate the power of the stars …
These laws were set by the Heavenly Dao.
The Heavenly Dao was set up by the gods.
The set up of laws was the Divine Dao of Truth. The Divine Dao of Truth was the set up of laws!
It was also the most important part of the three thousand Great Dao, the Great Dao that had been formed for billions of years, the foundation of the existence of the gods …
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's Comprehension of Truth could see through the movement of thousands of laws in the universe, so all he could see were lines and dots, which were the most basic picture of the universe!
Although the red tasseled long spear in his hand was a supreme divine weapon, it was not a very outstanding treasure.
Because he did not need it …
Anything in his eyes would show a huge flaw!
The Spear of Law that he cultivated was actually much more powerful than what people had seen, because the Spear of Law could directly destroy the opponent through those flaws!
That was why Xuanyuan Chenfeng had put so much pressure on Ku Deng.
Before he confronted Ku Deng, the Spear of Law had already locked on Ku Deng's biggest flaw.
Three inches to the right of Ku Deng's chest, there was a small round dot, and from that small dot, many thin lines spread out. Most of these thin lines spread out above Ku Deng's heart, and a small part spread out in his mind, even connecting with his soul!
If the Spear of Law broke it, it could directly destroy Ku Deng!
Soul, state of mind, body …
That was why he could put so much pressure on Ku Deng.
That dot was Ku Deng's Achilles' heel!
A few years ago, when Ku Deng cultivated the Golden Bell Shield, he also knew that his Achilles' heel was three inches to the right of his chest, but Ku Deng never thought that there would be such a big flaw under this Achilles' heel …
Actually, when Xuanyuan Chenfeng first saw Luo Zheng, he was a bit confused.
His Comprehension of Truth could see the flaws of all things.
The stronger one's cultivation, the fewer flaws there were …
A Spirit Sea Realm warrior usually had more than a hundred flaws on his body.
Extreme God realm martial artists had dozens of flaws.
Divine Transformation realm martial artists, in Xuanyuan Chenfeng's eyes, usually only had ten flaws.
Sector Lord realm martial artists, he could occasionally see one flaw, but most of the time, he could not see any.
Empyrean realm martial artists were beyond the scope of Xuanyuan Chenfeng's Comprehension of Truth, unable to observe.
However, Luo Zheng's body only had four lines, as for the Achilles' heel, Xuanyuan Chenfeng could not see where it was!
He was clearly just a Divine Sea realm martial artist, why did his body only have so few flaws? It was even rarer than Divine Transformation realm martial artists …
Xuanyuan Chenfeng could not figure it out.
However, this did not matter, Luo Zheng only had fewer flaws, but it did not mean that he did not have any flaws.
As long as there was a flaw, his Spear of Law would be able to display a terrifying destructive power.
At first, he shot out a spear light, pointing at Luo Zheng's earlobe. From the edge of Luo Zheng's earlobe, there was a thin line that extended all the way to the top of his head, and then went straight down!
Therefore, that purple spear light, which was not that powerful, almost split Luo Zheng in half from the middle!
From this, it could be seen that if Xuanyuan Chenfeng wanted to kill Luo Zheng, it would not be too difficult.
At this moment, Xuanyuan Chenfeng stepped forward, pointing his long spear at the sky. He did not choose to escape, but to fiercely stab at the sky!
"Break for me!"
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
Spears of Law abruptly shot out.
The purple spear light was like a precise thin line, pointing at the wild thunder.
Even the Heavenly Thunder had its flaws.
When those flaws were hit by the purple spear light, they were like snakes that were hit at their weakest point, breaking, collapsing, and dissipating!

Chapter 1379
Xuanyuan Chenfeng instantly cleared the wild lightning above his head …
This scene left countless martial artists dumbfounded.
These lightning tribulations could only be resisted by the physical body or channeled into the earth.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng used his spear to shatter them. This kind of visual shock was hard to describe. It had already exceeded the common sense of many warriors.
They did not know that even if a ball of fire was ignited, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's Spear of Law would shatter it with a single spear.
As soon as he dealt with Luo Zheng's Thunder Tribulation Annihilation, a gold and grey dual-colored sword light immediately stabbed towards him!
"Hmph!"
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's face revealed a trace of disdain.

And Xuanyuan Chenfeng, through his comprehension of the truth, could see it even more clearly!
Within that gold and grey dual-colored sword light, there was a big vital point. After Xuanyuan Chenfeng took a step forward, the long spear in his hand suddenly flicked, and the purple sword light hit that small point in the middle!

Luo Zheng faintly smiled, but shook his head.
Relying on the Thunder Tribulation Annihilation and the Gui Yuanyi Divine Sword to defeat Xuanyuan Chenfeng, he was thinking too lightly.
He only used these methods to observe his opponent.
After Xuanyuan Chenfeng left the stage, that strange feeling lingered in Luo Zheng's heart, and it could not dissipate.
Now, Luo Zheng saw a clue …
This guy's movements looked very casual, but it seemed to follow some kind of rule from beginning to end!
After careful observation, he discovered this rule, which was distance!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's movement was measured in feet. No matter where he moved from, the final position he stopped at was an integral multiple of feet!
If he moved a step, it would definitely be a distance of three feet. It wouldn't be two feet and nine inches, nor would it be three feet and one inch …
Although Luo Zheng was unable to reach Xuanyuan Chenfeng's level, his eyesight was not far from the mark.
This guy seemed to be strictly abiding by some kind of law!
Seeing through this, Luo Zheng did not mean that he could break Xuanyuan Chenfeng's spear!
Because of the 《Thunder Kill》, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was in such a difficult situation. At this moment, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was no longer in the mood to deal with Luo Zheng!
With a sudden tap of his feet, he charged towards Luo Zheng with the spear in his hand!
"This guy's trajectory is so straight …"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. His figure suddenly floated. He faced Xuanyuan Chenfeng and quickly left in a semicircle.
When a martial artist flew in the air, they were erratic. Even if they flew in a straight line, there would be a certain degree of swaying.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's trajectory was a pure straight line without any deviation. Looking at it roughly, there wasn't any problem, but looking at it carefully, it did give people a strange feeling.
Now that Luo Zheng thought about it again, the strange expression on his face became more and more intense.
When he faced Ku Deng, Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not make a move. Instead, he faced Ku Deng with his spear, standing in place and spinning non-stop …
Just now, when this guy faced him, it was the same. He stood in the same position and spun non-stop, never taking the initiative to attack.
The only two times Xuanyuan Chenfeng took the initiative to attack were when he held his spear in a pure straight line. Could it be that this guy could only walk in a straight line?
This answer was somewhat inexplicable. A crab could only walk horizontally, but this guy could only walk vertically. However, Luo Zheng felt that his guess was getting closer and closer to the truth …
So when Luo Zheng circled and dodged, Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not attack. Was it because he couldn't lock onto me? Or was it because this guy's attack required some kind of condition to be realized?
One spear to shatter all things, this kind of method was indeed too terrifying. In theory, this was also a kind of karmic method. It should be realized by relying on the Heavenly Dao.
This kind of method was so powerful, it should have a very powerful restriction.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was a Dao child. The Shinto he cultivated should not be the Heavenly Dao …
As Luo Zheng was thinking, he kept retreating in a zigzag pattern. Xuanyuan Chenfeng's spear was so terrifying, Luo Zheng could not take it head on. However, he did not know if this guy would attack with his spear while he was moving.
Soon, Luo Zheng found that his guess was verified.
Not only could Xuanyuan Chenfeng walk vertically, he could also move horizontally. He was really a crab!
Under this kind of fast horizontal movement, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's spear suddenly pointed at Luo Zheng. A purple spear light as thick as a finger pierced towards Luo Zheng!
The current Xuanyuan Chenfeng already had the intention to kill. He only wanted to get rid of Luo Zheng quickly. He did not hold back at all!
If this spear light hit Luo Zheng's body, it would be enough to completely disintegrate him!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng already had a guess in his heart. His figure once again circled around and moved forward!
According to his guess, this spear light should be very difficult to change its direction. It could only move at a very small angle!
At this moment, Xuanyuan Chenfeng also saw through Luo Zheng's intention. This guy seemed to have seen through something. His face revealed a cold smile. "Childish!"
As Luo Zheng flew in an arc, the purple spear light formed a strange turn. It turned at a 90 degree angle in the air and flew towards Luo Zheng!
"This …"
Luo Zheng also found that he had made a mistake in his judgment.
This spear light indeed could not turn, but it could change direction at a 90 degree angle!
This scene gave Luo Zheng a faint sense of familiarity. He seemed to have seen this thing somewhere before!
The purple spear light turned at a 90 degree angle and continued to chase after Luo Zheng …
As he kept retreating, his eyes suddenly flashed. He remembered!
When Luo Zheng was crossing the heavenly tribulation, an eye appeared. That eye released a black light. That black light moved in a straight line without any curve!
That black light almost had the power to destroy the world!
What was the connection between the two?
No matter what the connection was …
This thing was very dangerous.
He did not believe Xuanyuan Chenfeng's nonsense. He was even more unwilling to lose to this guy!
However, this spear light was still faster than him. No matter how he retreated, the distance between the two was still shrinking.
At the beginning, there was still a distance of ten zhang. After a few breaths, this distance was quickly shortened.
Fifty zhang …
Forty zhang …
Thirty zhang …
"Die." Xuanyuan Chenfeng shook his head slightly. In his eyes, Luo Zheng was already bound to lose.
He did not need to show too much power. This spear of law was enough to completely destroy Luo Zheng. There was no way he could escape.
Luo Zheng sucked in a deep breath of cold air as he retreated at top speed. His heart sank!
It seemed that he really did not have a chance to turn the situation around when facing Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
He was very unwilling to lose to this guy!
At this moment, Luo Zheng's expression was somewhat flustered. However, his eyes flashed. He seemed to have thought of something. He stretched out his hand and the mini Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain spiraled out of his hand! The moment the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain appeared, it began to expand rapidly. It turned into a hundred zhang tall mountain in front of Luo Zheng!

Xuanyuan Chenfeng sneered. The spear light drilled into the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain!

In the next moment, this mountain should collapse.
"Bang!"
The purple spear light instantly sank into the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.

He was slightly stunned. He immediately used his comprehension of truth. When he looked again, his expression became even more strange. Such a big mountain actually did not have any flaws?
"This is the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain!" Xuanyuan Chenfeng finally reacted.
When he was in the Ji Family, he had seen such a Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain!
This Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain's five elements were heterogeneous. It was because the five origin laws were evenly mixed together. When the five elements intertwined, they could make up for each other's flaws.
With Xuanyuan Chenfeng's current comprehension of truth, it was not enough to see through the flaws!
While he was thinking, Luo Zheng's figure quietly appeared on top of the mountain. He looked down at Xuanyuan Chenfeng and seemed to be holding an extremely sharp green light in his hand!

Chapter 1380
At this moment, the person that was the most shocked wasn't Xuanyuan Chenfeng.

Wasn't this speed a bit too shocking?
As for the members of the Ji Clan, they were extremely shocked when they saw Luo Zheng had actually refined the Divine Origin Magnet Mountain.
Their Ji Clan had left a Divine Origin Magnet Mountain there for countless years, yet they'd never heard of anyone in the universe being capable of refining this Divine Origin Magnet Mountain that was impervious to everything.

Chapter 1382

A huge black shadow streaked across the clouds, followed by a second one, a third one …
"What's that?"
As he continued to travel, he passed through the clouds and fog. Finally, those black shadows appeared in Luo Zheng's line of sight.
Those black shadows were green mountains that were arranged neatly. These mountains looked almost identical in appearance, as if they were clones of the same mountain.

When Luo Zheng approached one of the mountains, he found a milky white disc floating among the huge stone pillars.

His figure floated lightly and arrived in front of the disc. After carefully examining it, Luo Zheng couldn't help but let out a cry of surprise.

This was indeed very strange!

"…"

In other words, Jiang Yu 'er had helped Luo Zheng modify his luck in the universe.
If Luo Zheng's luck increased, it would be of great help to many probabilistic things. For example, it would be easier to attract the stars and so on.
Luo Zheng's providence had been very good to begin with, but when he had devoured the Fortune-Destroying Bamboo Snakes, his providence had reached its peak. However, it had only lasted for an extremely short period of time. At that time, Luo Zheng hadn't taken advantage of this opportunity. With his providence at its peak, cultivating the Stellar Combat Body would have been extremely easy.
His current luck was naturally not as heaven-defying as when he swallowed the Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes.
However, it was not easy to modify one's luck. This was the ability of the Heavenly Dao, right?
This Jiang Yu 'er was probably the controller of this Heavenly Dao. However, it was not clear what role she played in the Heavenly Dao. She did not seem to casually interfere with the various changes in the Heavenly Dao …
After waiting for a long time on this mountain.
Luo Zheng saw the second person appear from the thick white fog.
That person was tall and straight, with a heroic face. It was Hua Tianming.
The winner could enter the fog and carry the Divine Spark Plate first. Did that mean that Hua Tianming had also defeated Xuanyuan Chenfeng?
Next was Xuanyuan Chenfeng. His handsome face was full of malice. He had only gotten third place, so he must be very disappointed in his heart …
Qing Yu was fourth, and Ku Deng was fifth.
Xi Youqin, on the other hand, was ranked tenth...
Actually, in terms of strength, Xi Youqin's ranking should be higher.
However, after she entered the top ten, she admitted defeat all the way, so her final result was naturally the lowest.

Chapter 1383
The Illusory Battlefield came to an end …
The picture scroll that appeared in the entire universe slowly closed up and dissipated in front of everyone.
When Luo Zheng opened his eyes again, he felt as if he had been cut off from a lifetime ago.

At this moment, the entire universe was changing.
The first to change was the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele.
After some geniuses passed the Karma Hall, they chose to copy the Supreme Artifact in their hands. As a result, the ranking on the Myriad Spirit Stele underwent a drastic change.
Originally, there were only 3,000 Supreme Artifacts. Now, there were more than 1,000. Obviously, these Supreme Artifacts were all copied through the Karma Hall …
When the light that shone on Luo Zheng's body dissipated, three colored light spots appeared around him. These light spots were like countless little fairies, flying toward Luo Zheng's body.

After Mu Mingxue entered the Illusory Battlefield, she finally saw the experts of the universe. They were all in the Divine Sea Realm, but there was such a huge gap between them. Xun had the body of a sword spirit and was not sick. What was going on with her sickly face?

After the Illusory Battlefield, the number of supreme artifacts increased to more than 4,000. Many first-grade divine artifacts were squeezed out of the Myriad Spirit Stele.

After so many life-and-death experiences in the Illusory Battlefield, countless Divine Sea Realm warriors had accumulated cultivation and used this opportunity to break through their bottlenecks!
Countless Divine Sea Realm warriors in the universe chose to go into seclusion at this moment, and Luo Zheng was no exception!

Chapter 1384

Back when he entered Feng Nianyun's body, it was an island.

Waves surged out of the brown ocean, rolling towards the small island.
