If one were to carefully observe the Primal Chaos Ocean, one would be able to see some black floating things mixed within.
After some floating monsters were washed onto the brown island, they drilled into the "soil" of the island. This soil was also formed by the chaotic gas. Soon after, the surface of the island turned sporadic green, and in the end, it became a piece of slippery moss!
Only when the evolution of life reached this point did it slow down.
Regardless of whether it was the plankton or the moss, they were the most primitive forms of life.
If one wanted to reproduce all kinds of life forms, or even humans, they would need enough time.
Feng Nianyun had spent a hundred thousand years, and only managed to evolve a primitive tribe. How long would it take for him to evolve humans?
…
Outside the Divine Realm …
In the endless Primal Chaos.
This was a forbidden land even for True Gods.
Only an extremely small number of mighty figures could travel through the Primal Chaos …
In ancient times, there were mighty figures who had once traveled a distance of a million fathoms into the Primal Chaos (one fathom was equivalent to a million miles), but the depths of the Primal Chaos were still the Primal Chaos.
Where the end was, no one knew …
At this moment, somewhere in the Primal Chaos, a small sphere spread out …
The sphere was like a bubble, rapidly expanding!
However, after expanding to a certain extent, it stopped, as if it was waiting for the next expansion.
If a mighty figure were to see this scene, they would be so shocked that they would be speechless. The bubble contained a World Tree, forming a world of its own!
Ever since the Axe of Good Fortune split open the heavens, the construction of all worlds revolved around the Divine Realm!
No one had ever been able to create a world of their own in the Primal Chaos!

"Phew …"
Xun suddenly dissipated in front of Luo Zheng, turning into wisps of sword spirit and entering Luo Zheng's body.
"This …" Luo Zheng's face was full of confusion.

Luo Zheng smiled. "This is a Teleportation Token …"
In the Illusory Battlefield, the Teleportation Token could allow Luo Zheng to move back and forth in a large world. Therefore, Luo Zheng thought of copying this Teleportation Token.

Chapter 1385
Teleportation was the only way to move long distances in the universe.
One needed to have a deep understanding of the law of space and be a sector lord to be able to teleport. The level of one's cultivation also seemed to determine the distance one could teleport.
When the celestial venerables wanted to capture Luo Zheng, it only took them a day or two to gather.
As for the sector lords behind them, they had to wait for a long time before they could catch up.
Mu Mingxue rested her chin on her hands. She didn't understand, but she didn't ask. She just silently watched Luo Zheng's every move.

The instant he activated the Teleportation Token, the violent spatial fluctuations were like a tornado, wrapping him up within!
"Weng …"

And Luo Zheng once again fell into a space …
What appeared in front of Luo Zheng was a majestic scene. The stars in the sky seemed to be condensed together, densely packed together like dazzling grains of sand. And below these grains of sand were pieces of circular plates …
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered, his expression was somewhat excited.
He could already see that each circular plate was a great world. This was a miniature version of the entire universe!
The teleportation token could actually do such a thing. Its ability was almost beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
In addition to these circular plates, there were thin lines below each of them. Since there were a hundred thousand of these circular plates, they represented a hundred thousand great worlds. And these lines extended from the bottom of the circular plates all the way to the very bottom!
"That's a great world!" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered incessantly. These lines were tangled and complicated, they should represent various ascension passages.
The entire universe had shrunk countless times and appeared in front of him. At a glance, it was indeed shocking enough.
When Luo Zheng had ascended and used his consciousness to travel among the stars, he had used this to look down on millions of great worlds. But at that time, Luo Zheng could not see the whole picture. The worlds that extended to extremely far places were covered in endless darkness.

Among these great worlds, there were many lines that did not extend in the direction of the Upper Realm. They seemed to extend to other places. Soon, Luo Zheng understood that those places should be the True Dragon Realm, the True Phoenix Realm, and other independent realms …
After understanding this, Luo Zheng stared at those rhombus-shaped great worlds. These three sections of the great worlds seemed to be the back of some kind of Ferocious Beast! These rhombus-shaped great worlds were the scales of some kind of Ferocious Beast.
Was it really a Ferocious Beast? Did such a huge Ferocious Beast really exist in this world?
At this stage, Luo Zheng did not understand the mystery. With the help of this Teleportation Token, Luo Zheng seemed to be able to easily choose his own location. After confirming it, he floated toward one of the discs.

It was all her means. Even a Celestial Master could only teleport forward again and again. Moreover, it was not easy to control the direction of long-distance teleportation. If one was not careful, they would be teleported to an inexplicable place.
If one wanted to reach a destination, he would have to teleport dozens or even hundreds of times. Something as convenient as the Teleportation Token had never appeared in the universe before.
Accompanied by a fierce flash of light, Luo Zheng appeared from nothingness.

At this moment, he was high up in the sky. In front of him was a vast plain.
"White Sun Realm, an independent realm …"
There were not many independent realms in the universe. All the independent realms belonged to those big forces.
The White Sun Realm belonged to the independent warriors. It could be regarded as a place where countless independent warriors yearned for. This was because the famous "Sword Manor" was located in this independent realm.
Independent warriors were not restricted, and there were people of all races in this realm.
In fact, strictly speaking, the Sword Manor could be regarded as a big force. However, the Sword Manor did not have many restrictions on the independent warriors who joined it. For those independent warriors who yearned for freedom, it was an excellent home.
There were priorities. Luo Zheng still had many problems to solve. However, the most important thing now was to become a Daoist. Therefore, the trip to the Sword Manor was imperative for Luo Zheng …
After flying high up in the sky for a while, Luo Zheng noticed that many black dots appeared on the plain. They should be villages and cities. However, under a bird's eye view, they were as small as ants.

Chapter 1386
Although Luo Zheng used the teleportation token to enter the White Sun Realm, it still took a long time to travel through a large realm.
To get to the sword manor, one had to pass through a large realm passageway.

When they were halfway down, Luo Zheng patted his head and took out the Gen token.
He forgot to let Mu Mingxue out.
Afo had been staying in the immortal abode as a puppet for thousands of years, so it didn't matter.
As for Shenzi Shenzi, he was so engrossed in refining that he probably couldn't be dragged out …
However, Mu Mingxue had been trapped in the immortal abode for many years. When he first used the teleportation token, Mu Mingxue asked to come out with him, but Luo Zheng rejected her. After all, there was still a certain amount of danger at that time. Mu Mingxue also listened to him and never forced him.
Now that Luo Zheng had teleported here, even a Heavenly Venerable wouldn't be able to calculate it in a short time. Naturally, he wasn't afraid.
At this moment, after Luo Zheng activated the Gen token, he connected with the immortal abode and sent a voice transmission in.
Mu Mingxue was waiting at the door. However, her personality was like this. If she felt that Luo Zheng was in a difficult position, she wouldn't come out. In fact, her heart had already flown outside. Anyone who was trapped in the immortal abode for a few years would be bored.
Therefore, just as Luo Zheng transmitted his voice, Mu Mingxue suddenly appeared beside Luo Zheng. She didn't expect Luo Zheng to float in mid-air and fall straight down the moment she appeared.
After Luo Zheng pulled her, Mu Mingxue stabilized her body.
Returning to the universe, she looked at the vast plains and smiled slightly. She couldn't help but stretch. These few years had really suffocated her, but she didn't seem to have any complaints …
The two of them, one in front and one behind, were still a thousand feet above the city when huge maple leaves appeared, revealing a faint power. That was the restriction of flight.
"Let's enter through the city gate." Luo Zheng circled around with Mu Mingxue and entered through the front of the city.
After asking around, they found out that the city was in the north of the White Sun Realm, and the sword manor was in the southeast.
Therefore, after paying some supreme-grade True Origin stones, Luo Zheng took Mu Mingxue into the spatial passageway, and then turned into the great realm passageway …
Even traveling through the realm passageway, it took them about half a month's time.
Along the way, Mu Mingxue appeared to be very happy, as if she was a prisoner who had been locked up for many years and finally regained her freedom …
However, Xun remained immersed in Luo Zheng's body.
Half a month later, they left the realm passageway and arrived at the sword manor.
Although there was a 'manor' in the two words' sword manor ', it didn't mean that this force was small. After all, the sword manor was at the top level among independent martial artists. The sword manor had given birth to a Heavenly Venerable like Chen Huang Yijian.
As the light of the spatial laws dissipated, Mu Mingxue saw the scene in front of her and exclaimed, "What a big sword …"
The entire great realm of the White Sun Realm seemed to be a plain. After exiting the spatial passageway, the scenery in front of them could be seen. The great realm was located in a huge and flat city. In the distance, there was a huge sword inserted into the ground …
This great realm passageway was very busy. There were people coming in and out constantly. When someone heard Mu Mingxue's words, he couldn't help but roll his eyes and whisper, "I wonder which corner it came from …"
Before he could finish his sentence, the people around him interrupted him. They glanced at Luo Zheng, warning him not to speak carelessly.
Luo Zheng had already stepped into the Extreme Divine Realm. His strength had increased by a level, so his status in the eyes of others would naturally increase. After all, as long as one was a Extreme Divine Realm martial artist, they could find a good job in an ordinary tenth grade holy land. Ordinary martial artists didn't want to provoke Luo Zheng.
"It's really big." Luo Zheng looked at it and estimated that the sword was tens of thousands of feet high. This sword must have been made by someone. It was probably the symbol of the sword manor.
Luo Zheng only had one goal, and that was to go to the sword manor to train his sword intent. He hoped to complete the Emotion Severing Divine Path and perfect the last lotus flower.
This sword manor was the holy land of the world's swordsmen. Even the swordsmen of large forces would go to the sword manor to train their sword intent. Xun was one example …
After spending a few scattered True Essence Stones, Luo Zhengcai found a local martial artist. After some inquiries, Luo Zhengcai learned that the city beneath his feet was called Sword Hearing City. The culture of the Sword Dao was very rich. Along the way, he could see many Sword Pavilions. It was said that even ordinary people would practice the sword for self-defense.
"Young master, if you want to visit the sword manor, I'm afraid it won't be so convenient," the martial artist said, shaking his head.
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked.
The martial artist glanced at Luo Zheng, "Young master is also a Extreme Divine Realm martial artist. You can be considered a top figure among independent martial artists. But since ancient times, the rules have been very strict. To enter the sword manor, you must first go to the sword hall. You can only enter after obtaining a nine-star sword token …"
"Nine-star sword token?" Luo Zheng was surprised.
The martial artist smiled faintly, "If young master is a swordsman and has profound attainments in sword arts, it shouldn't be difficult for you to obtain this sword token. But there are only three days left this month …"
Luo Zheng thought that anyone could enter the sword manor at will. He didn't know that there was such a rule. Seeing that the martial artist was very cautious, he meant that this rule had never been broken since ancient times. To enter the sword manor, one must obtain a nine-star sword token.
At that time, Chen Huang's Yi Sword had left a sword-shaped mark on Luo Zheng's body. With this mark, it shouldn't be a problem to enter the sword manor.
According to this martial artist, this rule had never been broken before. Luo Zheng had to do as the Romans do …
After explaining the rules, Luo Zheng quickly understood. There were countless Sword Halls around the Sword Manor, and they taught swordsmen and martial artists. These martial artists would be able to obtain their own Sword Token after competing with each other for several levels.
In fact, when Luo Zheng stepped into the great world passage, he could be considered to have entered the sword manor. However, this was a sword manor in a broad sense. It could be said that it was a place and a big force. A real sword manor required a sword token to reach nine stars.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate. Under the martial artist's lead, he found a sword hall.
After entering the sword hall, he spent a few True Yuan Stones to buy his own sword token …
In this sword hall, there were many people like Luo Zheng. He was a swordsman from the outside world who had reached a bottleneck in his cultivation. Therefore, he came to the sword manor, hoping to break through his shackles. However, they didn't know that the one who guided Luo Zheng here was Chen Huang's Yi Sword, the former master of the sword manor.
Therefore, the sword hall quickly sent someone to compete with Luo Zheng. That person was only in the Life and Death realm. How could he be a match for Luo Zheng?
Seeing that Luo Zheng was in the Extreme God Realm, the opponent knew that he wasn't a match for him.
After one sword strike, the victor was decided. It could be considered as a formality. Luo Zheng left with a two star sword token.
Not far away, Luo Zheng entered a relatively bigger sword hall with a two star sword token. Once again, he used one sword strike and came out with a three star sword token …
As Luo Zheng went deeper and deeper, those sword halls became more and more imposing. Of course, Luo Zheng's opponents also became more and more powerful.
However, facing these Life and Death realm and Spirit Sea realm opponents …
Luo Zheng would always use one sword strike. He didn't need a second sword strike.
Not long after, Luo Zheng's token changed from three star to six star. The whole process only took half a day.
This speed was nothing. After all, Luo Zheng's cultivation base was very high. Moreover, this was only a threshold to enter the sword hall. If Luo Zheng encountered difficulties here, he might as well find a brick and smash himself to death.
Mu Mingxue followed Luo Zheng everywhere. During this process, Mu Mingxue bought a map of the sword hall and inquired about some information. She adapted very quickly. By the time Luo Zheng obtained a seven star token, she could already act as Luo Zheng's tour guide.
"Only the six sword halls can give out eight star and nine star sword tokens. The closest one … is in that direction!" Mu Mingxue thought for a moment and reminded, "These six sword halls are equivalent to traditional ninth grade forces. However, for Young Master, it should be very simple!"
Mu Mingxue had gotten into character. She was very detailed in gathering information.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1387
This sword pavilion was called the Breeze Sword Pavilion, and it was one of the six major sword pavilions under the Sword Villa. The eight-star and nine-star sword tokens could be exchanged in the Breeze Sword Pavilion.
According to Luo Zheng's estimation, it would be best to obtain the nine-star sword token in one day.
Along the way, they shuttled through various sword pavilions to obtain the tokens from one-star to seven-star. Their speed was already extremely fast, but it still took them half a day. When Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue arrived in front of the Breeze Sword Pavilion, the pavilion had already closed its doors …
"It seems that we can only stay for one more day." Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless smile.
Many martial artists spent years to advance from one-star sword tokens to nine-star sword tokens. That was also the path of growth for a swordsman.
After continuously advancing in the various sword pavilions and comprehending the way of the sword to a certain extent, one would naturally be qualified to enter the Sword Villa …
This was also the reason why the Sword Villa could attract so many independent martial artists.
The Sword Villa had no restrictions on independent martial artists. As long as they wholeheartedly focused on the way of the sword and comprehended the way of the sword to a certain level, the Sword Villa would selflessly open its doors to those independent martial artists.
Compared to the tens of thousands of people kneeling at the entrance of some tenth-rank Holy Lands, this kind of scene would not be seen at the entrance of the Sword Villa.
Luo Zheng, who was a "half-baked" person, naturally could not be counted among them. That was why he could obtain the seven-star sword token in one day. This speed was already extremely fast.
"We need to find a place to settle down tonight. Tomorrow, we will go to the Breeze Sword Pavilion," Luo Zheng said.
Mu Mingxue timidly said in a low voice, "Can we walk around the city?"
Hearing Mu Mingxue's small request, Luo Zheng smiled slightly. "Why not?"
Mu Mingxue's face immediately revealed a joyful expression. Her temperament was relatively stable, and it seemed that she was indeed very happy …
Not long after, Mu Mingxue inquired about some good markets in this big city.
Ever since Luo Zheng ascended, he had never understood the culture of the human race in the Upper Realm. Under the guidance of Mu Mingxue, this was the first time he had the leisure to wander around the bustling market.
During this time, Luo Zheng also asked Xun if she wanted to come out, but he didn't get a response from her …
This market square was very lively. Almost all of them were independent martial artists.
An independent cultivator was different from a cultivator who belonged to a great force. Cultivators who belonged to a Holy Land had the support of pills and Secret Arts from the Holy Land. With such benefits, they could concentrate on their cultivation, but the price was that they had to help the Holy Land complete missions, and most of the final rewards would be given to the Holy Land.
Independent martial artists didn't have those benefits, but they had to work hard for everything. Some martial artists went out to explore and found some good things to trade in the market square.
As they strolled through the city square, Luo Zheng didn't have much interest in the items. As for Mu Mingxue, she looked left and right and bought many small items. The price of these items was usually maintained at several top-grade true essence stones.
After a few rounds, Luo Zheng found that this market square had a much higher standard than Luo Zheng had imagined.
Many of the martial artists were in the Life and Death Realm, and many of them were in the Spirit Sea Realm. Along the way, they met several Extreme God Realm masters. Mu Mingxue brought him here because she knew that this market square was a relatively "high-end" trading place in the Sword Village. There were even some divine weapons for sale.
At this moment, there was a clamor in front of them …
"The auction is starting. Let's go!"
"It should be the last three Nine Star Sword Tokens this month. If we miss it, we won't have another chance!"
"But the price is so high. Where can we get so many Zhen Yuanyu?"
These martial artists rushed over there. Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue looked at each other with some doubts in their eyes.
How could the Nine Star Sword Tokens be bought?
Through the previous Sword Pavilions, Luo Zheng knew that the Sword Token was a kind of recognition for swordsmen. But he didn't know that there were people who could sell Sword Tokens. What was going on?
Luo Zheng's figure flashed, and he quietly shuttled through the crowd. Mu Mingxue also stepped forward and followed him.
There was a high platform in the center of the market square. On the platform, there were three Extreme God Realm warriors. Seeing that almost everyone had arrived, the leader of the Extreme God Realm warriors shouted in a cold voice, "Of the 500 Nine Star Sword Tokens this month, 497 have been sold. These are the last three tokens. You can name your price. If you miss this chance, you have to wait for another month …"
"The starting price is 100 Zhen Yuanyu!"
As soon as the leader finished his words, the martial artists below all started to bid.
"100!"
"115 …"
"120."
Most of the bidders were warriors in the Spirit Sea Realm. There were also a few Extreme God Realm warriors who bid.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng frowned even harder.
Before this, Luo Zheng had a trace of admiration for the Sword Village. After all, it was a righteous act to open such a big Sword Village to all the swordsmen in the world.
It was called the orthodox way of the sword. People who chose swords as weapons had a kind of righteousness …
"My friend, what do you mean by auctioning the Sword Token? Isn't the Sword Token won through the sword competition? "Luo Zheng asked.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the warrior next to him sneered, "What you said is old history. The Nine Star Sword Token is the qualification to enter the Sword Village. You can enter the Sword Village easily if you get the Sword Token? How can there be such a good thing in the world? "
"But isn't this the rule of the Sword Village? Aren't they using the name of the Sword Village for their own personal gain? " Luo Zheng asked again. He thought that he would be able to enter the Sword Village tomorrow. Judging by the situation, it wouldn't be easy to get the Nine Star Sword Token.
Although his voice wasn't loud, the Extreme God Realm warrior on the high platform heard it. The Extreme God Realm warrior stared at Luo Zheng, raised the Nine Star Sword Token in his hand, and walked to the edge of the high platform. He looked down at Luo Zheng and said, "You must be joking. The Sword Village gave us the right to the Six Sword Schools. So what if we auction it?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng raised his head and asked indifferently, "Then if I go to the Breeze Sword School to compete tomorrow, I won't be able to get the Nine Star Sword Token?"
The other party sneered, "Of course you won't be able to get it. If you want to get it, then wait for another month. You can get ten Nine Star Sword Tokens every month. Let's see if you are good enough! Haha … "
Following the rise of Yi Jian Tianzun, the reputation of the Sword Village spread far and wide. More and more warriors came to train. Not only were there independent warriors, but there were also many swordsmen from great powers.
However, there were only five hundred spots to enter the Sword Village every month. The Six Sword Schools firmly held onto the Nine Star Sword Token. At first, they were able to follow the rules. However, following the disappearance of Yi Jian Tianzun, the Sword Village gradually became more utilitarian. Although the rules set by Chen Huang Yijian were still there, no one followed them.
"You are breaking the rules of the Sword Village." Luo Zheng's voice also became cold. Chen Huang Yijian could be considered a favor to Luo Zheng. He first comprehended the Yi Shen Sword and was personally polished by him in the Karma Hall. He could be considered half a master. Chen Huang Yijian definitely didn't expect that after his "disappearance", the Sword Village would sell the right to him. How would he feel?
The warrior on the high platform originally had a smile on his face. However, after hearing these words, a chill appeared on his face. "Brat, this is the Sword Village. As the saying goes, disaster comes from the mouth. If you can't afford it, then scram. Don't be an eyesore here!"
These words were quite impolite. Obviously, he didn't take Luo Zheng's cultivation of the God Realm seriously.
Another warrior on the high platform, however, said with a strange smile: "The price of the Nine Star Sword Token is not expensive. Normally, it's only two or three hundred Zhen Yuanyu. If you don't have the money and really want it, hehe, give me that maid of yours at the Spirit Sea Realm and give me a hundred or so Zhen Yuanyu as a bonus. That should be enough!"
That warrior's eyes fell on Mu Mingxue.
There weren't many female warriors who had cultivated to the Spirit Sea Realm, and there were even fewer independent warriors. That warrior took a fancy to Mu Mingxue's beauty and saw that Mu Mingxue was following Luo Zheng. He was tempted.
Hearing these words, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly became cold. A faint killing intent flashed through his pupils.
Those warriors who were originally participating in the auction instantly distanced themselves. These independent warriors were as smart as rabbits. It seemed that it would be difficult to be kind.
But who on earth was this young man?
Relying on his cultivation of the God Realm, he intended to behave atrociously in the Sword Village. He would probably end up in a miserable state …
Most of the independent warriors looked at Luo Zheng and thought so in their hearts.
These independent warriors were all swordsmen. Some of them even went bankrupt to cross the big world. They traveled a long distance to come here because they were attracted by the Sword Village's reputation. They just wanted to improve their swordsmanship.

Chapter 1388
The people on the stage indeed did not put Luo Zheng in their eyes.
Auctioning the Nine Star Sword Token had already become something that the Sword Villa had tacitly agreed to. Zhen Yuanyu would even hand over a certain percentage of the auction proceeds.
This had already become a rule for entering the Sword Villa.
If the brat below the stage wanted to cause trouble and alarm the Sword Villa, he would probably be directly killed.
Seeing that this brat did not know anything, he was probably an independent martial artist without any backing. No one would come to ask if he killed an independent martial artist.
Mu Mingxue was somewhat regretful in her heart. It seemed that she had brought trouble to Luo Zheng again. But thinking about it, if she did not bring Luo Zheng around the market tonight, she would not be able to obtain the Nine Star Sword Token tomorrow.
At this moment, seeing the strange gazes of the Martial Warriors on the stage, her eyebrows furrowed slightly. However, Mu Mingxue did not say anything. Everything depended on Luo Zheng's plan.
"Are you willing or not?" The Martial Warriors on the stage stared at Luo Zheng with a face full of ridicule and sneered. He naturally knew that Luo Zheng was unwilling. However, these words could also disgust Luo Zheng, but he was certain that Luo Zheng would not dare to make a move here.
If Luo Zheng really had great ability and came from some great power, why would he need the Nine Star Sword Token? He directly entered the Sword Villa. The Sword Villa welcomed geniuses from great powers!
To put it bluntly, the Martial Warriors who fought for the Nine Star Sword Token every month were basically all independent Martial Warriors …
"Huff …"
Luo Zheng let out a light breath, and his gaze became colder and colder.
He did not want to disturb the Sword Villa too much. He only wanted to follow the path that Chen Huang Yijian had pointed out and improve his sword dao.
However, many things in this world were often like this. It was almost impossible for everything to go smoothly.
Today, he would probably have to replace the Sword-fighting Empyrean and teach his disciples a lesson.
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to make a move, a dense baleful aura leaked out from his body, and a slender figure rushed towards the stage …
Fighting was strictly prohibited in the city square, and very few people would break the rules here. Therefore, the mood of these martial artists in the city square was very relaxed.
As this infernal aura erupted, all the martial artists felt as if a stone was pressing down on their hearts. A stifling feeling arose spontaneously. Even those who hadn't paid attention to this side knew that a conflict had occurred here!
The slender figure was none other than Xun.
After Luo Zheng returned from the Dream Battlefield, Fumigation's reaction was a bit strange.
Other than being very happy when he first saw the Holy Spear of Slaughter, he was very quiet most of the time.
In the past, Fumigate didn't talk much, but now, Fumigate didn't have much energy.
Luo Zheng knew something was wrong when he saw Xun suddenly release such a huge amount of murderous aura …
Of course, Luo Zheng wouldn't let this group of people off for speaking rudely. He would definitely teach them a lesson.
But looking at Smoky's imposing manner, it was as if he was going to kill someone.
The three martial artists on the high platform still had mocking smiles on their faces a moment ago. They originally thought that Luo Zheng wouldn't make a move. But now, Luo Zheng indeed didn't make a move, but a figure rushed out from his body!
Feeling that astonishing murderous aura, the three martial artists knew something was wrong. Three long swords suddenly came out of their scabbards!
However, Fumigate's soul had already recovered by more than half. With the help of Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit, his strength had made a substantial leap. Now, Fumigate only lacked an outer shell. These Divine Realm martial artists couldn't resist Fumigate at all.
One of them had just pulled out his long sword when Fumigate's hand lightly brushed the edge of the sword. For some reason, that long sword automatically jumped into Fumigate's hand.
After the long sword was in her hand, Fumigate spun it in her hand and was about to cut down the throat of another martial artist!
That martial artist was just about to dodge when a red light flashed in Fumigate's eyes. A huge amount of soul pressure mixed with murderous aura erupted. That martial artist was actually unable to move as if he had been immobilized …
Just as Fumigate's sword was about to cut down without hesitation, another figure flashed by.
"Puff!" A sound was heard.
Luo Zheng sent that Divine Realm martial artist flying with a punch. He fell to the ground in the distance.
Fumigate didn't stop at all. She spun her long sword and reversed it, piercing through her ribs and stabbing at another Divine Realm martial artist behind her …
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng sent another punch. That martial artist was like a kite with a broken string. He was once again sent flying. He probably only had half of his life left.
"You!"
Fumigate's eyebrows suddenly jumped. She glared fiercely at Luo Zheng. Then, she slightly shifted her gaze and looked at the last remaining Divine Realm martial artist.
This time, Fumigate didn't make a move. Luo Zheng's figure was like a ghost. He took a step forward and sent out another punch.
"These people spoke rudely. It's enough to punish them. There's no need to take their lives," Luo Zheng looked at Fumigate and said softly.
Fumigate stared at Luo Zheng. She didn't refute Luo Zheng's words. She casually threw the long sword she snatched on the ground. It turned into bits of red light and entered Luo Zheng's body.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless expression. He was becoming more and more confused about Fumigate's thoughts.
"Young master, the sword tokens," Mu Mingxue had two nine-star sword tokens in her hand. She took advantage of the chaos just now to get these two sword tokens.
Luo Zheng disdained to use this kind of method to get the sword tokens. The problem was that Fumigate didn't plan on exchanging sword tokens for them. Apart from auctioning them, the only other method left was to snatch them.
Below the high platform, those independent martial artists were also discussing spiritedly.
They were shocked by Luo Zheng's methods …
When Fumigate appeared just now, they didn't see clearly what was going on. Now that they saw Fumigate into bits of red light and enter Luo Zheng's body, they understood that it was Luo Zheng's sword spirit.
"That sword spirit's soul power is so powerful …"
"The key is her mixed Evil Qi, right?"
"I don't know where this young man came from, but if he makes such a ruckus here, I'm afraid he will suffer a loss!"
In fact, there should be no one in the universe who didn't know the name Luo Zheng. They only needed to ponder for a moment to guess Luo Zheng's identity. After all, a human martial artist who used a woman from the Demon Night Clan as his sword spirit was very rare in the universe.
However, the universe was so vast and there were so many martial artists that it was impossible to count them. They simply didn't expect that the legendary Luo Zheng, the champion of this Dream War, would actually appear in front of them. Moreover, he was fighting over a nine-star sword token. This was too strange.
Most importantly, the Sword Villa only had one dream space, and only the core members of the Sword Villa were qualified to view it. The martial artists in this market were only disciples of the various Sword Pavilions. They naturally weren't qualified to see the picture scroll, nor had they seen Luo Zheng's appearance.
Luo Zheng grabbed the two sword tokens and nodded. Just as he was about to receive them, three people stepped on flying swords in the skies, hurtling towards him …
The surrounding cities of the Sword Villa also prohibited flying. Normally, when a martial artist flew in the skies, they couldn't fly higher than 100 feet, otherwise they would trigger a flying spell.
The three people howled through the air, clearly unaffected by the flying spell. The one in the lead roared out, "Who dares to cause trouble?"
"Divine Transformation realm." Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
Before entering the Dream War, if Luo Zheng were to encounter a Divine Transformation realm powerhouse, he would only be able to flee. But now, he should have the strength to fight a Divine Transformation realm powerhouse.
And the key was that there were three Divine Transformation realm powerhouses …
This market was originally under the jurisdiction of the Clear Breeze Sword Pavilion. It was only 4-5 miles away from the Sword Pavilion. To a martial artist, this was a distance that could be crossed with a single step.
In normal circumstances, there were very few people that dared to cause trouble here.
Of the three people that rushed over, one was the Pavilion Master of the Clear Breeze Sword Pavilion and the other two were high level figures of the Sword Villa.
The Pavilion Master of the Clear Breeze Sword Pavilion had a violent temper. As he saw the three people from his sect lying unconscious in three different places, he was immediately enraged. He unsheathed his Clear Breeze Sword and slashed out with a sharp blue sword light. This blue sword light flew a thousand feet towards Luo Zheng!
He raised his hand to kill!
As the people in the market saw this, they all cried out in alarm. They covered their heads and fled like rats.
They all knew that the Pavilion Master of the Clear Breeze Sword Pavilion had a fiery temper. If they weren't careful, they would be implicated in this mess and die in vain. It was indeed fun to watch the show, but it wasn't worth it to lose their lives.
As for the martial artists that were a bit further away, they couldn't help but sigh.
"Looking at that youth's skeletal age, he shouldn't be too old. With such strength, it's a pity …"
"Haha, did he really think that there was no price for causing trouble in the Sword Villa? If he didn't have eyes, then he deserved to die. He didn't even look at where he was. "

Chapter 1389
This blue sword light faintly had the intent to break through space!
The High Arena Master was a Divine Transformation realm powerhouse and his inner world had reached large success. As this sword slashed out, the strength contained within it caused one to feel dread.
"Humph!"
Luo Zheng lifted his hand and the Thunder Wind Divine Sword also slashed out. An unstoppable sword light brought with it a sharp cry as it collided with the blue sword light!
Chi la …
As the two sword lights collided, countless cracks appeared in the surrounding space, as if they had been bitten by a dog.
Luo Zheng had only used 80% of his strength in this sword strike and he hadn't used the Origin Returning Sword. Even so, it was enough to annihilate that blue light!
There were many martial artists in the city square that ran off to watch the show.
Although the High Arena Master had a violent temper, he hadn't come here to attack them. In order to ensure their own safety, they also wanted to see what would happen to that youth.
As a result, many of the martial artists were stunned by this sword strike.
This youth only had an early Divine Reaching realm cultivation, but with a casual sword strike, he was actually able to tie with the High Arena Master?
"How is this possible …"
"A Divine Reaching realm cultivation can easily block the High Arena Master's sword strike …"
"Could he be a proud son of heaven from some great influence?"
Luo Zheng had encountered many proud sons of heaven in the Dream Battlefield.
But, the chances of encountering a proud son of heaven in the universe were extremely low. There were 100,000 great worlds and less than 1000 proud sons of heaven. The chances of encountering a so-called 'proud son of heaven' were much lower than encountering a 'Holy Lord' of a Holy Land!
"It's impossible for him to be a proud son of heaven. A proud son of heaven needs a Nine Star Sword Token to enter the Sword Villa? I fear even the Villa Master of the Sword Villa would personally welcome him. Behind him is likely an Empyrean level powerhouse! "
"Then this fellow …"
The High Arena Master's sword strike was actually able to tie with Luo Zheng. Although he was stunned, he became even more enraged.
In truth, he already had a taboo in his heart. A Divine Reaching realm cultivation that was able to withstand his sword strike could not be underestimated. This youth needed to be at least at the level of a proud son of heaven!
What's more, Luo Zheng looked so calm that he didn't seem to be using his full strength!
However, as the owner of the Cool Breeze Martial House and this was his territory, he had lost a lot of face when his sword was blocked. Taking advantage of the anger in his chest, he did not bother to think about the identity of the other party and raised his hand, preparing to slash out a second time. This time, he would need to use his entire life's worth of cultivation.
However, just as the Cool Breeze Martial Artist raised his hand, an arm reached out from the side and grabbed him!
"Brother Qingfeng, you can't." That person was a senior deacon of the Sword Manor. His name was Han Ping, and he was in charge of communicating with the Sword Hall outside the Sword Manor.
He had paid close attention to the opening of the Illusory Battlefield this time. The three hundred people in the picture scroll were firmly imprinted in his mind. As for the top ten people, he remembered them even more clearly!
Just now, when he came over with the Master of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts Club, he felt that Luo Zheng looked familiar.
However, it was currently dark, and under the illumination of the lanterns, it was hard to distinguish clearly. However, when he saw the Thunder Wind Divine Sword in Luo Zheng's hand, he immediately remembered. When the Master of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts Club used his first sword strike, he knew that things were not going well!
In the end, when he saw that Luo Zheng had easily blocked it, he sighed in his heart. As expected of the number one genius, the main character of this generation.
The Master of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts Club was furious and wanted to slash out a second time, but Han Ping naturally stopped him …
"Brother Han Ping, why are you stopping me!" The Master of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts Club could not get out of this embarrassing situation. He could not slash out a second time, but he wanted to ask Han Ping for an explanation. As for what the explanation was, it was not important. The most important thing was that he could save some face.
"Brother Qingfeng, didn't you notice that his sword strike was very special?" Han Ping said with a faint smile.
The Master of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts Club frowned. After thinking for half a breath, his expression changed greatly!
Sword-fighting Technique …
This fellow cultivated the sword technique of the Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign!
As the former master of the Sword Villa, the Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign could be said to have used his own strength to forcefully raise the once insignificant Sword Villa by two levels, turning it into the largest power among the independent martial artists!
It was also because the Sword Villa believed in freedom and did not want to restrain independent martial artists, much less expand their territory. Therefore, they did not evolve into those large Holy Lands. However, the influence of the Sword Villa at that time was no longer comparable to that of a second-rate race in the universe!
There were countless sword techniques in the Sword Villa …
However, there was only one supreme sword technique, and that was the Sword-fighting Technique.
Unfortunately, the Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign did not pass down the Sword-fighting Technique. He only left behind a poem in the Orange Sea Realm …
In the past, the Sword Villa had sent a large number of people into the Orange Sea Realm in an attempt to unravel the secrets within, but in the end, they returned without success. Even a few years ago, when the Human Alliance had not expelled outsiders, there were still independent martial artists from the Sword Villa who were comprehending it.
However, the Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign's Sword-fighting Technique was not passed down in the end.
Because of the Illusory Battlefield, the people of the Sword Villa knew that there was someone in the universe who had almost perfectly inherited the Sword-fighting Technique. That person was naturally Luo Zheng.
However, wasn't Luo Zheng rumored to be trapped in the Heaven Region?
The Illusory Battlefield had just ended a few days ago, and he had already come to the Sword Villa? How could his speed be so fast? Even a Heavenly Sovereign would not be able to do so, right?
The Lord of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts School was also a shrewd person. Since it was Luo Zheng, this matter was easy to handle.
If he lost to an ordinary Divine Realm martial artist, then he, the Lord of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts School, would have lost face tonight.
However, Luo Zheng was different. He was already the legendary number one person in the future!
Just now, the Lord of the Cool Breeze Martial Arts School was still in a state of uncontrollable rage, but now, he laughed. "I was wondering who it was! So it's Luo Zheng Daozi who has graced our Sword Villa with his presence! However, he did not say anything in advance. Our Sword Villa has failed to welcome him! I was too impatient just now. I'm sorry! " When he said these words, there was a slight awkwardness in his eyes. It was very rare for someone of his status to apologize so cleanly.
However, the two words "Luo Zheng" were enough to alleviate this awkwardness!
Han Ping also took a step forward. His figure floated, and he arrived on the high platform. He clasped his hands towards Luo Zheng. "This one is Sword Villa's Han Ping. Daozi Luo Zheng Daozi has personally come to visit. Our Sword Villa has been rude."
Luo Zheng had actually not become a Daozi, but this was not important. Because he had defeated a Daozi, then in the eyes of others, he had the possibility of becoming a True God in the future!
"Luo Zheng?"
"The champion of the Illusory Battlefield this time!"
"No way, why did he come to our Sword Villa …"
When the originally silent martial artists in the marketplace heard the two words "Luo Zheng", they suddenly understood.
In their eyes, a person like Luo Zheng should have gone to the Demi-Immortal Clan, the Deities Without Thoughts, or the Human Alliance …
Compared to these great powers, the Sword Villa was somewhat insignificant. If there was a supreme expert like Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign in the past, it would have been a good match. Now, what could the Sword Villa have that could catch Luo Zheng's attention?
Of the three martial artists that were smashed out by Luo Zheng, two had already woken up. When they heard Han Ping's words, they almost fainted again.
These two brothers were even more furious in their hearts. Damn it, if I had said earlier that you were Luo Zheng, then I would have given you this Nine Star Sword Token.
Thinking about it, it was still not right. Since you are Luo Zheng, why do you want this Nine Star Sword Token? Just go straight to the Sword Villa, who would stop you?
The two of them cursed in their hearts, but they did not dare to show it on their faces. They only hoped that Luo Zheng would let them go. Otherwise, the Hall Master would push the responsibility onto them, and they would be finished.
There was still one martial artist who was still unconscious. That martial artist actually asked Luo Zheng to give Mu Mingxue to him. If he knew that it was Luo Zheng, he would probably faint again …
The coldness on Luo Zheng's face melted a little, but he still said sarcastically, "I have long heard of the Sword Villa's great name. It is a holy land in the hearts of independent martial artists, but I didn't know that the Sword Villa was so difficult to enter. Those who enter the Sword Villa do not rely on their comprehension of the Sword Dao or their passion for the Sword Dao, but they rely on the True Origin Stones in their pockets. Haha, I wonder how Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign would feel if he knew! In the future, when he returns and sees this, I'm afraid he will be incomparably disappointed! "
Luo Zheng's words struck a chord in the hearts of many independent martial artists present.
They had traveled a long distance to come here, relying on their dedication to the Sword Dao. They hoped to temper an even stronger Sword Intent, but in the end, they did not rely on their own strength. They still had to use Zhen Yuanyu to auction in order to be able to enter the Sword Villa. The disappointment in their hearts was hard to describe.
When Han Ping heard Luo Zheng's words, his heart suddenly lit up. He heard the meaning behind Luo Zheng's words. Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign did not fall?
As he thought this in his heart, his expression became more and more sincere. "This Nine Star Sword Token is indeed improper! I will report this matter and make adjustments. Since Daozi Luo Zheng has come to the Sword Villa, please come in for a chat! "

Chapter 1391
Bi Feiyu and the two elders behind him, as Sector Lords, understood the rules and restrictions even more than Luo Zheng.
Empyrean becoming a True God?
There was no precedent for this.
It was because of this that they found it hard to believe.
There had been Sector Lords who had become True Gods. Although the probability was small, it was still possible.
However, after Empyreans inherited the Mandate of Heaven, this possibility was erased. They would never be able to cross this Heavenly Dao.

This time, "Soft Whisper", also known as Ying, had easily crossed the Heavenly Dao's restrictions. This had already opened the eyes of everyone in the universe.
The rules were dead, but people were alive.

From the upper half of his arm, a bright blue light began to flash. It was a sword imprint!

World Lords were all characters that could dominate a large world. Why were they all like this?
Mu Mingxue had ascended to the Upper Realm not long ago and spent most of her time in the Immortal Mansion. After settling Luo Zheng down, Bi Feiyu and the two old men moved to another large hall. This was the Sword Manor's Sword Heart Pavilion. The Elders that arrived first saw Bi Feiyu gather everyone here in such a hurry and thought something important had happened. They hurriedly asked, but Bi Feiyu only shook his head and didn't answer.
After the 27 Sword Holding Hall Elders had all gathered, Bi Feiyu said, "Gathering all of you here in the middle of the night, there is naturally something that must be discussed."
The 27 Sword Holding Elders had worried looks on their faces. Looking at this situation, they suspected that the great crisis of the universe had already erupted, and the location of the eruption was the Sword Manor? If so, then they were far too unlucky …

These words immediately caused everyone to fall into silence. For a moment, they didn't know how to express their opinions …
This Wen Qing sword heart was the ultimate inheritance of the Sword Villa!
According to the arrangements of the Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign back then, if he were to fall, the next Villa Master would have to bring along the Wen Qing sword heart in order to open the mountain gate of the Killing Sword Mountain. Only then would he be able to properly inherit the Sword Villa and become the Villa Master of the Sword Villa.
Bi Feiyu did not have the Wen Qing sword heart, he was only the acting Villa Master.
But all these years, the Wen Qing sword heart had never appeared, and everyone had chosen to forget about it. Then, Bi Feiyu was their Villa Master, and no one would doubt it!
After all, Bi Feiyu had contributed a lot to the development of the Sword Villa over the years.
Suddenly, a person appeared, and even brought along the Wen Qing sword heart. Everyone felt extremely strange in their hearts.
The most important thing was not this problem.
Because the person who came was Luo Zheng.
If an ordinary nameless person wanted to accept the inheritance of the Sword Villa with a Wen Qing Sword Heart, the people of the Sword Holding Hall would have clamored and attacked him directly. At the very least, they would expel him or even kill him …
However, when it came to Luo Zheng, the problem was completely different.
When the great world descended, no one would be able to escape the impending great calamity.
These people who competed in the Illusory Battlefield would eventually bear heavy responsibilities and become indispensable characters in helping the world through this crisis.
This time limit could be ten years, or a hundred years. To the martial artists in the world, it was actually a very short period of time, and it would arrive very quickly.

Chapter 1393
Walking all the way here, Luo Zheng also did not understand.
They should be heading to the Killing Sword Mountain, so why did they walk into this cave?
Could it be that the Killing Sword Mountain was in this cave?
The huge door in front of him was greenish-gray, and the surface of the door was engraved with all kinds of exquisite patterns. A thin layer of dust had accumulated in the grooves of these patterns, as if no one had been here for a long time.
Although Bi Feiyu had never entered this huge door, he was very familiar with the world outside.
With a wave of his hand, a gust of wind whistled and swept away all the dust!
After the dust dissipated, the patterns on the huge door were completely revealed. Luo Zheng saw that there was a huge spider carved on the door. The spider seemed to have thousands of legs, and the patterns that Luo Zheng had seen before were the patterns on the spider's legs.
Seeing the patterns on the carvings, Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of surprise.
From the moment they entered the cave, the surroundings were filled with a strange aura, and the huge door revealed a strange evil aura.
The way of the sword was orthodox. Normally, people who cultivated the way of the sword would rarely walk the path of evil. That would affect their sword heart.
Yi Jian Tianzun was probably the strongest swordsman in the universe, so why would he set up such a door?
Luo Zheng revealed a strange feeling in his heart, but gradually, he had a guess.
The Killing Sword Mountain originally did not follow the path of a gentleman. There should be a huge secret hidden within. However, according to the current clues, Luo Zheng did not know anything about these secrets.
From Luo Zheng's point of view, this secret might have a lot to do with Yi Jian Tianzun being able to become a True God. It might even be related to the things that Qing Yu grasped …

Luo Zheng didn't dodge. It was impossible for this bronze relief to bite through his skin. However, just as this thought appeared in his mind, a sharp pain came from his arm …
There seemed to be an extremely sharp object piercing Luo Zheng's arm, but the mouth was already closed, so Luo Zheng couldn't see it clearly.

Just as he was thinking about what it was, the huge bronze door of the folding fan made a "kacha" sound, and the door slowly retreated to both sides. Then, a strong murderous aura gushed out from the Slaughter Sword Mountain, as if there was a ferocious and evil thing lurking inside.
Everyone standing outside the door was affected by the evil spirit, especially Mu Mingxue. Her face was pale, and she was under great pressure.
"Coo, coo …"

"This door is open, so we can only send you here." Bi Feiyu had a faint smile on his face. At this moment, a green ruyi appeared in his hand, emitting a faint light curtain. It wrapped Mu Mingxue and the people of the Sword Holding Hall in it, so that they wouldn't be affected by the evil spirit.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate too much, and only then did he rush forward and enter the huge door. The moment he entered the huge door, the door closed behind Luo Zheng. In the empty environment, the sound of the door closing was extremely ear-piercing.

Sword Pavilion, Market Square, and then he met Han Ping. This seemed to be going too smoothly.

Since the door had already closed, it would be better to complete the Sword Fighting Heavenly Sovereign's trial as soon as possible. He hoped that his Emotion Severing Divine Dao would reach perfection. The last fragment to fill the gap was in this sword mountain. He had no room to retreat.

The closer he got to the Slaughter Sword Mountain, the more Luo Zheng felt that something was wrong. The mountain was still the same mountain, but it was missing a crucial detail.
"The sword … is gone …"
The first time Luo Zheng entered the Slaughter Sword Mountain was when he sparred with Hua Tianming and comprehended the perfect sword intent.
The second time, it was when Chen Huang set up an illusion with his sword.
However, the two times he entered, the surrounding swords of the Slaughter Sword Mountain were densely packed, from the foot of the mountain all the way to the top of the mountain. But now, Luo Zheng didn't see a single sword.
With a face full of doubt, Luo Zheng continued to fly towards the Slaughter Sword Mountain. With his speed, this distance was covered in an instant. After circling around the foot of the mountain, Luo Zheng followed the mountain ridge all the way up, and quickly reached the top of the mountain.
From beginning to end, Luo Zheng didn't notice anything strange. It was as if in this cave, other than this mountain, there was nothing else …
Then was this still the Slaughter Sword Mountain?
Since the Sword Fighting Heavenly Sovereign had asked him to come here to temper his own way of the sword and make up for the last flaw of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, there must be something here that he didn't know about.
Floating on top of the mountain, Luo Zheng thought for a moment. Looking at the main body of the Slaughter Sword Mountain, his eyebrows suddenly raised.
"Baleful Qi!"
Luo Zheng didn't find anything on the mountain, but there was a strong baleful Qi in the cave. The source of this baleful Qi was floating out from the body of the Slaughter Sword Mountain!
He wanted to enter the interior of the Slaughter Sword Mountain?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all …
"Swish swish swish!"
The three sword beams intersected with each other, drawing a triangle on the top of the mountain. He didn't use much strength, and the sword beams only reached a distance of three meters below the top of the mountain.
Then, Luo Zheng's sword pierced in from the side. With a slight pry, he pried out a piece of the mountain rock on the top of the mountain …
The moment it was pried out, Luo Zheng felt a majestic baleful Qi surging out from the opening he had cut out. With the naked eye, he could almost see a red mist. At the same time, Luo Zheng smelled a strange fragrance.
The fragrance was very sweet, similar to the smell of blood, but it wasn't annoying.
And after Luo Zheng pried open the mountain rock, he discovered that the soil at the bottom of the mountain rock was a bright red color, as if it was a mixture of blood and soil.

Chapter 1394
The Slaughter Sword Mountain was empty.
Luo Zheng stood at the opening and looked down.
A deep red light shone from the inside of the mountain, but he couldn't see clearly what was inside.
Then, a green luster appeared in Luo Zheng's eyes. He looked again, but he still couldn't see clearly.
Although Luo Zheng had sensed a strange aura from the moment he entered the cave, Luo Zheng naturally would not turn back now that he had come this far.
Without thinking too much, Luo Zheng stepped into the opening and started to descend from the top of the Slaughter Sword Mountain.
At the moment, Luo Zheng was also under a lot of pressure!
The Evil Spirit inside the Slaughter Sword Mountain was so dense that it was almost tangible. At first glance, it looked like a red fog. The fog was grotesquely shaped, as if all kinds of demons and ghosts were hovering around Luo Zheng. There were even strange cries coming from the fog.
When Luo Zheng listened carefully, he couldn't hear anything. It was as if those sounds were just what Luo Zheng felt in his heart.
"Whoosh …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a black shadow pass by above his head.
"What's that?"
Luo Zheng suddenly became alert. Although he could be said to be skilled and bold, he had just reached the Extreme Divine Realm, so he wasn't invincible.
While looking up, Luo Zheng also released his perception.
However, the dense Evil Spirit inside the cave affected Luo Zheng's perception. Maybe he was immersed in the Evil Spirit, which made him hallucinate.
The Evil Spirit here was too dense. If he stayed here for too long, there might be unpredictable consequences.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng sped up his diving speed. Soon, he landed on the ground. The Slaughter Sword Mountain was in the middle of a mountain, and Luo Zheng was in the middle of the mountain. It was easy to imagine the oppressive feeling it gave off.
Just as he descended to the ground, he heard a panting sound by his ear.
"Huff, huff …"
Luo Zheng suddenly turned around, but there was nothing behind him. It was still the thick blood-red Evil Qi.

Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes slightly and continued to move forward with his sword in hand. Since he had already come this far, he had no reason to retreat. Therefore, he continued to move forward with more vigilance.

Thinking about it, it didn't seem right. Purely honing the way of the sword, there was no need for Luo Zheng to enter such a strange place. The Sword-fighting Empyrean probably didn't know the meaning behind this …
As he walked forward, Luo Zheng saw a bare tombstone by the side of the road. The tombstone looked very out of place.
Luo Zheng walked forward and sized it up. There were no carvings on the tombstone, only a tombstone, and no inscriptions. Who was sleeping under the tombstone?
After confirming that this tombstone had nothing to do with his trip, Luo Zheng took a detour and continued forward. Not long after, Luo Zheng saw another tombstone. It was also bare, and there were no inscriptions on it.
As he continued forward, a third tombstone appeared!
At this moment, Luo Zheng realized that something was wrong. Before passing by the tombstone, Luo Zheng lightly tapped his finger, and a thin wind blade directly hit the top of the tombstone, leaving behind a small mark.
When the fourth tombstone appeared, Luo Zheng's gaze fell on the top of the tombstone.
"It's the same tombstone as before." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He already knew where the problem was. He had been walking in circles around this tombstone.
Martial artists who cultivated the Space Laws were very sensitive to space. Ordinary martial artists might get lost in the fog, because it was very difficult to determine one's position without a reference.
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, did not. He knew very well that he was walking in a straight line without any deviation.
But he walked in a straight line, and yet he ended up back at the starting point. This meant that something had trapped him.
"A very clever method," Luo Zheng thought to himself as he stood in front of the tombstone.
When one cultivated the Space Laws to a high level, they could connect two spaces. Luo Zheng could also do this. For example, traveling through space was the same principle …
However, when changing space, there would eventually be fluctuations of Space Laws. For example, where the space in front of him was connected to the space behind him, there would definitely be fluctuations of Space Laws. However, Luo Zheng did not feel any of these fluctuations.
Looking at it this way, either Luo Zheng had seen a ghost, or the person who used this Space Maze was extremely clever.
Four tombstones meant that Luo Zheng had walked around this damned place four times.
After standing there for a while, Luo Zheng kicked off the ground, two feet above the ground, and sped away!
"Whoosh!"
Flying close to the ground like this, his speed was extremely fast!
"The fifth tombstone …"
"The sixth tombstone …"
Every tombstone he saw was equivalent to Luo Zheng passing through a space node in front of him, and then being teleported to the back. According to his flying speed, Luo Zheng calculated that the range of this Space Maze was about eight thousand feet.
But even if he knew the range of this Space Maze, Luo Zheng would find it very difficult to get out, unless his Space Laws reached a higher level …
"The Evil Qi is getting thicker and thicker, haven't you noticed?"
Xun's voice suddenly came, and then gradually condensed in front of Luo Zheng, revealing her slender figure.
Her eyes flashed as she looked around and said, "This seems to be a trap …"
"Trap?" Luo Zheng was startled.
It wasn't that Luo Zheng didn't have this guess, but he felt that there was no need for Sword-fighting Heavenly Sovereign to trick him here and then trap him here.
Xun stared at Luo Zheng and sneered. She knew what Luo Zheng was thinking, but she pointed upwards and said, "If you don't believe me, take a look for yourself."
Following the direction that Xun was pointing at, Luo Zheng raised his head and saw a blood-red face staring at him with a pair of empty eyes. That pair of empty eyes didn't have the slightest bit of life, and that kind of expression wasn't an expression that a human should have.
It wasn't that Luo Zheng had never seen those vengeful spirits before, but he had never felt such a creepy feeling.
Almost instantly, Luo Zheng's Thunder Wind Ghost Sword slashed out. No matter what the hell it was, he would kill it first …
"Whoosh!"
Perhaps Luo Zheng really couldn't hold it in any longer. When this sword slashed out, he used Yi Shen's Sword. Its power was also extremely strong!
If this sword beam pierced out, it would probably directly split apart the Slaughter Sword Mountain. It might even pierce through the mountain outside of the Slaughter Sword Mountain!
However, Luo Zheng still underestimated this spatial maze. Or it could be said that Luo Zheng underestimated the Slaughter Sword Mountain.
If it was so easy to destroy, with just that bronze door, who could this Holy Land stop? Any Divine Realm martial artist could force their way in. In reality, the secrets of the Slaughter Sword Mountain far exceeded Luo Zheng's imagination.
The sword light disappeared into the distance without a sound, as if it had fallen into an endless space and disappeared for no reason.
As for that blood-red face, it was indeed cut in half by Luo Zheng's sword. However, it quickly merged back into one and slowly approached downwards.
At the same time, Luo Zheng also discovered that besides above his head, all around him were these creepy giant faces that constantly appeared. These giant faces quietly floated, approaching Luo Zheng!
"Give me the spear." Kaoru stretched out her hand.
Luo Zheng flicked his finger and the Holy Slaughter Spear flew out from his spatial ring, falling into Kaoru's hands!
"Woosh!"
"One Thought Without Turning!"
Kaoru's spear was even fiercer, but it didn't seem to be of much use. Under this spear, it only pierced a large hole in one of the giant faces. However, that giant face didn't suffer any damage!
"Use thunder." Kaoru decisively said.
As her voice fell, a silver arc as thick as a wrist lashed out from the Thunder Wind Ghost Sword!
"Pa!"

Chapter 1395
This roar reverberated in the hollow Mountain of Killing Swords, as if it was a malicious spirit.
Speaking of which, these giant faces were no different from malicious spirits.
If he was alone, he would definitely be scared out of his wits.
But at this moment, with Xun by his side, Luo Zheng's heart calmed down a lot. No matter what, two people were always better than one, even though Xun was just a soul.
Since the lightning was effective against these giant faces, Luo Zheng did not hold back.
There were three types of sword skills in the Dark Thunder Wind Sword. Aside from the Dark Thunder Wind Sword, there were also the Thunder Wind Sword and the Wind Blade.
Chaotic energy was continuously poured into it, and the silver snakes in the air turned into countless whip shadows, crazily whipping those giant faces.
"Pa pa pa pa …"
The giant face closest to Luo Zheng suffered the densest lightning strikes, and finally scattered into a cloud of baleful energy.
This scene calmed Luo Zheng even more.
He was afraid that these fellows would not suffer anything. Since they were afraid of lightning, everything was easy.
These giant faces floated very slowly. Although there were many of them, Luo Zheng had plenty of time.
After the time it took to brew a cup of tea, the giant faces around Luo Zheng were scattered …
"Phew …" Luo Zheng exhaled, and a trace of relaxation appeared on his face. "Now, let's focus on finding a way out of here."
As soon as his voice fell, he suddenly felt that there was something wrong with Xun's gaze. Her gaze did not seem to be looking at him, but behind him!
"Cough cough cough cough …"
Luo Zheng looked back and saw a giant head opening its mouth to bite him!
The face of this head was similar to those giant faces, but in comparison, those giant faces were like a mask for this head, as if this fellow was the real owner.
"Whoosh!"
Before that mouth could bite him, Luo Zheng's figure floated, and he dodged with the Eight Winding Smoke. As he retreated, he brewed a long lightning whip from the Dark Thunder Wind Sword, and continued to lash at the head.
Indeed, being stared at by those strange eyes was not something to be happy about.
Luo Zheng originally thought that the lightning would work as usual, but the tenacity of this brain seemed to be beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. Or rather, this thing was not afraid of lightning at all …
Not only was it immune to lightning, its speed was also much faster than the giant faces from before!
"Go!"
Smoke stabbed out with her spear, and the huge spear shadow directly pierced through the head, creating a big hole in it.
The Holy Spear was invincible, and the key was that this thing could not be pierced through at all. After a large hole appeared in its head, this fellow's facial features became even more distorted, making it even more unbearable to look at.
As Luo Zheng moved forward rapidly, he saw another huge head on the left. Not far away, there were some red shadows floating in the red mist of Evil Qi. They were probably those huge heads.
It seemed that these things had been hiding in the killing intent of the Slaughter Sword Mountain, and now Luo Zheng had finally attracted them.
Luo Zheng couldn't figure out why these things would appear in the Massacre Sword Mountain.
Could it be that the amount of Evil Qi was too much, and in the end, it formed Evil Qi Spirits?
Originally, there were so many swords stuck in the Massacre Sword Mountain, and these people were able to place their own Evil Qi into the Massacre Sword Mountain. With the passage of time, this kind of mutation might have occurred.
Under this reasoning, it made sense, but what was going on with this tombstone? What was the matter with the space maze …
Luo Zheng's mind was in a mess. He was afraid that he had run into real trouble this time.
After circling a few times, Luo Zheng returned to his original point, and a tombstone appeared in front of him. The space maze was nothing, but the number of huge heads was increasing. They were pushing against each other, almost forming a wall, and were heading towards Luo Zheng.
This place was full of strangeness. Although Luo Zheng's body was tyrannical, when faced with these heads, his end wouldn't be good!
The key was to break the maze …
But looking at the two walls of huge heads coming towards him, Luo Zheng knew that he had no chance.
Just at this moment, a black shadow suddenly flashed by from the side!
This time, Luo Zheng saw it clearly. The black shadow looked very agile, like a black cat. But of course, ordinary cats and dogs couldn't survive here, nor could they have such amazing speed. It should be a small ferocious beast?
"Follow it!" Xun reminded him.
Luo Zheng didn't need Xun to say anything, as he had already moved.
The situation here was very bad, and he couldn't find any clues to break the space maze. Luo Zheng didn't know what the appearance of the black shadow meant, but at this moment, following it was a wise choice.
"Whoosh!"
The black shadow was moving forward, hidden in the Evil Qi. Even though Luo Zheng was chasing it with all his attention, he could only see a vague shadow.
This shadow had four very slender legs, and its running speed was almost the same as Luo Zheng's flying speed, which was close to the ground!
The most important thing was that the black shadow's running route was very strange. It didn't escape in a straight line, but in a series of twists and turns.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng used the Eight Paths of Flying Smoke, which was originally a very tricky body movement, so he could barely keep up with the black shadow.
After rushing out like this for a long time, the black shadow was still in front of him. Luo Zheng suddenly found that the scenery around him seemed to have changed. He didn't see the tombstone anymore, so he was finally sure that he had walked out of the space maze.
"I'm out!" Luo Zheng said with a smile.
"Don't be happy too early," Xun said lightly.
After finding that he had left the space maze, Luo Zheng slowed down, but at the same time, he found that the black shadow also slowed down a lot.
"Do you see anything?" Xun asked.
Luo Zheng nodded, and his face became serious again. This black shadow was also abnormal. It seemed to be deliberately guiding him.
Where does this guy want to lead me?
The situation in the Massacre Sword Mountain was so complicated that it was beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. If he was deliberately led into a desperate situation by this black shadow, Luo Zheng would be in even more trouble.
Is this black shadow really leading me forward? Where does it want to lead me to?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly stopped in his tracks. However, the moment Luo Zheng stopped, the black shadow also stopped. It was about thirty meters in front of Luo Zheng. After the black shadow stopped, it seemed to have turned its head to look at Luo Zheng.
Separated by the thick Evil Qi, Luo Zheng might not be able to see what the black shadow was, but on the head of the black shadow, there was a pair of green eyes staring back at him. This still made Luo Zheng's hair stand on end. He couldn't help but want to immediately kill the black shadow, but it was too agile!
Luo Zheng and the black shadow confronted each other for a long time. The first thing he needed to figure out was what this black shadow was!
So Luo Zheng's legs quietly began to gather strength. If he wanted to catch this thing, he had to catch it by surprise.
After moving forward slightly and finding an angle, Luo Zheng suddenly burst out his potential and rushed toward the black shadow. But the moment he rushed out, the black shadow pulled away from Luo Zheng at an incredible speed. Its reaction was too fast.
