Chapter 1396
Luo Zheng sprinted forward like an arrow leaving the bow. His speed was extraordinarily fast!
The speed of the black shadow was not slow either. Through the eyes of the evil spirit, it disappeared in an instant. Apparently, it changed direction and sped forward at almost the same time.
The distance between the two did not decrease at all.
It was as if the black shadow had also practiced the Eight-Zigzag Flying Smoke and was able to keep the distance between them at about ten zhang.
Luo Zheng knew that it was impossible. The principle of the Eight-Zigzag Flying Smoke determined that the distance could not be too long. For example, Luo Zheng was now controlling the distance at three inches. If he continued to practice, it could reach five inches or even further. However, it was impossible to reach ten zhang.
As the black shadow chased after the black shadow one after the other, the surrounding landscape also changed.
Small circular holes appeared on the ground. These holes were about one meter wide, and baleful aura constantly spewed out from them. They were like geysers, but what was spewing out was not spring water, but baleful aura.
Because of this, the evil spirit became more and more intense. He could only see what was in front of him!
In this environment, Luo Zheng could not lock onto the black shadow at all. Soon, he was completely lost …
"I can't find it." Luo Zheng stopped.
Xun floated behind Luo Zheng. She looked around and said in shock, "I didn't expect there to be such a strange scene in the Killing Swords Mountain. How did it form?"
Xun's knowledge was definitely more profound than Luo Zheng's. Seeing the scene in front of her, she was also stunned.
"Pop, pop, pop …"
Clouds of evil spirit were like smoke rings being spat out by the giant mouth of a monster.
Having been in the Killing Swords Mountain for too long, Luo Zheng was infected by the evil spirit. He also felt restless.
"This is still not right. Is this evil spirit coming from the depths of the ground?" Unable to find the black shadow, Luo Zheng landed next to a small hole. Taking advantage of the small hole to rest, he stretched out his head and looked down.
It would have been better if he hadn't looked, but when he did, Luo Zheng saw a face.
To be precise, it was not a human face. It was like the face of a demon from the abyss. A pair of fangs covered the upper lip.
The eyes on that face were tightly shut, and it was motionless like a dead object.
Not long after, it opened its mouth!
The moment he opened his mouth, Luo Zheng retracted his head. Then, a thick column of murderous Qi gushed out of the hole.
"What did you see?" Xun was puzzled.
Luo Zheng pointed at the entrance of the cave with a strange expression. After the air column stopped, Xun also looked at the entrance of the cave.
At first glance, Xun's expression changed drastically. She was a Sword Spirit, so naturally, she would not be as pale as a real person, but Luo Zheng could read the fear in her eyes.
This kind of expression rarely appeared on Xun's face. Her Dao of Slaughter was extremely decisive, enough to resist any fear in her heart. However, at this moment, Luo Zheng knew that Xun was a little scared …
"How is this possible …" Xun murmured, and then glanced around. "Are these things all in these small holes?"
Luo Zheng also glanced at another small hole. "It should be. What are these things?"
Xun thought for a while, then pursed her lips and shook her head. "I've only seen them once, and there should only be one of them. Why are there so many buried at the bottom of the Slaughter Sword Mountain … Is it related to the catastrophe of the world …"
"What on earth are they?" Luo Zheng was very curious.
"I can't tell you now." Xun stared at Luo Zheng and continued shaking her head.
Xun was a straightforward person. Since the first day she spoke, she had been very frank with Luo Zheng, without any concealment.
"What are these things related to? You can't tell me? " Luo Zheng stared at Xun in a daze. He also felt that after he returned from the Dream Battlefield, Xun's temperament seemed to have changed. She became more real, but at the same time, she became more indifferent to Luo Zheng …
"Let's keep moving forward." Xun also knew what Luo Zheng was thinking, but she didn't want to explain.
Luo Zheng had no choice but to continue exploring this area. Because he had to avoid the Evil Qi columns gushing out from the small holes, Luo Zheng could only slow down. He bypassed the Evil Qi columns one by one, and moved forward at a much slower speed.
After moving forward like this for a while, Luo Zheng finally found something serious in the hinterland of the Slaughter Sword Mountain.
It was a cyan palace …
At the entrance of the palace, there were two strange sculptures. They looked like two goats, but the goats' heads were carved extremely ferocious. Luo Zheng didn't know which culture they belonged to, but they were definitely not human.
Walking to the front of the sculptures, Luo Zheng looked at them. He stretched out his fingers and gently rubbed them. He found that the sculptures were very exquisite, but he didn't know what material they were made of. They had been standing here for many years without any signs of decay.
Looking up the stairs, the palace was pitch-black, like a monster's mouth. It was wide open, and quietly waiting for Luo Zheng.
Clenching the Thunder and Wind Divine Sword in his hand, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate.
All the fear came from his state of mind. Since he had already found this place, Luo Zheng had no reason to turn back. Even if it was a dragon's pool or a tiger's den, he had to go.
After Luo Zheng and Xun entered, a figure slowly appeared outside the palace. At the feet of this figure, there was a black shadow crawling on the ground. A pair of green eyes were very eye-catching in the darkness.
The figure appeared for a very short time. After noticing that Luo Zheng had entered the palace, it hid in a corner.
Luo Zheng walked around the palace. The evil aura here seemed to have lessened a lot. The restlessness that was originally affected by the evil aura also weakened a lot.
Although the evil aura had lessened, Luo Zheng found that the palace was dark. Even if he used the Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes, it didn't help much. With no other choice, Luo Zheng could only take out a Night Pearl for illumination.
"The Law of Darkness is scattered here, preventing the transmission of light," Xun reminded him from behind.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. Holding the Night Pearl, he approached the side of the palace. The light reflected on the wall, and a huge mural appeared.
This mural was also carved. It seemed to be made of the same material as the "goat" sculpture outside. It was unknown how many years it had been there, but it was still vivid and lifelike …
"This is …"
Seeing the contents of the mural, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
There were many strange creatures carved on the mural. There were big and small creatures, but Luo Zheng couldn't recognize most of them. However, he recognized the biggest one!
Luo Zheng had seen this giant beast when he entered the Scarlet Castle in the second stage. It was the huge statue of the ferocious beast at the entrance of the castle. However, when the statue crawled at the entrance of the castle, it gave many people a great sense of oppression.
On the mural, the giant beast was like a big ship. It opened its mouth and made a swallowing motion, wanting to swallow the countless creatures above.
Luo Zheng looked at the mural. As he walked forward, a second mural appeared.
On the second mural, the creatures were resisting with all their strength, as if they wanted to kill the giant beast. On the third mural, Luo Zheng only saw the giant beast. The other creatures had all disappeared.
This mural wasn't difficult to understand.
It seemed that many creatures resisted the giant beast, but in the end, they failed and were recorded on the mural.
From the lifelike carvings of the giant beast, those creatures should also be very realistic. However, when Luo Zheng looked carefully, he didn't find a single human.
In other words, there wasn't a single humanoid creature.
Most of the intelligent creatures in the universe had a deep relationship with the human race. There were the demon race, the demon race, the human race, the giant race, and so on.
They were nothing more than four limbs, a head, and a torso.
However, the creatures in the mural had very strange appearances. They were even stranger than some ferocious beasts.
This palace wasn't built by humans. Why was it hidden here?
Luo Zheng found that after entering the Slaughtering Swords Mountain, he couldn't explain a single thing he saw. Everything was strange.
Xun clearly knew something, but she chose not to answer.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng stopped in the darkness and communicated with Qinglong in his mind.
"Qinglong, do you know what this is?"

Chapter 1397
Qinglong didn't respond to Luo Zheng. He still kept silent.
Luo Zheng was a bit depressed.
What on earth were these things related to?
Since he couldn't find the answer, Luo Zheng decided to explore by himself.
Therefore, he continued forward with the luminous pearl. Except for the wall paintings, the rest of the palace was empty.
Soon, he came to the back of the palace and stepped into another room. As soon as he stepped in, he seemed to step on something. There came a cracking sound.
"Bang …"
Immediately, a cloud of dust flew up.
Luo Zheng lowered the luminous pearl and found that he had stepped on a skeleton, a human skeleton.
These skeletons had been lying here for so many years that they had already become brittle. With one step, they turned into powder.
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered under the illumination of the light as he continued to move forward along the skeletal remains …
There was more than one skeleton here!
These skeletons were piled up in disorder, but most of them were intact, which meant that these people's corpses were intact before they died.
Or rather, all of them had died here …
The time it took for skeletons to decay was determined by the cultivation of the warriors.
To be able to enter the Slaughter Sword Mountain, the cultivations of the warriors were definitely not low. Ordinary warriors simply could not withstand the destruction of the Slaughter Qi, and would go crazy and die on the way.
Powerful warriors above the Spirit Sea Realm could turn their skeletons into Bone Jade after death, which could be stored for a long time.
However, Bone Jade was not indestructible. After a long time, the spirituality in the Bone Jade would be lost and it would also be catalyzed. It was just that this process took a long time. According to the cultivation of the warriors, it would take tens of thousands of years or even hundreds of thousands of years.
That was to say, these skeletons had been lying here for a very long time.
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng didn't want to step on these skeletons. He floated two inches above the ground and floated in the room.
The room at the back of the palace was not big. It was only fifty or sixty feet in both length and width. Luo Zheng circled around and found that this room didn't even have a window. Besides these skeletons, there seemed to be nothing here.
There was nothing meaningful here.
Luo Zheng's face was once again filled with confusion.
It was not easy for him to enter the Slaughter Sword Mountain. He did not want to return empty-handed.
This was completely different from Luo Zheng's previous judgment. Originally, he thought that there was a trial waiting for him to open in the Massacre Sword Mountain. Then, he would comprehend the most important part of the Severing Emotions Divine Way and become a Dao Child. Everything was so smooth and perfect.
But now, the situation was completely different from what he had imagined.
At least until now, Luo Zheng had not gained anything from it.
"The center, the center of those skeletons." Xun's figure floated gently, and the Holy Spear of Slaughter in her hand pointed downwards.
Only then did Luo Zheng see that there seemed to be a disc under these skeletons. The color of the disc was almost the same as the skeletons below. Although Luo Zheng had been searching carefully, he had accidentally missed this place.

"You're welcome," Kaoru replied.
Hearing Xun's answer, Luo Zheng's heart slightly pounded. This feeling of alienation made Luo Zheng very uncomfortable.

It was difficult for her to communicate with others in the Immortal Mansion, and Luo Zheng had the authority to enter and exit the Immortal Mansion.
Mu Mingxue was very simple, and Kaoru would not talk to her about anything.
Shenzi Shenzi was immersed in refining, and probably would not even leave the refining workshop.
Qinglong?
When Luo Zheng entered the Illusory Battlefield and was locked in that light, Kaoru was able to communicate with Qinglong.
Thinking of Qinglong's silence just now, could it be that Qinglong had said something to Kaoru? Both Qinglong and Kaoru were people that Luo Zheng absolutely trusted, but it seemed that both of them were hiding something from him …
With so many doubts in his head, Luo Zheng was too lazy to pay attention to them. No matter what was under the room, he would go in and take a look first.
Luo Zheng stepped on the disk, and a "dong" sound echoed. The bottom of the disk was indeed empty!

Then he threw the Luminous Pearl in his hand into the dark room below.
A very strange scene appeared. The Luminous Pearl, which was originally flickering with light, instantly disappeared from Luo Zheng's eyes.
This Luminous Pearl could emit light on its own, and it lasted for a long time. After throwing it in, Luo Zheng heard a "bang" sound, presumably it hit the floor below.
The Luminous Pearl did not disappear, but Luo Zheng could no longer see it.
This meant that there was a strong law of darkness in the dark room below, which completely blocked all the light.
Luo Zheng tried to use his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes to look down, but it was still dark, and he could not see anything.
Xun stood beside Luo Zheng, and did not give him any advice.
Luo Zheng gritted his teeth, turned over, and sank into the dark room …
The moment he entered, Luo Zheng could not see anything at all, not even his five fingers. When he raised his head, he could not see the faint light that originally existed above his head. In this absolute darkness, Luo Zheng was abnormally vigilant!
The Qi of Primal Chaos had already been poured into the Thunder and Wind Ghost Sword. As long as there was any movement around him, he would immediately launch a counterattack.
But he stood in place for a while, and there was no movement around him. Other than the sound of his own light breathing, the surroundings were silent.
"Xun?"
Luo Zheng called out.
"I'm here." Xun had obviously followed Luo Zheng down, and from the sound of her voice, she should be behind Luo Zheng.
"Can you see anything?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I can't see, but there must be something releasing a strong law of darkness here," Xun replied from behind.
Luo Zheng nodded, and realized that Xun could not see him, so he made a sound of acknowledgment, and slowly moved forward in the darkness …
Enemies that could not be seen were the most troublesome. Luo Zheng now understood this principle. From the moment he entered Sword Mountain, Luo Zheng had always felt that something was not right here. There was always something following him, but from beginning to end, Luo Zheng had never seen the true face of that thing.
In this dark room, he walked a distance of thirteen steps, and Luo Zheng firmly remembered every step.
If there was trouble, he could return to the disc in the shortest time possible and leave this dark room. Of course, if the situation was critical, he was afraid that he would directly break open this dark room.

After walking thirty steps in this dark room, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a "zi zi zi" sound.
It was the sound of something scratching on the ground with its claws.
"Something is coming." In the darkness, Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes slightly, and finally closed them completely. Anyway, he could not see anything, and opening his eyes would create an illusion.
With his eyes closed, Luo Zheng released his perception to feel the movement outside.
Using his perception, Luo Zheng could also draw the surrounding scene in his mind, but as soon as he released his perception, it seemed to hit an invisible force and was immediately bounced back …
"My perception is also blocked!"
Luo Zheng knew that this was bad, and then he heard the "zi zi" sound become louder and louder. That thing should be getting closer and closer to him!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"

The sword light should have been incomparably dazzling, but at this moment, all the light was immersed in the law of darkness, and did not emit the slightest bit of light.
What was even worse was that at the instant he slashed out with his sword, he heard a few clear and melodious sounds coming from his sword!
"Ding ding ding ding …"
Along with the crisp sound, several extremely sharp auras shot past him from his front, back, left, and right.
Rebound!
What were the walls of this dark room made of?
Luo Zheng could not see clearly, but he knew that if the sword light he stabbed out were to hit him, it would definitely cause a lot of trouble, and might even take his life. What was worse was the sword light he stabbed out.
In a panic, Luo Zheng activated the Eight Zigzags Flying Smoke and stood quietly in place.
If he dodged at will, it would only be more dangerous if he could not see clearly.
Feeling the fluctuations of energy around him, Luo Zheng was like a lone boat drifting in the water, and could avoid the path of the sword light in advance.
But this kind of dodging also had a limit. If the sword light went straight for Luo Zheng's face, with Luo Zheng's current stage of cultivating the Eight Zigzags Flying Smoke, he would not be able to completely avoid it, and would be able to break through the three-inch boundary in an instant.

Chapter 1398
The situation in the dark room suddenly became chaotic.

The lightning on the Dark Thunder Wind Divine Sword had just begun to brew when it was pushed away by something. With a clang, the sword fell to the ground!
Soon after, the surrounding dark laws dissipated, and Luo Zheng discovered that he was lying on a huge spider web.
The web was bright gold, as if gold had been pulled into golden threads. Many of the threads were wrapped around his body, firmly binding him to the web.
Not far away, there was a hexagonal seal, and Xun was sitting in the middle of the seal, hugging her Sacred Spear!
At this moment, Luo Zheng realized that he had fallen into a trap.
Luo Zheng suddenly twisted his body, trying to break free from the golden spider web. However, his strength was not effective against this kind of flexible spider web. Of course, it could also be that Luo Zheng's strength was not enough. His current strength was still top-notch among those of the same level, but it might not be enough to face some true powerhouses.
As he continued to struggle, the golden spider web increased in number, binding Luo Zheng tighter and tighter.
Xun hugged the Sacred Spear of Slaughter, and her expression was very calm. That calmness even made Luo Zheng feel a little strange.

At this moment, Bi Feiyu seemed to have changed into a different person. When he heard Luo Zheng's voice, he only chuckled and did not answer.

A flame of anger rose in Luo Zheng's heart, and a huge aura burst out of his body.
Luo Zheng had his guard up against the Sword Manor. Before entering the Killing Sword Mountain, Luo Zheng had hesitated to bring Mu Mingxue in. However, Bi Feiyu had repeatedly emphasized that only he could enter the Killing Sword Mountain, so he had left Mu Mingxue outside.
"Don't worry, he's probably lying to you," Xun said calmly. "He won't kill Mu Mingxue in front of his subordinates. Besides, Mu Mingxue is not important. She's too easy to deal with."
Hearing Xun's words, Luo Zheng's expression calmed down a little.

Whether it was Luo Zheng or Xuanyuan Chenfeng, these Dao children were indeed outstanding, but in the eyes of these Realm Masters who had lived for countless years, they were undoubtedly just children. They were too immature.
Xun replied indifferently, "You don't have such a fate. Let alone you, even if you were a Celestial Venerable, you still wouldn't have such a fate."

"Besides, even if I don't have such a fate, I should have it in a while," Bi Feiyu added.

As he was busy, Bi Feiyu replied, "This is an obvious thing. There are some things that you should not complete."

What exactly was the inheritance of the Sword-fighting Empyrean?

Even Xun's actions were very strange. There seemed to be something extremely important within.
But no matter if it was important or not, it had nothing to do with Luo Zheng's goal. His goal was only to complete his Emotion Severing Divine Dao.

"Sou!"
Just as Xun's voice fell, an extremely sharp sword beam shot out from Bi Feiyu's hand. He was actually using his finger as a sword, but the power was extremely terrifying!
As expected of a great realm lord.
If this sword beam landed on Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng would definitely have no way to survive.
But this sword beam did not slash towards Luo Zheng. Instead, it went straight for Xun who was trapped in the hexagon seal!
The sword beam slashed across, and Xun's arm was instantly cut off from the base. It turned into specks of red light, and then condensed back into an arm …
Because Xun was a sword spirit body, she did not suffer too much damage, but Xun's face turned ugly.
As long as Bi Feiyu wanted to, he could completely destroy Xun's soul. After Xun's soul recovered, her strength increased greatly, but she was still unable to fight against a realm lord. Moreover, Bi Feiyu had the strength of a great realm lord.
Bi Feiyu laughed coldly, "Whether I have it or not, I will only know after I try. Moreover, for this day, I have studied this broken place for countless years! In my arrangements, there are no loopholes, and there is no reason for failure! "
Xun did not speak anymore, and only coldly looked at Bi Feiyu.
Luo Zheng also understood that Bi Feiyu was very clear that the inheritance of the Sword-fighting Sky Sovereign was at the bottom of the Killing Sword Mountain. At the same time, it was not the first time he entered the Killing Sword Mountain. Clearly, he had already disobeyed Chen Huang Yi Jian's orders.
His scheme was Chen Huang Yi Jian's inheritance.
But from the looks of it, this inheritance was definitely not a trial. Otherwise, Bi Feiyu would not have placed so much importance on it …
Then what was it?
Xun seemed to know a little, but she refused to say anything.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a depressed expression. He could only be trapped here and watch Bi Feiyu busying himself up and down.
"Sizzle, sizzle, sizzle …"
When Bi Feiyu activated another mechanism, the crawling sound rang out again. However, this time, the crawling sound was even more concentrated, as if there were countless small things crawling forward quickly from all directions.
Very quickly, Luo Zheng saw a shocking scene.
From the cracks in the surrounding space, a large number of small spiders appeared. Those small spiders were dark red in color. It was like a sea of spiders, spreading towards Luo Zheng.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1400
Only an Empyrean's body appeared in front of him. To Bi Feiyu, it did not cause any trouble.
Although his expression was puzzled, it was filled with certainty. After planning for so long, Bi Feiyu had a strong determination.
Bi Feiyu was one of the first people to follow the Sword-fighting Empyrean.
The origin of the Sword-fighting Empyrean was a mystery, and the process of becoming an Empyrean was also a mystery.
Logically speaking, it was impossible for a power like the Sword Villa to give birth to an Empyrean.
The reason was simple.
The Crown of Destiny in the universe was monopolized by those great powers.
Several factions of the Demon Night Clan, Demon Clan, Gods Without Thoughts, Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region …
The difference in strength between an Empyrean and a Realm Master was very large. Even if some weak powers obtained the Crown of Destiny, they would not have the time to bear the Mandate of Heaven.
In most cases, it would be directly "confiscated" by the large powers. The weak powers simply did not have the power to resist.
Therefore, once an Empyrean appeared in a certain race, it meant that this power had entered the mainstream.
The Sword Villa only became a mainstream power in the universe because Chen Huang Yijian had become an Empyrean.
At that time, a Demon Clan Empyrean had fallen in the universe. After the Crown of Destiny disappeared, it would immediately appear anywhere in the universe. The Demon Clan would definitely do their best to snatch back the new Crown of Destiny …
Almost every faction participated in the fight for the Crown of Destiny, but in the end, the Crown of Destiny was inherited by Chen Huang Yijian. No one knew what method Chen Huang Yijian used to make the various large clans stop fighting. Even the Devil Race did not attack Chen Huang Yijian. After all, it was the Devil Race's lost Crown of Destiny.
Bi Feiyu really could not think of any reason that could convince the Demon Clan, or even the many powers outside of the Demon Clan!
At that time, it was not only Bi Feiyu who was puzzled. Some of the Empyrean powerhouses in the universe who did not participate in the fight had also voiced their doubts through the roar command.
However, those who participated in the fight for the Crown of Destiny, without exception, all of them remained silent …
Bi Feiyu could not think of a reason, but he came to his own conclusion.
That reason must be very shocking, and the value of the reason was definitely greater than a Crown of Destiny. Then, in this universe, what was more valuable than the Crown of Destiny?
The Heavenly Mandate Crown was the pinnacle of the universe. Becoming a Celestial Venerable was the 128 strongest people in the universe.
Therefore, at that time, Bi Feiyu, who was only a deacon of the Sword Manor, had a seed of curiosity planted in his heart. Now, this seed had grown into a towering tree in his heart. It was a tree of greed. He had to find the answer.
Back then, before the Sword-fighting Empyrean left, he seemed to have already gained the understanding of foresight. He knew that he would fall, so he arranged his own affairs in advance and placed his inheritance at the bottom of the Killing Sword Mountain.
At that time, Bi Feiyu had already become the Vice Manor Lord and was Chen Huang Yijian's most important subordinate. That was why he was the Acting Manor Lord, waiting for the person with the Wen Qing Sword Heart to appear.
After that, Empyrean Yi Jian disappeared, becoming an eternal mystery.
From the moment Heavenly Venerable Yi Jian disappeared, Bi Feiyu had been trying to open the things left behind by Heavenly Venerable Yi Jian.
The first door was not difficult to enter, or rather, the Sword-fighting Empyrean did not encrypt it in front of the first door. In a month's time, Bi Feiyu had entered the Killing Sword Mountain.
Originally, he thought that everything would be smooth sailing, but he was stuck in front of the second door.
He was unable to destroy the door guarded by the Thousand Feet Hidden Spider. This kind of spider came from hell, and that was a time that Bi Feiyu himself had never been to.
He tried to step in from the side of the door, but he was still unable to succeed. The space around the door was completely folded, and that was an extremely brilliant method. There were no loopholes, and Bi Feiyu was unable to enter.
Therefore, he could only choose to wait. In the end, he waited for this day.
When he saw the Sword-fighting Empyrean, he was indeed very confused and nervous, but he was even more excited. The mystery had finally been revealed.
The Thousand Feet Hidden Spider threw Luo Zheng in front of the Sword-fighting Empyrean, and then quietly disappeared.
Luo Zheng crawled up from the ground, his face was also filled with a strange expression, he had no idea what he should do. Looking at Chen Huang Yijian's corpse, Luo Zheng's face was also filled with confusion, it couldn't be that he had to kneel down and take him as his master?
However, just as Luo Zheng moved, Chen Huang Yijian's corpse suddenly moved.
With this movement, Luo Zheng immediately stopped in his tracks. Even if an Empyrean expert was dead, he could not be underestimated. If Chen Huang Yijian had set up some kind of trap, it was enough for Luo Zheng to die a hundred times.
Not only Luo Zheng, but Bi Feiyu also trembled as he looked around vigilantly.
Chen Huang Yijian's corpse slowly raised its arm, extended its gray finger, and pointed behind Luo Zheng.
The mountain rocks behind Luo Zheng trembled, and a jade-green pool appeared. A faint light appeared in the pool, and it was a broken sword. It could also be said to be a sword hilt.
There was not much left of the broken sword on the sword hilt, and it was less than half an inch tall. This broken sword looked very strange, as if it was not broken by someone. More than half of the sword's body had melted away.
There was still a faint light floating above the sword's body, and it seemed to be melting down, but the melting speed was not fast.
"What is this?" Luo Zheng was stunned. He could not understand the meaning of this broken sword appearing here.
Bi Feiyu's eyes flickered, and the long sword was already pointed at Luo Zheng. "Take out this broken sword!" Bi Feiyu was very vigilant. This place was already strange enough, and this broken sword would not be easy to obtain.
Luo Zheng's brows furrowed slightly. There seemed to be nothing wrong with the clear water in the pool.
However, just as Luo Zheng's finger touched the surface of the pool, there was a sizzling sound, and a heart-wrenching pain. Half of Luo Zheng's finger was already pitch-black …
"This is … corrosion!" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
Bi Feiyu's face also sank. "Tai Qing Water!"
"Luo Zheng, don't use your hands. This thing can corrode everything!" Seeing this scene, Xun also frowned. Luo Zheng was not afraid of strong poison, but this Tai Qing Water was not poisonous. It only had a strong corrosive power …
Seeing Bi Feiyu's long sword pointing at him, Luo Zheng took out an immortal weapon and stabbed it into the pool of water, trying to fish out the broken sword.
However, just as the broken sword entered the Tai Qing Water, potholes appeared on the sword's body. When the immortal weapon was completely submerged, it directly disappeared in the Tai Qing Water. The corrosive power of this pool of clear water was beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
"I don't have much patience. Just use your hands to take it out." Bi Feiyu sneered. With a sudden shake of his long sword, his shoulder directly pierced through Luo Zheng's shoulder. Immediately, blood blossomed.
Facing Bi Feiyu, Luo Zheng had no room to resist. However, looking at the Tai Qing Water, a trace of hesitation appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
"Humph." Bi Feiyu did not give Luo Zheng any time to think. He backhanded his sword and directly hit Xun.
Xun's sword spirit body was instantly scattered and turned into little red lights. After a long time, it condensed back into a human form. Even with the sword spirit body, Luo Zheng could feel that Xun was seriously injured. Bi Feiyu's sword seemed to contain the power to destroy the soul.
Luo Zheng's face sank. He clenched his teeth and could only reach down with his hands!
"Sizzle …"
The moment his hands reached down, the flesh on Luo Zheng's hands disappeared at a visible speed. The corrosive power of this Tai Qing Water could be seen at a glance!
He felt as if his hands were being burned by a raging fire, and an intense pain was transmitted from his hands.
When Luo Zheng's hands grabbed the short sword, the flesh on his hands had completely disappeared, leaving only the bones …
"Swish!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng grabbed the short sword and threw it out of the pool!
"Whoosh!"
Before Luo Zheng could even react, Bi Feiyu appeared in a flash like a ghost. Several sword beams struck the short sword, shaking off the clear water that was stained on the sword. Then, he took the broken sword in his hand and sized it up.
This was the inheritance of the Sword-fighting Empyrean? Bi Feiyu's heart was filled with ecstasy.
"That doesn't belong to you. Give it to Luo Zheng." A strange voice sounded out.
This voice was a bit old, and it came from the direction of the corpse. Bi Feiyu was shocked, Chen Huangyi's corpse was placed here, and it was a huge pressure on Bi Feiyu. Almost at the same time the voice sounded, the sword in his hand had already slashed out!
The sword gleam slashed towards the corpse. Just as the sword gleam was about to reach Chen Huangyi's sword, the sword gleam began to fold. The originally sharp sword gleam was like a folded piece of paper. It kept folding in space until it finally dissipated into nothingness.
Bi Feiyu was very clear that there weren't many people in the universe who could so easily resolve his sword attack. Aside from the few Empyreans and Great Realm Lords, no one else could do it.
His heart trembled. He chose to take the broken sword and retreat. All the secrets must be in this broken sword. As long as this broken sword was in his hands, he would be able to figure it out …
"Whoosh!"
Wind blew under his feet as he stepped on a profound sword step. This step was many times more profound than the sword step that Luo Zheng had comprehended!
However, after sliding for less than ten meters, he was frozen in the air.
An invisible force restrained him, causing him to be unable to move at all!
Xun looked at Bi Feiyu with an indifferent gaze. She had expected someone to make a move, but she didn't expect the person to be so powerful. He was probably not an ordinary Empyrean …
Bi Feiyu's face was filled with fear. He struggled with all his might in that force, but this force was not something he could resist!
"Who is it, who is it!"
In the end, Chen Huangyi had already passed away. He might have set up some powerful methods, but he definitely couldn't control Bi Feiyu to this extent.
A faint figure quietly appeared and stood behind Chen Huang Yijian.
